# Deuteronomy 22:1-25:19 derivation — Onkelos + the Sifrei on Deuteronomy as the spine (THE SPINE ON THE FOUR CHAPTERS — seventy-five piskaot 222-296 on 22:1-25:19, 434 rows read whole in both files, FIVE HEADLESS; FOURTEEN rows beyond the spine citing the chapters found by the union of both files' citations and read whole — ten reread, four EXCLUDED) — THE LEAN FORM (owner-ruled 2026-09-23) in THREE RUNS + the tail

THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 17 — CHAPTERS 22-25, LEAN, Chapters 22-25 — You shall not see your brother's ox or sheep straying and hide yourself: return them; if he is not near or not known, gather it into your house until he seeks it — so his ass, his garment, every lost thing; you may not hide. Raise his fallen ass with him. No man's gear on a woman, no woman's garment on a man — an abomination. The bird's nest: let the mother go, take the young, that it be well with you and your days long. Build a parapet on your new house, that no blood fall from it. Sow no mixed seed in your vineyard, plow not with ox and ass together, wear not wool and linen mingled; make tassels on the four corners of your covering. When a man takes a wife and hates her and lays a charge of shame, saying he found no virginity — her father and mother bring the tokens to the elders at the gate; the elders take the man and chastise him and fine him a hundred of silver for her father, and she is his wife, he may never send her away; but if the charge is true, they bring her to her father's door and the men of her city stone her, for she committed folly in Israel — purge the evil. A man found lying with a married woman: both die — purge the evil. A betrothed virgin found in the city with a man: both stoned at the gate — she did not cry out, he humbled his neighbor's wife; in the field, the man alone dies — she cried and none saved her, as a man rises against his neighbor and murders him. A virgin not betrothed, seized and lain with: the man gives her father fifty of silver, she is his wife because he humbled her, he may never send her away. A man shall not take his father's wife nor uncover his father's skirt. None wounded or cut shall enter the LORD's assembly; no bastard, even to the tenth generation; no Ammonite or Moabite ever — they met you not with bread and water and hired Balaam to curse you, and the LORD turned the curse to blessing; seek not their peace. Abhor not the Edomite, your brother, nor the Egyptian, whose land you sojourned in: their third generation enters. In the camp against your enemies keep from every evil thing: the man unclean by a night's chance goes outside and returns at evening after washing; a place outside the camp, a spade among your gear, cover what comes from you — for the LORD walks in your camp: let it be holy, and let Him see nothing unseemly and turn from you. Hand not the escaped slave to his master: he dwells with you where he chooses; oppress him not. No harlot of Israel's daughters, no sodomite of her sons; bring not a harlot's hire or a dog's price into the LORD's house for any vow. Lend not on interest to your brother — silver, food, anything; to the foreigner you may; that the LORD bless you in the land. A vow: delay not to pay it, it is sin in you; to refrain is no sin; what your lips utter, keep, as you vowed freely. In your neighbor's vineyard eat grapes to your fill, but put none in your vessel; in his standing grain pluck ears with your hand, but swing no sickle. When a man takes a wife and she finds no favor because he found in her an unseemly thing, he writes her a bill of cutting-off and sends her from his house; she marries another who hates her and writes her a bill, or he dies: her first husband may not take her back after she was defiled — an abomination; sin not the land. A new husband is free one year for his house, to rejoice his wife. Take no millstone in pledge — it is a life. A man stealing a soul of his brothers and selling him dies — purge the evil. Keep the plague of leprosy as the priests teach — remember Miriam on the way from Egypt. Lend to your neighbor but enter not his house for the pledge; stand outside; a poor man's pledge you return by sunset, that he sleep in his garment and bless you — righteousness before the LORD. Oppress not a poor hireling, brother or sojourner: give his wage the same day before sunset, lest he cry to the LORD and it be sin in you. Fathers not put to death for sons nor sons for fathers: each for his own sin. Pervert not the sojourner's or orphan's justice; take no widow's garment in pledge — you were a slave in Egypt. The forgotten sheaf, the olive tree's second beating, the vineyard's second gleaning are the sojourner's, the orphan's and the widow's, that the LORD bless your work — remember Egypt. A dispute comes to judgment: they justify the righteous and condemn the wicked; if the wicked deserves stripes, the judge lays him down and beats him by his wickedness in number — forty, no more, lest your brother be degraded before your eyes. Muzzle not the threshing ox. Brothers dwell together and one dies without a son: his wife shall not marry outside; her husband's brother takes her, and the firstborn stands in the dead brother's name, that his name be not blotted from Israel; if he refuses, she goes up to the elders at the gate: 'he refuses to build his brother's house'; if he stands and says 'I do not desire her', she draws his shoe from his foot and spits in his face — 'so is done to the man who builds not his brother's house' — the house of the unshod. Men strive and the wife of one seizes the other's secrets to save her husband: cut off her hand, your eye shall not pity. No two weights or two measures in your bag or house, great and small: a whole and just weight and measure, that your days be long; all who do such are an abomination. Remember Amalek who met you on the way and cut off the stragglers, faint and weary, fearing not God: when the LORD gives you rest from your enemies in the land, blot out the memory of Amalek from under heaven — do not forget (2026-09-25; the owner: "Reread and go" after the compaction at #216, "reread and go" after #217, "Just run" at #218 — the seventh sitting of THE LEAN PASS and the first on FOUR chapters, read in THREE RUNS under the cost rules' cap: RUN 1 the measurements, the ink, the design and chapter 22's rows; RUN 2 chapters 23 and 24's rows; RUN 3 chapter 25's rows, this ledger, the manifests, the seat and the chain — the state doc's #217, #218 and #219). THE UNITS: deu_22_return_sex_laws (22:1-29), deu_23_qahal_purity_vows (23:1-26 — qahal is the assembly), deu_24_divorce_poor (24:1-22), deu_25_courts_yibbum (25:1-19 — yibbum is the levirate marriage); the portion Ki Teitzei (21:10-25:19) holds all four, no portion edge inside; the chapter the unit (CHAPTER NUMBERS). DECLARED (THE_STEPS Step 2, the spine default of 2026-08-27 — Deuteronomy = the Sifrei on Deuteronomy + Onkelos — read at the chapter's grain; THE LEAN PASS: the spine and Onkelos whole, no docket, the Mishnah rows the spine cites the compile's cases): Onkelos Deuteronomy 22:1-29, 23:1-26, 24:1-22 and 25:1-19 (96 DB verses — the export's rows, the identity in all four, asserted) + the Sifrei on Deuteronomy ON THE FOUR CHAPTERS: piskaot 222-296 (434 rows read whole here and this ledger's source — 157 on chapter 22 in twenty-four piskaot (222 headless at the chapter's head opening on Exodus 23:5; 232 and 233 both on 22:11; 236 headless inside 22:16-17), 99 on chapter 23 in twenty-two (246-267 contiguous), 99 on chapter 24 in eighteen (269 headless with fourteen rows, 283 headless), 79 on chapter 25 in eleven (286 with seventeen rows the largest; 295 headless); every head in verse order; 221 heads on 21:22 before and 297 on 26:1 after — no tail folded, the four edges clean (the split's asserts); the twelve rows read before REREAD WHOLE and marked from the earlier ledgers by computation) + the Sifrei's rows citing the chapters from outside the spine (14 found by the union of both files' citations over the four dumps, each read whole: 41:3 on 11:13, 63:5 on 12:5, 87:3 on 13:7, 94:3 on 13:16, 110:1 on 14:29, 117:5 on 15:9, 122:1 on 15:17, 155:9 on 17:12, 190:14 on 19:17, 352:9 on 33:11 — ten READ BEFORE and REREAD WHOLE; 336:2 on 32:47 fresh; 352:8, 352:10 and 352:17 fresh — THE FOUR ROWS OF 352 EXCLUDED after the whole read: their English alone cites "Dt.23:12" for the blessing of Benjamin's 33:12, the translator's misprint; the Hebrew cites nothing of chapter 23; read whole, kept in this ledger, not in its cite index) + THE KIN'S SPINE CREDITED BY NAME, read at earlier sittings and not this ledger's sources (18 ledgers, the counts computed; NEVER READ AHEAD — no ledger holds an Onkelos row of Deuteronomy 26-34 or of the Prophets: 2 Kings 14:6 for 24:16, 1 Samuel 15 for 25:19, Jeremiah 3:1 for 24:4 and 27:15 for 291:5's analogy are POINTERS AHEAD, owed). ## Sifrei Devarim piskaot 222-296 — the spine ON the four chapters (the heads: 222 on 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass), 223 on 22:2, 224 on 22:3, 225 on 22:4, 226 on 22:5, 227 on 22:6, 228 on 22:7, 229 on 22:8, 230 on 22:9, 231 on 22:10, 232 on 22:11, 233 on 22:11, 234 on 22:12, 235 on 22:13, 236 on 22:16-17 (headless — inside the father's words), 237 on 22:17, 238 on 22:18, 239 on 22:20, 240 on 22:21, 241 on 22:22, 242 on 22:23, 243 on 22:26, 244 on 22:28, 245 on 22:29, 246 on 23:1, 247 on 23:2, 248 on 23:3, 249 on 23:4, 250 on 23:5, 251 on 23:7, 252 on 23:8, 253 on 23:9, 254 on 23:10, 255 on 23:11, 256 on 23:12, 257 on 23:13, 258 on 23:15, 259 on 23:16, 260 on 23:18, 261 on 23:19, 262 on 23:20, 263 on 23:21, 264 on 23:22, 265 on 23:23, 266 on 23:25, 267 on 23:26, 268 on 24:1, 269 on 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows), 270 on 24:2, 271 on 24:5, 272 on 24:6, 273 on 24:7, 274 on 24:8, 275 on 24:9, 276 on 24:10, 277 on 24:12, 278 on 24:14, 279 on 24:15, 280 on 24:16, 281 on 24:17, 282 on 24:19, 283 on 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf), 284 on 24:20, 285 on 24:21, 286 on 25:1, 287 on 25:4, 288 on 25:5, 289 on 25:6, 290 on 25:8, 291 on 25:9, 292 on 25:11, 293 on 25:12, 294 on 25:13, 295 on 25:15 (headless — the weights' reward), 296 on 25:17; 434 rows read whole in both files at the shelf's row grain, none credited; the 12 rows read before marked REREAD WHOLE from the ledgers by computation; the rows in the shelf's order; the 21 rows citing nothing in either file: 224:2, 224:3, 224:4, 227:5, 228:2, 230:9, 234:2, 234:3, 234:7, 235:7, 237:3, 244:2, 247:3, 247:5, 257:2, 257:4, 259:2, 259:4, 281:3, 287:3, 294:3)
- Sifrei Devarim 222:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "WHEN YOU SEE" (Exodus 23:5) — EVEN A FULL MIL AWAY? THE TEACHING SAYS "WHEN YOU ENCOUNTER" (Exodus 23:4); ENCOUNTER IN ITS PLAIN SENSE? "WHEN YOU SEE" — HOW? THE SAGES ESTIMATED ONE IN SEVEN AND A HALF OF A MIL, A RIS. The head row of the headless piska 222, opening on Exodus 23:5 and not on 22:1 (שִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים אֶחָד מִשִּׁבְעָה וּמֶחֱצָה בַּמִּיל "the Sages estimated one of seven and a half in a mil"; שֶׁהוּא רִיס "which is a ris"): THE DISTANCE PARAMETER of the return's duty — the sighting within a ris (some 150 yards), between "see" and "encounter"; the answer sheet's own estimate (Tosefta Bava Metzia 2:25, not opened). The piska on the twin law read at the twin's verse — chapter 22's spine opens in Exodus. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "AND YOU REFRAIN FROM RELEASING — YOU SHALL SURELY RELEASE WITH HIM" (Exodus 23:5) — WE LEARN THAT HE TRANSGRESSES A POSITIVE AND A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT. (מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "a positive commandment"; מִצְוַת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "a negative commandment"): the two classes of the one duty — the compile's effect carries both (the refusal a prohibition broken, the release a command undone). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "RELEASE WITH HIM" — UNLOADING; "RAISE WITH HIM" (22:4) — LOADING: R. JUDAH B. BETHYRA. (זוֹ פְּרִיקָה "this is unloading"; זוֹ טְעִינָה "this is loading"): THE TWO DUTIES DISTINGUISHED — Exodus commands the unburdening, Deuteronomy the reloading; two effects of the fallen-beast cell (Bava Metzia 2:10 the answer sheet on the two). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "YOU SHALL NOT SEE YOUR BROTHER'S OX" (22:1) — A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT; ELSEWHERE "WHEN YOU ENCOUNTER" (Exodus 23:4) — A POSITIVE ONE. The first row citing chapter 22 (מִצְוַת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "a negative commandment"): the lost thing's duty a pair as the fallen beast's (222:2) — the Deuteronomy verse the prohibition, the Exodus verse the command. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "YOUR BROTHER'S OX" — ONLY YOUR BROTHER'S? YOUR ENEMY'S FROM "YOUR ENEMY" (Exodus 23:4) — ANY OWNER; WHY "BROTHER"? THE TORAH SPEAKS ONLY AGAINST THE INCLINATION. (שׁוֹר אוֹיִבְךָ מִנַּיִן "your enemy's ox — whence?"; כְּנֶגֶד הַיֵּצֶר "against the inclination"): THE SCOPE every owner, brother or enemy — and the reason the word "brother" stands: the law addresses the inclination, not the class (225:2 the same words for the ass). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "OR HIS SHEEP STRAYING" — IN THE MANNER OF THEIR STRAYING; HENCE (BAVA METZIA 2:9): WHAT IS A LOST OBJECT? AN ASS OR A COW GRAZING ON THE ROAD — NOT LOST; AN ASS WITH ITS GEAR OVERTURNED, A COW RUNNING AMONG THE VINEYARDS — LOST. THE MISHNAH CITED IN THE HEBREW BY NAME (אֵיזוֹ הִיא אֲבֵדָה "what is a lost object?"; חֲמוֹר וְכֵלָיו הֲפוּכִים "an ass with its gear overturned"): THE PREDICATE "lost" defined by the beast's manner — the compile's condition on the duty (the answer sheet inside the spine). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "AND YOU HIDE YOURSELF FROM THEM" — SOMETIMES YOU HIDE AND SOMETIMES NOT: A PRIEST AND IT IS IN A GRAVEYARD; AN ELDER AND IT IS BENEATH HIS DIGNITY; HIS OWN LOSS GREATER THAN HIS FELLOW'S — EXEMPT. (הָיָה כֹהֵן "he was a priest"; לְפִי כְבוֹדוֹ "according to his dignity"; פָּטוּר "exempt"): THE THREE EXEMPTIONS read out of "hide yourself" — the priest's purity, the elder's honor, the finder's greater loss — the compile's exemption parameters (Bava Metzia 2:9 the answer sheet's ground). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 222:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:1 (headless — opening on Exodus 23:5's fallen ass). "SURELY RETURN THEM" — RETURNED AND IT FLED, RETURNED AND IT FLED, EVEN FIVE TIMES — LIABLE; RETURNED TO A PLACE WHERE OTHERS SAW IT — THEY NEED NOT TEND IT; STOLEN OR LOST — LIABLE FOR IT, ALWAYS, UNTIL HE BRINGS IT INTO HIS OWNER'S DOMAIN. (אֲפִלּוּ חָמֵשׁ פְּעָמִים חַיָּב "even five times — liable"; עַד שֶׁיַּכְנִיסֶנָּה לִרְשׁוּתוֹ "until he brings it into his domain"): the doubled verb "surely return" the repetition's ground and the finder's LIABILITY STATUS until the owner's domain (Bava Metzia 2:9; Tosefta 2:23) — the compile's status on the finder. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 223:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:2. "IF YOUR BROTHER IS NOT NEAR" (22:2) — ONLY NEAR? FAR FROM "AND YOU KNOW HIM NOT". The head row of piska 223 (רָחוֹק מִנַּיִן "far — whence?"): the distance no limit — the unknown owner the far one. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 223:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:2. "GATHER IT INTO YOUR HOUSE" — AND NOT INTO ANOTHER HOUSE. (וְלֹא לְבַיִת אַחֵר "and not to another house"): the finder's own keeping — no delegation. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 223:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:2. "UNTIL YOUR BROTHER SEEKS IT" — WOULD YOU GIVE IT BEFORE HE GIVES ITS MARKS? WHY "UNTIL YOUR BROTHER SEEKS IT"? UNTIL YOU SEEK OUT YOUR BROTHER — WHETHER HE IS A FRAUD OR NOT. (עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתֵּן סִימָנִים "before he gives marks"; אִם רַמַּי הוּא "whether he is a fraud"): THE MARKS (simanim) the claimant's proof and the finder's inquiry into the claimant — the return's two conditions (Bava Metzia 2:7 the answer sheet). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 223:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:2. "AND RETURN IT TO HIM" — EVEN HIMSELF YOU RETURN TO HIM. (אַף אֶת עַצְמוֹ אַתָּה מֵשִׁיב לוֹ "even himself you return to him"): THE LOST MAN — the duty widened from the beast to the wanderer guided home (Tosefta Bava Metzia 2:28, not opened); a find of the reading. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. "SO SHALL YOU DO WITH HIS ASS" (22:3) — AN ASS: IT WORKS AND EATS. The head row of piska 224 (עוֹשָׂה וְאוֹכֶלֶת "it works and eats"): the kept beast's upkeep from its labor (Bava Metzia 2:7) — the compile's parameter on the keeping. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. A GARMENT — HE SHAKES IT ONCE IN THIRTY DAYS; SPREADS IT FOR ITS OWN NEED, NOT FOR HIS HONOR. (אַחַת לִשְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם "once in thirty days"): A CLOCK PARAMETER on the kept garment's care (Bava Metzia 2:8 the answer sheet) — the keeping's calendar. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. VESSELS OF SILVER AND OF COPPER — HE USES THEM FOR THEIR OWN NEED, NOT TO WEAR THEM OUT. (אֲבָל לֹא לְשָׁחֳקָם "but not to wear them out"): the use bounded by the object's good. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. VESSELS OF WOOD — HE USES THEM SO THEY DO NOT ROT (BAVA METZIA 2:8). (כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָקְבוּ "so that they do not rot"): the third material — the keeping by kind. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. THE GARMENT WAS IN THE CLASS OF ALL THESE — WHY SINGLED OUT? TO COMPARE TO IT: AS A GARMENT HAS MARKS AND CLAIMANTS, SO EVERYTHING WITH MARKS AND CLAIMANTS; ONLY THESE? "EVERY LOST THING OF YOUR BROTHER". (לְהַקִּישׁ אֵלֶיהָ "to compare to it"; סִימָנִים וְיֵשׁ לָהּ תוֹבְעִים "marks and it has claimants"): THE CLASS DEFINED BY THE PROTOTYPE — marks and claimants the two properties of returnable property (I3 the prototype), "every lost thing" the widening (Bava Metzia 2:8's list). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. "WHICH IS LOST FROM HIM" — EXCLUDING LESS THAN A PERUTAH'S WORTH; R. JUDAH SAYS: "AND YOU FOUND IT" — INCLUDING LESS THAN A PERUTAH. (פְּרָט לְפָחוֹת מִשָּׁוֶה פְּרוּטָה "excluding less than a perutah's worth"): THE THRESHOLD PARAMETER of the duty — a perutah (the smallest coin) — with R. Judah's dissent, a disputed cell. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 224:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:3. "YOU MAY NOT HIDE YOURSELF" — TO SET IT UNDER A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT. (בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "under a negative commandment"): 22:3's close the prohibition's seat for every kind of lost thing. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "YOU SHALL NOT SEE" (22:4) — A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT; ELSEWHERE "WHEN YOU SEE" (Exodus 23:5) — A POSITIVE ONE. The head row of piska 225 (מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "a positive commandment"): the fallen ass's pair as the lost ox's (222:4) — the two books one law in two forms. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "YOUR BROTHER'S ASS" — ONLY YOUR BROTHER'S? YOUR HATER'S FROM "YOUR HATER'S ASS" (Exodus 23:5) — ANY OWNER; WHY "BROTHER"? THE TORAH SPEAKS ONLY AGAINST THE INCLINATION. (חֲמוֹר שׂוֹנַאֲךָ מִנַּיִן "your hater's ass — whence?"): 222:5's words for the fallen beast — the scope every owner. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "FALLEN" — AND NOT STANDING. (וְלֹא עוֹמְדִים "and not standing"): the duty's condition the beast down. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "ON THE WAY" — AND NOT IN THE STABLE; HENCE (BAVA METZIA 2:10): FOUND IT IN THE STABLE — NO DUTY; IN THE PUBLIC DOMAIN — THE DUTY. THE MISHNAH CITED IN THE HEBREW BY NAME (וְלֹא בָּרֶפֶת "and not in the stable"; בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים חַיָּב בָּהּ "in the public domain — liable for it"): THE PLACE PARAMETER — the way and the public domain against the stable. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "AND YOU HIDE YOURSELF" — SOMETIMES YOU HIDE AND SOMETIMES NOT: THE PRIEST IN THE GRAVEYARD, THE ELDER'S DIGNITY, THE GREATER LOSS — EXEMPT. (פָּטוּר "exempt"): 222:7's three exemptions repeated for the fallen beast — one exemption table for the two laws. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 225:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:4. "RAISE WITH HIM" — RAISED AND IT FELL, EVEN FIVE TIMES — LIABLE; THE OWNER WENT AND SAT AND SAID "SINCE THE COMMANDMENT IS ON YOU, RAISE IT IF YOU WISH" — EXEMPT, FOR IT SAYS "WITH HIM"; EVEN AN ELDER, A SICK MAN, ONE SMITTEN WITH BOILS? "WITH HIM". (הוֹאִיל וְעָלֶיךָ מִצְוָה "since the commandment is on you"; וַאֲפִלּוּ מֻכֵּה שְׁחִין "and even one smitten with boils"): THE OWNER'S PART A CONDITION — "with him" exempts the helper when the owner sits down, and binds even the infirm (Bava Metzia 2:10 the answer sheet); the compile's two arms. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 226:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:5. "NO MAN'S GEAR ON A WOMAN" (22:5) — WHAT DOES THE VERSE TEACH, THAT A WOMAN NOT WEAR WHITE GARMENTS NOR A MAN COLORED ONES? "ABOMINATION" — A THING THAT LEADS TO ABOMINATION; THE RULE: A WOMAN NOT DRESS AS A MAN AND GO AMONG MEN, A MAN NOT ADORN HIMSELF WITH WOMEN'S ORNAMENTS AND GO AMONG WOMEN. The head row of piska 226 (דָּבָר הַבָּא לִידֵי תּוֹעֵבָה "a thing that comes to abomination"; וְתֵלֵךְ לְבֵין הָאֲנָשִׁים "and go among the men"): THE PRINCIPLE — the garment's law read by its purpose (mixing among the other sex), not by color; the compile's predicate on the act. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 226:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:5. R. ELIEZER B. JACOB SAYS: WHENCE THAT A WOMAN NOT WEAR WEAPONS AND GO OUT TO WAR? "NO MAN'S GEAR ON A WOMAN"; THAT A MAN NOT ADORN HIMSELF WITH WOMEN'S ORNAMENTS? "A MAN SHALL NOT WEAR A WOMAN'S GARMENT". (כְּלִי זַיִן וְתֵצֵא לַמִּלְחָמָה "weapons, and go out to war"): ONKELOS'S RENDERING IS R. ELIEZER B. JACOB'S — "the armament of a man" (the ink's find 15): the Aramaic writes this row's reading into the verse; the war daemon's exemption of women a status. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 226:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:5. "FOR AN ABOMINATION OF THE LORD YOUR GOD IS EVERYONE WHO DOES THESE" — HE IS CALLED BY FIVE NAMES: CONDEMNED, ABOMINATION, HATED, DETESTABLE AND PERVERSE (SIFRA, KEDOSHIM). (קָרוּי חֲמִשָּׁה שֵׁמוֹת "called by five names"): THE FIVE EPITHETS of the abomination formula's subject — the sanction's vocabulary at the formula's fifth seat of eight (the ink's find 5); 25:16's "all who do these" the twin run. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. "IF A NEST CHANCE" (22:6) — EXCLUDING THE READY AT HAND. The head row of piska 227 (פְּרָט לַמְזֻמָּן "excluding the ready at hand"): the nest's law on the CHANCE find alone — the domesticated bird excluded (Chullin 12:1 the answer sheet); the compile's condition. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. THE LEAST OF "FLEDGLINGS" IS TWO, THE LEAST OF "EGGS" TWO; WHENCE THAT WITH ONE FLEDGLING OR ONE EGG HE MUST SEND HER? "A NEST" — A NEST OF ANY NUMBER. (מִעוּט אֶפְרוֹחִים שְׁנַיִם "the least of fledglings — two"; קַן מִכָּל מָקוֹם "a nest — from anywhere"): THE COUNT PARAMETER — the plural's minimum two overridden by "nest": even one (Chullin 12:3). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. FROM "ON THE WAY … THE MOTHER SITTING ON THE FLEDGLINGS OR THE EGGS" MIGHT I EXCLUDE GEESE AND HENS THAT NESTED IN THE ORCHARD? "BEFORE YOU". (אַוָּזִים וְתַרְנְגוֹלִים שֶׁקִּנְּנוּ בַּפַּרְדֵּס "geese and hens that nested in the orchard"): the orchard's escaped fowl inside the law by "before you" (228:2 the ruling) — the domestication boundary. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. ONLY IN A PRIVATE DOMAIN? THE PUBLIC DOMAIN FROM "ON THE WAY". (בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מִנַּיִן "in the public domain — whence?"): the place — every domain. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. ON THE TREES — WHENCE? "IN ANY TREE". (עַל הָאִילָנוֹת מִנַּיִן "on the trees — whence?"): the second place clause; a row citing nothing in either file. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. ON THE GROUND — WHENCE? "OR ON THE GROUND". (עַל הָאָרֶץ מִנַּיִן "on the ground — whence?"): the third place clause — the verse's three places read one by one. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. "THE MOTHER SITTING ON THE FLEDGLINGS OR ON THE EGGS" — AS FLEDGLINGS ARE VIABLE, SO EGGS VIABLE: THE ADDLED EXCLUDED; AS EGGS NEED THEIR MOTHER, SO FLEDGLINGS NEED THEIR MOTHER: THE FLIERS EXCLUDED. (יָצְאוּ מוּזָרוֹת "the addled are excluded"; יָצְאוּ הַמַּפְרִיחִים "the fliers are excluded"): the two exclusions by the pair's mutual measure — the young's STATE a parameter (Chullin 12:3). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. "THE MOTHER SITTING" — WHEN SHE SITS ON THEM, EXCLUDING THE HOVERING; EVEN WITH HER WINGS TOUCHING THE NEST? "THE MOTHER SITTING ON THE FLEDGLINGS" — EVEN THOUGH SHE IS NOT WITH THEM. (פְּרָט לִמְעוֹפֶפֶת "excluding the hovering one"): the mother's POSTURE the condition — sitting, not flying; the touch of the wings enough (Chullin 12:3). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:9 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. AN UNCLEAN BIRD SITTING ON A CLEAN BIRD'S EGGS, A CLEAN ON AN UNCLEAN'S — MUST HE SEND? "THE MOTHER SITTING" — UNTIL ALL ARE ONE KIND. (עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כֻלָּם מִין אֶחָד "until they are all one kind"): the kind's identity a condition (Chullin 12:2). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. "TAKE NOT THE MOTHER WITH THE YOUNG" — FROM "THE PRIEST SHALL TAKE FOR THE ONE PURIFIED TWO LIVING CLEAN BIRDS" (Leviticus 14:4) MIGHT HE TAKE HER TO PURIFY THE LEPER? "TAKE NOT THE MOTHER" — NOT EVEN TO PURIFY THE LEPER. (אֲפִלּוּ לְטַהֵר בָּהּ אֶת הַמְּצוֹרָע "even to purify the leper with her"): THE LEPER'S BIRDS BARRED FROM THE NEST — the metzora rite's CALL on the nest's mother refused (Chullin 12:5); a cross-daemon condition. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 227:11 — MATERIAL. On 22:6. "TAKE NOT THE MOTHER" — A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT. (מִצְוַת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "a negative commandment"): the prohibition's seat. THE ENGLISH ROW IS MISPLACED — the export's 227:11 in the English carries 229:9's text (R. Ishmael's fish, Ecclesiastes 9:12) and not this row's: the Hebrew is read, the English file's row noted as the export's slip. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. "SURELY SEND" (22:7) — A POSITIVE COMMANDMENT; SENT HER AND SHE RETURNED, SENT HER AND SHE RETURNED, EVEN FIVE TIMES — HE MUST SEND. The head row of piska 228 (מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "a positive commandment"; אֲפִלּוּ חָמֵשׁ פְּעָמִים "even five times"): the doubled verb's repetition as at 222:8 and 225:6 — three "five times" in the two piskaot. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. GEESE AND HENS THAT REBELLED AND NESTED IN THE ORCHARD — HE MUST SEND; IN THE HOUSE — EXEMPT FROM SENDING. (שֶׁמָּרְדוּ וְשֶׁקִּנְּנוּ בַּפַּרְדֵּס "that rebelled and nested in the orchard"; בַּבַּיִת פָּטוּר מִלְּשַׁלֵּחַ "in the house — exempt from sending"): THE DOMESTICATION BOUNDARY ruled — the escaped fowl wild again, the house-nest exempt; 227:3's question answered. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. "FLEDGLINGS OR EGGS" — AS FLEDGLINGS HAVE USE, THE DEAD EXCLUDED; SO EGGS WITH USE, THE ADDLED EXCLUDED; THE LEAST TWO, EVEN ONE — HE MUST SEND; ANOTHER WORD: "IF A NEST CHANCE BEFORE YOU" — ANYWHERE. (יָצְאוּ מֵתִים "the dead are excluded"; מִכָּל מָקוֹם "from anywhere"): 227:2-3 reprised in short (the translator: a condensed reprise, thinly attested) — the young's use the measure. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. "ON THE WAY, IN ANY TREE OR ON THE GROUND" — AS THE WAY IS FREE, THE TIED BIRDS IN ANOTHER'S DOMAIN ARE EXCLUDED. (יָצְאוּ מְקֻשָּׁרִים "the tied are excluded"): the bird's freedom the condition — the tied bird owned. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:5 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_14_reeh_2026-09-21.md). On 22:7. ANOTHER WORD: "SURELY SEND" — OF A CLEAN BIRD; OR EVEN AN UNCLEAN? "EVERY CLEAN BIRD YOU SHALL EAT" (14:11) — THIS IS A PROTOTYPE: WHEREVER "BIRD" IS SAID, THE CLEAN IS MEANT, AS R. JOSIAH; IF HE SENT HER AND TURNED HIS FACE ON THE WAY — EXEMPT FROM SENDING. (זֶה בִּנְיַן אָב "this is a prototype"; בִּטְהוֹרָה הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר "of the clean the verse speaks"): THE PROTOTYPE (I3) from chapter 14's clean bird — "bird" means clean throughout the Torah; the nest's law on clean birds only; the sender who walked on exempt. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 14, deu_14_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 228:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. "SURELY SEND" — OF FEMALES THE VERSE SPEAKS, NOT OF MALES. (בַּנְּקֵבוֹת הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר "of females the verse speaks"): "the mother" the sex parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. A MALE PARTRIDGE — R. ELIEZER OBLIGATES THE SENDING, "SURELY SEND"; THE SAGES EXEMPT, "THE MOTHER" — EXCLUDING THE MALE PARTRIDGE. (פְּרָט לְקוֹרֵא זָכָר "excluding the male partridge"): the brooding male a DISPUTED cell (Chullin 12:2) — the sages' exemption the answer sheet's. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 228:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:7. ONE WHO TAKES THE MOTHER FROM THE YOUNG — R. JUDAH SAYS: HE IS FLOGGED AND DOES NOT SEND; THE SAGES SAY: HE SENDS AND IS NOT FLOGGED — "AND SO ON, THE MISHNAH, UNTIL 'THE WEIGHTY COMMANDMENTS OF THE TORAH'". (לוֹקֶה וְאֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּחַ "flogged and does not send"; מִצְווֹת חֲמוּרוֹת שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה "the weighty commandments of the Torah"): THE SPINE POINTS TO THE MISHNAH BY ABBREVIATION — "etc., the Mishnah until …" (Chullin 12:4-5 the answer sheet, the lashes and the a fortiori (I1) from the commandment worth an issar (the smallest coin) to the weighty ones) — the answer sheet cited as a text to open, not copied; the compile's sanction parameter (a prohibition repaired by an act carries no lashes). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "WHEN YOU BUILD A NEW HOUSE" (22:8) — ONLY A BUILDER? ONE WHO BOUGHT, INHERITED, WAS GIVEN — "A HOUSE" ANYWHERE. The head row of piska 229 (לָקַח יָרַשׁ וְנִתַּן לוֹ בְּמַתָּנָה מִנַּיִן "bought, inherited, given as a gift — whence?"): the parapet's duty on every owner — 194:1's words for the war exemption's new house (20:5), the twin. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "PUT NOT BLOOD IN YOUR HOUSE" — ONLY A HOUSE? THE STRAW HOUSE, THE CATTLE HOUSE, THE WOOD HOUSE, THE STOREHOUSES — "PUT NOT BLOOD IN YOUR HOUSE". (בַּיִת הַתֶּבֶן בֵּית הַבָּקָר "the straw house, the cattle house"): the duty widened to every roofed building by the blood clause. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. EVEN A GATEHOUSE, A PORTICO, A BALCONY? "HOUSE" — AS A HOUSE IS A DWELLING, THESE ARE EXCLUDED, NOT DWELLINGS. (שֶׁהוּא בֵּית דִּירָה "which is a dwelling house"): the dwelling the class's mark — the unroofed and the open excluded. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "MAKE A PARAPET FOR YOUR ROOF" — ONLY A ROOF? PITS, DITCHES, CAVES, TRENCHES — "PUT NOT BLOOD IN YOUR HOUSE"; WHY "ROOF"? EXCLUDING THE RAMP; "HOUSE" — INCLUDING THE SANCTUARY; "ROOF" — EXCLUDING THE PORCH. (פְּרָט לְכֶבֶשׁ "excluding the ramp"; לְרַבּוֹת הֵיכָל "including the Sanctuary"; פְּרָט לָאוּלָם "excluding the porch"): THE TEMPLE INSIDE THE PARAPET LAW — the altar's ramp and the porch out, the Sanctuary's roof in (Middot 3:5, 4:6-7 the answer sheet); the hazards of the ground under the blood clause. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "NEW" — RABBI SAYS: FROM THE TIME OF ITS NEWNESS MAKE IT A PARAPET; HOW HIGH? WHERE THEY ROLL, THREE HANDBREADTHS; WHERE THEY TREAD, TEN. (מַעְגִּילוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים "its rolling-place three handbreadths"; בֵּית דָּרְסוֹ עֲשָׂרָה "its treading-place ten"): THE PARAPET'S TWO MEASURES — three and ten handbreadths by the roof's use — PARAMETERS; the duty from the building's day. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "MAKE A PARAPET FOR YOUR ROOF" — A POSITIVE COMMANDMENT; "PUT NOT BLOOD IN YOUR HOUSE" — A NEGATIVE ONE. (מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "a positive commandment"): the pair inside one verse. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "WHEN THE FALLER FALLS FROM IT" — THIS ONE WAS FIT TO FALL! BUT MERIT IS ROLLED THROUGH THE MERITORIOUS AND GUILT THROUGH THE GUILTY. (רָאוּי זֶה שֶׁיִּפּוֹל "this one was fit to fall"; מְגַלְגְּלִים זְכוּת עַל יְדֵי זַכַּי "they roll merit through the meritorious"): HEAVEN'S RECKONING inside the building code — the faller's desert and the owner's guilt two ledgers (Tosefta Yom Kippur 5:12, Sifrei Numbers 114 the parallels); a heaven entry for the compile. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "FROM IT" — AND NOT INTO IT: IF THE PUBLIC WAY WAS TEN HANDBREADTHS HIGHER AND ONE FELL FROM IT INTO THE HOUSE — EXEMPT, FOR "FROM IT" NOT "INTO IT". (מִמֶּנּוּ וְלֹא לְתוֹכוֹ "from it and not into it"; עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים "ten handbreadths"): the direction of the fall the liability's condition — the sunken roof exempt. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:9 — CONTEXT. On 22:8. ANOTHER WORD: "WHEN YOU BUILD" — R. ISHMAEL SAYS: THE VERSE TEACHES THAT NO MAN KNOWS HOW HE IS JUDGED, AS "MAN KNOWS NOT HIS TIME, LIKE FISH CAUGHT IN A NET" (Ecclesiastes 9:12). (שֶׁאֵין אָדָם יוֹדֵעַ כֵּיצַד הוּא נִדּוֹן "that no man knows how he is judged"): the homily on the new house — the judgment unknown; no rule. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 229:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:8. "HOUSE" — HENCE THEY SAID: A HOUSE WITHOUT FOUR BY FOUR CUBITS IS EXEMPT FROM THE PARAPET, THE DOORPOST SCROLL AND THE COURTYARD'S JOINING; IT DOES NOT EXTEND THE CITY, DOES NOT BIND PRODUCE TO THE TITHE, GETS NO FOUR CUBITS AT ITS DOOR; ONE WHO VOWS OFF "THE HOUSE" MAY SIT IN IT; IT IS NOT DEFILED BY PLAGUE-SIGNS, DOES NOT REVERT AT THE JUBILEE, AND THE WARRIORS DO NOT RETURN FOR IT. (אַרְבַּע עַל אַרְבַּע "four by four"; פָּטוּר מִן הַמַּעֲקֶה וּמִן הַמְּזוּזָה "exempt from the parapet and from the doorpost scroll"): THE HOUSE'S SIZE PARAMETER — four by four cubits — SHARED BY NINE LAWS (the parapet, the doorpost, the Sabbath joining, the city's bound, the tithe, the door's space, the vow, the house-plague, the Jubilee, the war exemption): one parameter across ten daemons — the compile's registry row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. "SOW NOT YOUR VINEYARD WITH MIXED SEED" (22:9) — WHY NEEDED, WHEN "YOUR FIELD YOU SHALL NOT SOW WITH MIXED SEED" (Leviticus 19:19) IS SAID? WHOEVER KEEPS MIXED SEED IN A VINEYARD TRANSGRESSES TWO PROHIBITIONS. The head row of piska 230 (עוֹבֵר בִּשְׁנֵי לָאוִים "transgresses two prohibitions"): the vineyard's seat a second prohibition over the field's — the count of transgressions the compile's effect (Sifra Kedoshim the parallel). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ONLY A WHOLE VINEYARD? EVEN A SINGLE VINE BEARING FRUIT — "VINEYARD" ANYWHERE. (גֶּפֶן יְחִידִי וְעוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת "a single vine bearing fruit"): the vineyard's minimum one vine (Kilayim 4:6 the answer sheet's measures). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. WHENCE THAT THE VINEYARD'S MIXED SEED IS FORBIDDEN FOR BENEFIT? "SANCTIFIED" IS SAID HERE AND "HOLY" THERE (Leviticus 5:15) — AS THE HOLY THERE IS FORBIDDEN FOR BENEFIT, SO HERE. (אָסוּר בַּהֲנָיָה "forbidden for benefit"; נֶאֱמַר כָּאן תִּקְדַּשׁ "here it says 'be sanctified'"): THE VERBAL ANALOGY (I2) on the root "holy" — the trespass offering's sanctity teaches the mixture's ban on benefit; the effect's class (forbidden for benefit, not for eating alone). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. "LEST THE FULLNESS OF THE SEED BE SANCTIFIED" — FROM WHEN IS THE SEED SANCTIFIED? FROM ITS ROOTING; AND GRAPES? FROM THEIR BECOMING LIKE A WHITE BEAN. (מִשֶּׁתַּשְׁרִישׁ "from its rooting"; כְּפוֹל לָבָן "like a white bean"): THE CLOCK OF THE BAN — the seed's rooting and the grape's size (Kilayim 7:7 the answer sheet) — two growth parameters. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. "THE SEED" — EXCLUDING SEED THAT WENT OUT WITH THE DUNG OR WITH THE WATER, OR THE SOWER WHOSE SEED THE WIND STORMS; MIGHT I EXCLUDE THE SOWER WHOM THE WIND ASSISTS? "WHICH YOU SOW". (עִם הַזְּבָלִים אוֹ עִם הַמַּיִם "with the dung or with the water"): the INTENT condition — the unintended sowing out, the wind-helped sowing in (Kilayim 5:7). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ONE WHO KEEPS THORNS IN A VINEYARD — R. ELIEZER SAYS: SANCTIFIED, FOR "WHICH YOU SOW"; THE SAGES SAY: "SEED" EXCLUDES THE KEEPER OF THORNS. (פְּרָט לַמְקַיֵּם קוֹצִים בַּכֶּרֶם "excluding one who keeps thorns in a vineyard"): the thorns a DISPUTED cell (Kilayim 5:8) — the sages' exclusion the answer sheet's. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. "AND THE PRODUCE OF THE VINEYARD" — FROM WHEN IS THE PRODUCE SANCTIFIED? FROM ITS ROOTING; GRAPES FROM THEIR RISING LIKE A WHITE BEAN. (מִשֶּׁתַּשְׁרִישׁ "from its rooting"): 230:4's clock read again on the produce clause — one clock for the seed and the vine. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ONLY A FRUIT-BEARING VINEYARD? A BARREN ONE — "VINEYARD" ANYWHERE; ONLY YOUR OWN? OTHERS' — "SOW NOT YOUR VINEYARD WITH MIXED SEED" ANYWHERE. (כֶּרֶם שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים מִנַּיִן "others' vineyard — whence?"): the scope — barren and another's. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:9 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ONE WHO STRETCHES A VINE'S TENDRIL OVER SEEDS, EVEN A HUNDRED CUBITS — THE VINE AND ITS FRUIT ARE FORBIDDEN. (אֲפִלּוּ מֵאָה אַמָּה "even a hundred cubits"): the tendril's reach the mixture — a distance parameter with no limit (Tosefta Kilayim 4:10); a row citing nothing in either file. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ["PLOW NOT WITH AN OX AND AN ASS" (22:10) —] NOT WITH THIS ONE ALONE AND THAT ONE ALONE? "TOGETHER" — EACH ALONE IS PERMITTED. (תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר יַחְדָּו "the Teaching says: together"): 22:10 read inside piska 230 before its own piska 231 — "together" the condition, the single beast free. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:11 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ANOTHER WORD: "MIXED SEED" — TO MAKE LIABLE ON ACCOUNT OF THE VINEYARD AND ON ACCOUNT OF THE FIELD. (מִשּׁוּם כֶּרֶם וּמִשּׁוּם שָׂדֶה "on account of the vineyard and on account of the field"): 230:1's two prohibitions named from the word itself. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:12 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. ANOTHER WORD: "LEST THE FULLNESS BE SANCTIFIED" — LEST THE FULLNESS BE FORBIDDEN, AS R. JOSIAH. (״פֶּן תֵּאָסֵר הַמְלֵאָה "lest the fullness be forbidden"): the word "sanctified" glossed "forbidden" — the verb's sense the effect's (230:3's analogy in one word). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 230:13 — MATERIAL. On 22:9. "WHICH YOU SOW" — ONLY WHAT HE SOWED? HIS FELLOW SOWED AND HE WISHES TO KEEP IT — "YOU SOW" ANYWHERE. (זָרַע חֲבֵירוֹ וְרָצָה לְקַיְּמוֹ "his fellow sowed and he wished to keep it"): the keeper liable as the sower — maintaining is sowing. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 231:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:10. "PLOW NOT WITH AN OX AND AN ASS TOGETHER" (22:10) — FOREVER? "THAT YOUR OX AND YOUR ASS MAY REST" (Exodus 23:12) — THEY ALREADY WORK; SO "TOGETHER" — THIS ONE WITH THAT ONE; ONLY OX AND ASS? EVERY BEAST, ANIMAL AND BIRD FROM "PLOW NOT"; WHY "OX AND ASS"? WITH AN OX AND AN ASS YOU MAY NOT, BUT WITH A MAN AND AN ASS YOU MAY. The head row of piska 231 (זֶה עִם זֶה "this one with that one"; בְּאָדָם וּבַחֲמוֹר "with a man and with an ass"): THE PAIR'S LAW — every two kinds (Kilayim 8:2), the man excluded from the pair (Kilayim 8:6); the Sabbath verse the ground that the beasts work. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 231:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:10. "PLOW NOT" — ONLY PLOWING? THRESHING, SITTING AND DRIVING FROM "TOGETHER" — ANYWHERE; R. MEIR EXEMPTS THE SITTER. (הַדָּשׁ וְהַיּוֹשֵׁב וְהַמַּנְהִיג "the thresher, the sitter and the driver"; רַבִּי מֵאִיר פּוֹטֵר בַּיּוֹשֵׁב "R. Meir exempts the sitter"): every labor of the pair — the sitter in the wagon a DISPUTED cell. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 231:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:10. "TOGETHER" — EXCLUDING THE MULE; "TOGETHER" — EXCLUDING ONE WHO TIES A HORSE TO THE WAGON'S SIDE OR BEHIND IT, AND THE LIBYAN ASS WITH CAMELS. (פְּרָט לְרָמָךְ "excluding the mule"; לְצִדֵּי הַקָּרוֹן "to the sides of the wagon"): the mule one beast, the tied horse not yoked — the pair's definition (Kilayim 8:4). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 232:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "WEAR NOT MINGLED STUFF" (22:11) — NOT SHORN WOOL AND LINEN SHREDS? "SHAATNEZ" (the mingled stuff) — A THING COMBED, SPUN AND WOVEN; R. SHIMON B. ELAZAR SAYS: HE IS TWISTED AND TWISTS HIS FATHER IN HEAVEN AGAINST HIMSELF. The head row of piska 232 (דָּבָר שֶׁשּׁוּעַ טָווּי וְנוּז "a thing combed, spun and woven"): THE WORD READ BY NOTARIKON (the acronym reading) — shaatnez as shua-tavui-nuz, the three operations the definition (Kilayim 9:8, Sifra Kedoshim); the compile's predicate on the fabric. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 232:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "WEAR NOT" — ONLY WEARING? COVERING FROM "IT SHALL NOT COME UPON YOU" (Leviticus 19:19); A PACK ON HIS BACK? "WEAR NOT" — WEARING WAS IN THE CLASS AND WAS SINGLED OUT TO COMPARE: AS WEARING BENEFITS THE BODY, SO ALL THAT BENEFITS THE BODY. (שֶׁהִיא הֲנָיַת הַגּוּף "which is a benefit of the body"): the act's class by the prototype — bodily benefit, not carrying (I3). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 232:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "TOGETHER" — A WOOL SHIRT OVER A LINEN SHIRT, A LINEN OVER A WOOL? "TOGETHER". (חָלוּק שֶׁל צֶמֶר עַל גַּבֵּי חָלוּק שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן "a shirt of wool over a shirt of linen"): two garments no mixture — one fabric the condition. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 232:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. R. HANANIAH B. GAMLIEL SAYS: WHENCE THAT ONE MAY NOT KNOT A WOOL STRIP TO A LINEN ONE TO GIRD HIS LOINS, EVEN WITH A STRAP BETWEEN? "TOGETHER" — ANYWHERE; SO THE SACK AND THE BASKET COMBINE INTO MIXED STUFF. (אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָרְצוּעָה בָּאֶמְצַע "even though the strap is between"; הַשַּׂק וְהַקֻּפָּה מְצָרְפִים "the sack and the basket combine"): the joining a mixture even through a third material (Kilayim 9:10). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 232:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "WOOL AND LINEN TOGETHER" — BUT EACH ALONE IS PERMITTED; THE FELTS ARE FORBIDDEN AS MIXED STUFF — NO WEAVING IN THEM, BUT COMBING. (הַלְּבָדִים אֲסוּרִים מִשּׁוּם כִּלְאַיִם "the felts are forbidden as mixed stuff"): one of the three operations enough — combing alone binds the felt (Kilayim 9:9). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 233:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_05_vaetchanan_2026-09-16.md). On 22:11. "WEAR NOT MINGLED STUFF … TASSELS MAKE FOR YOURSELF" (22:11-12) — BOTH WERE SAID IN ONE UTTERANCE; "REMEMBER" (Exodus 20:8) AND "KEEP" (5:12) — BOTH IN ONE UTTERANCE. The head row of piska 233, the second on 22:11 (שְׁנֵיהֶם נֶאֶמְרוּ בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד "both were said in one utterance"): THE ONE UTTERANCE — the tassels of wool on a linen garment permitted against the mingled stuff because the two were spoken as one (the exception born with the rule; Menachot 4:1's ground), and the Sabbath's two words the paradigm — the piska's five pairs a LIST OF EXCEPTIONS SPOKEN WITH THEIR RULES: the compile's permission edge (tassels -> mingled stuff). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 5, deu_05_vaetchanan).
- Sifrei Devarim 233:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "ITS PROFANERS SHALL SURELY DIE" (Exodus 31:14) AND "ON THE SABBATH DAY TWO YEARLING LAMBS" (Numbers 28:9) — BOTH IN ONE UTTERANCE. (שְׁנֵיהֶם נֶאֶמְרוּ בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד "both were said in one utterance"): the Sabbath's offering slaughtered on the Sabbath — the second exception spoken with its rule. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 233:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "THE NAKEDNESS OF YOUR BROTHER'S WIFE YOU SHALL NOT UNCOVER" (Leviticus 18:16) AND "HER HUSBAND'S BROTHER SHALL GO IN TO HER" (25:5) — BOTH IN ONE UTTERANCE. (שְׁנֵיהֶם נֶאֶמְרוּ בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד "both were said in one utterance"): THE LEVIRATE AS THE EXCEPTION to the brother's wife — 25:5 cited inside chapter 22's spine (this row sat among chapter 25's outside set at the dump); the third pair. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 233:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. "EVERY DAUGHTER INHERITING AN INHERITANCE" (Numbers 36:8) AND "THE INHERITANCE SHALL NOT TURN FROM TRIBE TO TRIBE" (Numbers 36:9) — BOTH IN ONE UTTERANCE. (שְׁנֵיהֶם נֶאֶמְרוּ בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד "both were said in one utterance"): the heiress's marriage inside her tribe with the inheritance's fixity — the fourth pair (Zelophehad's daughters on the tape). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 233:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:11. WHAT FLESH AND BLOOD CANNOT — TO SAY TWO THINGS AS ONE — AS "GOD SPOKE ONCE; TWO HAVE I HEARD" (Psalm 62:12). (״אַחַת דִּבֶּר אֱלֹהִים שְׁתַּיִם זוּ שָׁמָעְנוּ״ "God spoke once; two have I heard"): the ground of the one utterance — the divine speech's form; the translator notes the whole piska thinly attested before the Tosafists. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. "TASSELS MAKE FOR YOURSELF" (22:12) — WHY SAID, WHEN "THEY SHALL MAKE THEM FRINGES" (Numbers 15:38) IS SAID? MIGHT ONE MAKE A SINGLE THREAD ALONE? "TASSELS". The head row of piska 234 (יַעֲשֶׂה חוּט אֶחָד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ "make one thread alone"): the plural "tassels" against the single thread — the count's floor. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. HOW MANY TASSELS ARE MADE? NOT LESS THAN THREE THREADS, AS THE SCHOOL OF HILLEL; THE SCHOOL OF SHAMMAI SAYS: FOUR THREADS OF BLUE AND FOUR OF WHITE, FOUR FINGERS EACH. (אֵין פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה חוּטִים "not less than three threads"; מֵאַרְבָּעָה חוּטִים שֶׁל תְּכֵלֶת "from four threads of blue"): THE TASSELS' PARAMETERS — three or four threads, four fingers — the two schools' numbers. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. AND THE LAW IS AS THE SCHOOL OF SHAMMAI; WHEN? AT THE START; BUT ITS REMNANTS AND SHREDS — ANY LENGTH. (וַהֲלָכָה כְּדִבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּיי "and the law is as the words of the school of Shammai"): SHAMMAI'S RULING STANDS — a rare verdict for that school (Menachot 41b the Talmud's discussion, not opened); the length at making only. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. "ON FOUR" — EXCLUDING GARMENTS OF THREE, FIVE, SIX, SEVEN AND EIGHT CORNERS; ANOTHER WORD: "TASSELS MAKE" — THE WHITE; WHENCE THE BLUE? "THEY SHALL PUT ON THE FRINGE OF THE CORNER A THREAD OF BLUE" (Numbers 15:38). (יָצְאוּ בַּעֲלֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה "those of three are excluded"; זֶה לָבָן "this is the white"): the four corners exact, and Deuteronomy's white with Numbers' blue — the two verses one commandment (Sifrei Numbers 115). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. "MAKE" — AND NOT FROM THE MADE: NOT DRAWING LOOSE THREADS FROM THE CLOAK; "ON FOUR" — AND NOT ON EIGHT; "YOUR COVERING" — EXCLUDING THE TOGA AND THE OTHER CLOAKS, NOT BEING SQUARE; R. ELIEZER B. JACOB SAYS: NOT IN THE GARMENT'S MIDDLE BUT ON ITS EDGE — "ON FOUR CORNERS". (וְלֹא מִן הֶעָשׂוּי "and not from the made"; לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָם מְרֻבָּעִים "because they are not square"): the making's form — new threads, the square garment, the corner's edge (Kilayim 9:6's list of cloaks). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. "YOUR COVERING" — EXCLUDING THE PILLOW; "YOUR COVERING" — EXCLUDING THE SHEET; "YOU COVER" — EXCLUDING THE SOLDIER'S BLANKET. (פְּרָט לְכַר "excluding the pillow"; פְּרָט לְסָגוֹס "excluding the soldier's blanket"): the garment worn, not the bedding (the English cites "Dt.11:12" — the translator's slip for 22:12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 234:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:12. "WITH IT" — EXCLUDING A SMOCK THAT DOES NOT COVER HIS HEAD AND MOST OF HIM. (פְּרָט לְמַעֲפוֹרֶת "excluding the smock"): THE GARMENT'S SIZE PARAMETER — the head and most of the body. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE AND GOES IN TO HER AND HATES HER" (22:13) — R. JUDAH SAYS: IF HE WENT IN TO HER — HE IS FLOGGED; IF NOT — NOT. The head row of piska 235 (אִם בָּא עָלֶיהָ לוֹקֶה "if he went in to her — he is flogged"): the intercourse a CONDITION of the slanderer's penalty (R. Judah; 236:2 again) — the compile's condition on the bride's case. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:2 — CONTEXT. On 22:13. "AND HATES HER" — HENCE: ONE WHO TRANSGRESSES A LIGHT COMMANDMENT WILL TRANSGRESS A WEIGHTY ONE: "LOVE YOUR NEIGHBOR" (Leviticus 19:18), THEN "TAKE NO VENGEANCE", THEN "HATE NOT YOUR BROTHER IN YOUR HEART" (19:17), THEN "THAT YOUR BROTHER MAY LIVE WITH YOU" (25:36) — UNTIL HE COMES TO BLOODSHED. (עָבַר אָדָם עַל מִצְוָה קַלָּה "a man transgressed a light commandment"; לִידֵי שְׁפִיכוּת דָּמִים "to bloodshed"): the homily of the hater's descent — no rule. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "AND LAYS A CHARGE OF WORDS" (22:14) — EVEN "YOU BURNED THE DISH" WHEN SHE DID NOT? "A CHARGE OF WORDS" HERE AND "A CHARGE OF WORDS" (22:17) — A VERBAL ANALOGY: AS THERE A CLAIM ON THE TOKENS, SO HERE; OR AS THERE THE PLACE OF THE TOKENS, SO HERE — WHENCE ANOTHER MANNER OF INTERCOURSE? "AND BROUGHT ON HER AN EVIL NAME". (לִגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה "for a verbal analogy"; טַעֲנַת בְּתוּלִים "a claim on the tokens"): THE ANALOGY INSIDE THE CHAPTER (I2 — 22:14 with 22:17): the charge is a claim of unchastity, not any complaint; the compile's predicate on the charge. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "AND SAYS: THIS WOMAN" — HE SPEAKS HIS WORDS ONLY IN HER PRESENCE. (אֶלָּא בַּעֲמִידָתָהּ "only when she stands"): the charge before the accused — a procedure parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "I TOOK, CAME NEAR TO HER AND FOUND NO TOKENS" — HERE ARE WITNESSES THAT SHE PLAYED THE HARLOT IN HER FATHER'S HOUSE. (הֲרֵי עֵדִים שֶׁזִּינְּתָה בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ "here are witnesses that she fornicated in her father's house"): THE CHARGE IS WITNESSES — the husband's claim carried by witnesses of her unchastity while betrothed (Ketubot 4:3's case), not the cloth alone; the compile's evidence parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE" — WHY SAID? "A MAN WHO COMMITS ADULTERY WITH A MAN'S WIFE" (Leviticus 20:10) — WITNESSES IN HER HUSBAND'S HOUSE THAT SHE SINNED IN HER FATHER'S, OR IN HER FATHER'S HOUSE THAT SHE SINNED THERE — JUDGED AT THE CITY GATE? THE VERSE TAKES OUT THE FIRST: SHE IS JUDGED AT HER FATHER'S DOOR. (עַל פֶּתַח בֵּית אָבִיהָ "at the door of her father's house"): THE PLACE OF EXECUTION by the witnesses' timing — the father's door for the bride (22:21), the gate for the betrothed girl (22:24); two arms of one status. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:7 — CONTEXT. On 22:13. "AND HATES HER" — R. ISHMAEL SAYS: COME AND SEE WHAT HATRED CAUSES — IT BRINGS TO EVIL SPEECH. (לִידֵי לָשׁוֹן הָרָע "to evil speech"): the second homily on the hater; a row citing nothing in either file. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "THE FATHER OF THE GIRL AND HER MOTHER TAKE" (22:15) — ONLY WITH A FATHER AND A MOTHER? A FATHER WITHOUT A MOTHER, A MOTHER WITHOUT A FATHER, NEITHER — "THE GIRL" ANYWHERE; WHY "FATHER AND MOTHER"? THEY WHO RAISED BAD OFFSPRING COME AND ARE DISGRACED WITH THEM. (לֹא אָב וְלֹא אֵם מִנַּיִן "neither father nor mother — whence?"; שֶׁגִּדְּלוּ גִּדּוּלִים רָעִים "who raised bad offspring"): the orphan bride inside the law; THE SPINE WRITES "THE GIRL" IN FULL where the Torah writes her as a boy (the ink's find 13 — the citation carries the read form). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:9 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "AND BRING OUT THE GIRL'S TOKENS" — AS IT SOUNDS. (כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ "as it sounds"): the tokens literal — the cloth (237 the fuller treatment). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "THE FATHER OF THE GIRL SAYS TO THE ELDERS" (22:16) — HENCE A WOMAN HAS NO STANDING TO SPEAK IN A MAN'S PLACE. (שֶׁאֵין רְשׁוּת לָאִשָּׁה לְדַבֵּר בִּמְקוֹם הָאִישׁ "that a woman has no standing to speak in the man's place"): the father the speaker, not the mother — a procedure parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 235:11 — MATERIAL. On 22:13. "MY DAUGHTER I GAVE TO THIS MAN" — THE FATHER HAS THE RIGHT TO BETROTH HIS MINOR DAUGHTER. (לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת בִּתּוֹ קְטַנָּה "to betroth his minor daughter"): THE FATHER'S RIGHT over the minor — a status of the family daemon (Kiddushin 2:1 the answer sheet). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 236:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:16-17 (headless — inside the father's words). "TO WIFE, AND HE HATES HER; AND HE LAYS A CHARGE OF WORDS, SAYING: I FOUND NO TOKENS IN YOUR DAUGHTER — AND THESE ARE MY DAUGHTER'S TOKENS" (22:16-17) — HERE ARE WITNESSES TO REFUTE HIS WITNESSES. The head row of the headless piska 236 (הֲרֵי עֵדִים לְהַזִּים עֵדָיו שֶׁל זֶה "here are witnesses to refute this one's witnesses"): THE TOKENS ARE COUNTER-WITNESSES — the plotting witnesses' law (19:18-19) inside the bride's case: the father's "tokens" refute the husband's witnesses, and 22:18-19's penalty is the plotter's; the compile's CALL on the false-witness cell. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 236:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:16-17 (headless — inside the father's words). R. JUDAH SAYS: HE IS NEVER LIABLE UNLESS HE HAD INTERCOURSE. (אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן בָּעַל "unless he had intercourse"): 235:1's condition repeated for the fine — R. Judah's arm. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 236:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:16-17 (headless — inside the father's words). "THE FATHER OF THE GIRL SAYS" — THE PLAINTIFF OPENS THE PLEADING FIRST. (שֶׁתּוֹבֵעַ מַתְחִיל בַּדְּבָרִים תְּחִלָּה "that the plaintiff begins the words first"): COURT ORDER — the claimant speaks first (Bava Kamma 46b the Talmud's rule "the claimant opens", not opened); a procedure parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 236:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:16-17 (headless — inside the father's words). "MARRIED TO A HUSBAND" (22:22) — R. ISHMAEL SAYS: THE VERSE TEACHES ON THE LEVIRATE WIDOW THAT ONE WHO LIES WITH HER IS NOT LIABLE UNTIL SHE IS BEDDED. (עַל הַיְּבָמָה "on the levirate widow"; עַד שֶׁתִּבָּעֵל "until she is bedded"): 22:22 read inside piska 236 for the yevamah (the widow awaiting her husband's brother) — her status "married" begins at the levir's act; the adultery cell's condition on 25:5's status ahead. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 237:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:17. "AND THEY SPREAD THE GARMENT" (22:17) — THEY MAKE THE WORDS CLEAR AS A GARMENT; ONE OF THE THINGS R. ISHMAEL EXPOUNDED FROM THE TORAH AS A PARABLE — "IF THE SUN ROSE ON HIM" (Exodus 22:2): DOES THE SUN RISE ON HIM ALONE? AS THE SUN IS PEACE TO THE WORLD, SO IF HE KNEW THE THIEF WAS AT PEACE WITH HIM AND KILLED HIM — LIABLE; "ON HIS STAFF" (Exodus 21:19) — ON HIS HEALTH; SO "THEY SPREAD THE GARMENT". The head row of piska 237 (יְחַוְּרוּ דְּבָרִים כַּשִּׂמְלָה "they make the words clear as a garment"; בְּמָשָׁל "as a parable"): THE GARMENT A FIGURE — the evidence made plain, not the cloth itself; R. Ishmael's three parables of the law (Mekhilta Neziqin 6) — the compile reads "the garment" as the proof's clarity. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 237:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:17. R. AKIVA SAYS: "THEY SPREAD THE GARMENT BEFORE THE ELDERS OF THE CITY" — THE HUSBAND'S WITNESSES ARE FOUND PLOTTING; "THEY SPREAD THE GARMENT" — THIS ONE'S WITNESSES AND THAT ONE'S COME AND SPEAK BEFORE THE ELDERS. (נִמְצְאוּ עֵדֵי הַבַּעַל זוֹמְמִים "the husband's witnesses are found plotting"): THE PLOTTING WITNESSES NAMED — 19:18-19's law inside the bride's case (236:1's reading made explicit); the two parties' witnesses before the court. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 237:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:17. R. ELIEZER B. JACOB SAYS: THE WORDS AS THEY ARE WRITTEN. (דְּבָרִים כִּכְתָבָם "the words as written"): the garment literal — three readings of one clause (the parable, the witnesses, the letter); the compile carries the witnesses' arm with the cloth as evidence. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "THE ELDERS OF THAT CITY TAKE THE MAN" (22:18) — AND NOT THE MINOR. The head row of piska 238 (וְלֹא אֶת הַקָּטָן "and not the minor"): the slanderer's age condition — an adult (245:1 the rapist's twin). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "AND CHASTISE HIM" — WITH LASHES. (בְּמַכּוֹת "with lashes"): the chastisement the court's lashes — the first of the slanderer's two penalties. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "AND FINE HIM" (22:19) — MONEY. (מָמוֹן "money"): the second penalty — lashes and money together for the slanderer (the one case of both, Makkot 1:3's ground). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "A HUNDRED OF SILVER" — TYRIAN SILVER. (כֶסֶף צוֹרִי "Tyrian silver"): THE COIN'S STANDARD — the fine in Tyrian silver, the heavy coin (Onkelos "a hundred sela of silver" — the ink's find 15); the compile's money PARAMETER. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "AND GIVE TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" — THAT THEY BE HIS. (שֶׁיְּהוּ שֶׁלּוֹ "that they be his"): the fine the father's property. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "AND GIVE TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" — AND NOT TO THE ADULT WOMAN'S FATHER; "TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" — EXCLUDING A CONVERT CONCEIVED NOT IN HOLINESS AND BORN IN HOLINESS, WHO HAS NO HUNDRED OF SILVER. (וְלֹא לַאֲבִי הַבּוֹגֶרֶת "and not to the adult woman's father"; פְּרָט לְגִיּוֹרֶת "excluding a convert"): THE AGE PARAMETER — the na'arah (a girl of twelve to twelve and a half) against the bogeret (the adult) — and the convert's exclusion (Ketubot 4:3 the answer sheet). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "FOR HE BROUGHT AN EVIL NAME" — NOT ON HER ALONE, BUT ON ALL THE VIRGINS OF ISRAEL. (עַל כָּל בְּתוּלוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל "on all the virgins of Israel"): the slander's reach the penalty's ground (240:3 the bride's twin). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "AND SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE" — HE DRINKS FROM HIS OWN POT: EVEN LAME, EVEN BLIND, EVEN SMITTEN WITH BOILS; A LEWD MATTER FOUND IN HER, OR SHE IS UNFIT TO ENTER ISRAEL — MAY HE KEEP HER? "SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE" — A WIFE FIT FOR HIM. (שֶׁשּׁוֹתֶה בַּעֲצִיצוֹ "that he drinks from his pot"; אִשָּׁה הָרְאוּיָה לוֹ "a wife fit for him"): THE FORCED MARRIAGE and its one exception — the forbidden union (Ketubot 3:5 the answer sheet); the compile's status "his wife, undivorceable" with the fitness condition. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:9 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. "HE MAY NOT SEND HER AWAY ALL HIS DAYS" — EVEN AFTER TIME. (וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר זְמַן "even after time"): the bar permanent. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 238:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:18. ["ALL HIS DAYS"] — HE SENDS HER TO THE LEVIR. (מְשַׁלְּחָהּ הוּא לְיָבָם "he sends her to the levir"): the one release — death without a son sends her to the husband's brother (25:5 ahead; 245:7 the twin close): the levirate's pointer inside chapter 22. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 239:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:20. "IF THE THING WAS TRUE" (22:20) — ONLY IN HER NATURAL WAY? UNNATURAL FROM "WAS"; MIGHT I INCLUDE INTERCOURSE IN ANOTHER PLACE? "THIS". The head row of piska 239 (שֶׁלֹּא כְּדַרְכָּהּ מִנַּיִן "not in her way — whence?"): the charge's substance widened and bounded by two words. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 239:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:20. "NO TOKENS WERE FOUND FOR THE GIRL" — THERE ARE NO WITNESSES TO REFUTE HIS WITNESSES. (אֵין עֵדִים לְהָזֵם עֵדָיו שֶׁל זֶה "there are no witnesses to refute this one's witnesses"): 236:1's mirror — the "tokens" are witnesses on both arms; the true charge is the unrefuted testimony. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 239:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:20. "BRING THE GIRL OUT TO HER FATHER'S DOOR" (22:21) — A FATHER AND A FATHER'S DOOR ONLY? A DOOR AND NO FATHER — "BRING THE GIRL OUT" ANYWHERE; WHY "HER FATHER'S DOOR"? A COMMANDMENT. (פֶּתַח בֵּית אָב וְאֵין לָהּ אָב מִנַּיִן "a father's door and no father — whence?"; מִצְוָה "a commandment"): the place of execution preferred, not conditioned (Ketubot 4:3) — the orphan bride stoned all the same. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 240:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:21. "AND ALL THE MEN OF HER CITY STONE HER" (22:21) — DO ALL THE MEN OF HER CITY STONE HER? IN THE PRESENCE OF ALL THE MEN OF HER CITY. The head row of piska 240 (בְּמַעֲמַד כָּל אַנְשֵׁי עִירָהּ "in the presence of all the men of her city"): the stoning public — 220:1's words for the rebellious son. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 240:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:21. "WITH STONES" — MANY STONES? "WITH A STONE" (Leviticus 20:2); ONE STONE? "WITH STONES" — IF SHE DIED NOT BY THE FIRST, LET HER DIE BY THE SECOND. (אִם לֹא מֵתָה בָרִאשׁוֹנָה תָּמוּת בַּשְּׁנִיָּה "if she did not die by the first she dies by the second"): THE STONING'S PROCEDURE — the stone and the stones (Sifrei Numbers 114; Sanhedrin 6:4 the answer sheet's rite); the compile's stoning cell reused. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 240:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:21. "FOR SHE COMMITTED FOLLY IN ISRAEL" — NOT HERSELF ALONE SHE DISGRACED, BUT ALL THE VIRGINS OF ISRAEL. (כָּל בְּתוּלוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל "all the virgins of Israel"): the folly's reach (238:7's twin on the other side). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 240:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:21. "TO PLAY THE HARLOT IN HER FATHER'S HOUSE" — "HER FATHER" HERE AND "HER FATHER" THERE (Leviticus 21:9): AS THERE HARLOTRY UNDER A HUSBAND'S BOND, SO HERE. (זְנוּת עִם זִיקַת הַבַּעַל "harlotry with a husband's bond"): THE PRIEST'S DAUGHTER'S ANALOGY (I2) — the bride's sin is adultery while betrothed, not unchastity before (Sifra Emor the parallel); the compile's predicate. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 240:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:21. "AND PURGE THE EVIL FROM YOUR MIDST" — PURGE THE DOERS OF EVIL FROM ISRAEL. (בַּעֵר עוֹשֵׂי הָרָעוֹת מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל "purge the doers of evil from Israel"): THE PURGE FORMULA'S SIXTH SEAT of nine read as persons (155:9's words, 86:2 the first) — the effect on the doer. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "IF A MAN BE FOUND" (22:22) — BY WITNESSES; "LYING WITH A WOMAN" — ANY LYING; "MARRIED TO A HUSBAND" — TO INCLUDE ONE BEDDED IN HER FATHER'S HOUSE WHILE STILL BETROTHED. The head row of piska 241 (בְּעֵדִים "by witnesses"; וַעֲדַיִן הִיא אֲרוּסָה "and she is still betrothed"): the adulterer's three conditions — witnesses, any act, the married or betrothed woman. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "AND THEY SHALL DIE" — THE UNSPECIFIED DEATH SAID IN THE TORAH: STRANGLING. (בְּחֶנֶק "by strangling"): THE DEFAULT DEATH — where the Torah names no mode, strangulation (Sanhedrin 7:1-3's rule; 155:1's precedent for the rebellious elder); the adulterer's mode the compile's parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "BOTH OF THEM" — AND NOT THOSE WHO DO HEROD'S DEED. (מַעֲשֵׂה הִירוֹדָס "Herod's deed"): the dead woman no adultery — the living pair the condition (the Talmud's tale of Herod, Bava Batra 3b, not opened). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. WHEN IT SAYS "EVEN BOTH" — TO INCLUDE THOSE WHO COME AFTER THEM. (לְרַבּוֹת הַבָּאִים מֵאַחֲרֵיהֶם "to include those who come after them"): the later partners liable too — "even" the widening word. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "THE MAN LYING WITH THE WOMAN" — EVEN THOUGH SHE IS A MINOR. (שֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה "that she is a minor"): the man liable for a married minor. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "AND THE WOMAN" — EVEN THOUGH SHE WAS BEDDED BY A MINOR. (שֶׁנִּבְעֲלָה לְקָטָן "that she was bedded by a minor"): the woman liable though the man is not — the two arms of "both". Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 241:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:22. "AND PURGE THE EVIL" — PURGE THE DOERS OF EVIL FROM ISRAEL. (בַּעֵר עוֹשֵׂי הָרָעוֹת מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל "purge the doers of evil from Israel"): the formula's seventh seat, "from Israel" (17:12 its other) — the effect on the pair. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "IF THERE BE A VIRGIN GIRL BETROTHED TO A MAN" (22:23) — HE IS NOT LIABLE UNTIL SHE IS A GIRL, A VIRGIN, BETROTHED TO A MAN. The head row of piska 242 (עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא נַעֲרָה בְתוּלָה מְאֹרָשָׂה לְאִישׁ "until she is a girl, a virgin, betrothed to a man"): THE THREE CONDITIONS of the stoning — age, virginity, betrothal — the cell's predicate. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "AND A MAN FOUND HER IN THE CITY" — HAD SHE NOT GONE OUT IN THE CITY, HE WOULD NOT HAVE MET HER. (לֹא הָיָה מִסְתַּקֵּף לָהּ "he would not have happened on her"): the city's presumption — her going out the ground of "she did not cry" (243:3 unmakes the place as a condition). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "IN THE CITY, AND HE LAY WITH HER" — THE BREACH CALLS THE THIEF. (שֶׁהַפִּרְצָה קוֹרְאָה לַגַּנָּב "that the breach calls the thief"): the proverb of the breach (the English adds "any lying" — in the English alone). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "BRING BOTH OF THEM OUT TO THE GATE OF THAT CITY" (22:24) — THIS IS WHAT WE SAID: THE GATE WHERE THEY WERE FOUND, NOT THE GATE WHERE THEY WERE JUDGED. (שַׁעַר שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ בוֹ וְלֹא שַׁעַר שֶׁנִּדּוֹנוּ בוֹ "the gate where they were found and not the gate where they were judged"): THE PLACE OF EXECUTION — the city of the deed (148's rule for the idolater at 17:5); the compile's parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "AND STONE THEM WITH STONES" — MANY? "WITH A STONE" (Leviticus 20:27); ONE? "WITH STONES" — IF THEY DIED NOT BY THE FIRST, LET THEM DIE BY THE SECOND. (יָמוּתוּ בַּשְּׁנִיָּה "let them die by the second"): 240:2's rite for the pair (Sifrei Numbers 114). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "THE GIRL BECAUSE SHE DID NOT CRY OUT" — WHEN IT SAYS "BECAUSE OF THE WORD" — BY A WARNING: TO INCLUDE THE DELIBERATE ONE WARNED BY WITNESSES. (עַל פִּי הַתְרָאָה "by a warning"; בְּהַתְרָיַת עֵדִים "by the witnesses' warning"): THE WARNING (hatra'ah) — the capital penalty conditioned on the witnesses' warning, read from "because of the word" (250:1 the same reading ahead); the compile's condition on every stoning cell. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "AND THE MAN BECAUSE HE HUMBLED HIS NEIGHBOR'S WIFE" — WHEN IT SAYS "BECAUSE OF THE WORD" — BY A WARNING. (עַל פִּי הַתְרָיָה "by a warning"): the man's warning as the woman's — the condition on both. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:8 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. ONLY IN THE CITY? IN THE FIELD FROM "IF IN THE FIELD THE MAN FINDS HER" (22:25). (בַּשָּׂדֶה מִנַּיִן "in the field — whence?"): the second arm opened. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:9 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "AND HE LAY WITH HER" — EXCLUDING ONE WHO HOLDS AND ONE WHO LIES: R. JUDAH. (פְּרָט לְשֶׁאֶחָד מַחֲזִיק וְאֶחָד שׁוֹכֵב "excluding one holding and one lying"): the accomplice's liability a DISPUTED cell (R. Judah excludes the holder from the rapist's death). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 242:10 — MATERIAL. On 22:23. "AND THE MAN WHO LAY WITH HER ALONE DIES" — THE FIRST BY STONING, THE SECOND BY STRANGLING. (הָרִאשׁוֹן בִּסְקִילָה וְהַשֵּׁנִי בְּחֶנֶק "the first by stoning and the second by strangling"): THE STATUS MOVES WITH THE FIRST ACT — the betrothed virgin's violator stoned, her next violator strangled as an adulterer (Sanhedrin 7:9 the answer sheet); the compile's status change on the girl. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 243:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:26. "AND TO THE GIRL DO NOTHING" (22:26) — THE VERSE EXEMPTS HER FROM DEATH; FROM THE OFFERING TOO? "SIN"; FROM LASHES TOO? "SIN OF DEATH". The head row of piska 243 (שֶׁפְּטָרָהּ הַכָּתוּב מִן הַמִּיתָה "that the verse exempted her from death"; אַף מִן הַמַּכּוֹת "even from the lashes"): THE COERCED EXEMPT from all three — death, offering, lashes — the sanction table's three columns cleared. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 243:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:26. "FOR AS A MAN RISES AGAINST HIS NEIGHBOR AND MURDERS HIM" — ALL THE COERCED IN THE TORAH ARE EXEMPT, AND THEY ARE SAVED AT THE COST OF LIFE; ONLY THIS? THE PURSUER AFTER HIS FELLOW TO KILL HIM AND AFTER A MALE — "SO IS THIS THING"; EVEN THE PURSUER AFTER A BEAST, THE SABBATH-PROFANER, THE IDOLATER? "THIS" — THIS IS BY STONING AND NOT ALL THESE. (שֶׁכָּל אֲנוּסִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה פְּטוּרִים "that all the coerced in the Torah are exempt"; וּמַצִּילִים אוֹתָם בְּנַפְשָׁם "and they save them at the cost of his life"): THE PURSUER'S LAW born here — the rescue by the pursuer's life for the murderer and the rapist (Sanhedrin 8:7 the answer sheet — the beast, the Sabbath and the idol excluded); the compile's new cell on the coerced and the pursuer. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 243:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:26. "FOR IN THE FIELD HE FOUND HER" (22:27) — IN THE CITY LIABLE, IN THE FIELD EXEMPT? "THE BETROTHED GIRL CRIED OUT AND NONE SAVED HER" — IF SHE HAS A SAVIOR, CITY OR FIELD, LIABLE; IF NONE, CITY OR FIELD, EXEMPT. (בֵּין בָּעִיר בֵּין בַּשָּׂדֶה פְּטוּרָה "whether in the city or in the field — exempt"): THE SAVIOR IS THE CONDITION, NOT THE PLACE — the city and the field the Torah's examples (Mekhilta Neziqin 13); the two arms collapse into one predicate. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 243:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:26. "SHE CRIED OUT" — EXCLUDING ONE WHO SAID "LET HIM BE": R. JUDAH. (פְּרָט לְשֶׁאָמְרָה הַנַּח לוֹ "excluding one who said: let him be"): the consent after the beginning — R. Judah's exclusion. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 244:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:28. "IF A MAN FINDS A VIRGIN GIRL" (22:28) — "VIRGIN" HERE AND "VIRGIN" IN THE SEDUCER (Exodus 22:15): IF THE GRAVE RAPIST IS LIABLE ONLY FOR A VIRGIN, THE LIGHT SEDUCER SURELY; OR THE REVERSE; WHY "VIRGIN" IN THE RAPIST? EXCLUDING THE BEDDED, AND THE ONE STRUCK BY WOOD; "NOT BETROTHED" HERE AND THERE — THE BETROTHED AND DIVORCED; SAID TWICE, THAT HE GIVE NEITHER TO HER NOR TO HER FATHER; "GIRL" HERE AND NOT IN THE SEDUCER — "TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" (22:29), NOT THE ADULT'S; THE A FORTIORI CANNOT BE REVERSED, FOR "GIRL" IS SAID TWICE. The head row of piska 244 (פְּרָט לִבְעוּלָה "excluding the bedded"; מֻכַּת עֵץ מִנַּיִן "the one struck by wood — whence?"; קַל וָחֹמֶר "a fortiori"): THE RAPIST AND THE SEDUCER PAIRED BY THREE A FORTIORI ARGUMENTS (I1) with their reversals — the exclusions the parameters (the bedded, the wood-struck, the divorced betrothed, the adult; Ketubot 3:1-4 the answer sheet's table); the largest row of the chapter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 244:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:28. "AND HE LAY WITH HER" — ANY LYING. (כָּל שְׁכִיבָה "any lying"): 241:1's clause for the rapist. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 244:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:28. "AND THEY ARE FOUND" — BY WITNESSES. (בְּעֵדִים "by witnesses"): the fine on testimony, not confession. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:1 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "AND THE MAN GIVES" (22:29) — AND NOT THE MINOR. The head row of piska 245 (וְלֹא הַקָּטָן "and not the minor"): the rapist's age condition (238:1's twin). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:2 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "WHO LAY WITH HER" — ANY LYING; "TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" — THAT THEY BE HIS. (שֶׁיְּהוּ שֶׁלּוֹ "that they be his"): the fifty the father's (238:5's twin). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:3 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "TO THE GIRL'S FATHER" — AND NOT TO THE ADULT WOMAN'S FATHER. (וְלֹא לַאֲבִי הַבּוֹגֶרֶת "and not to the adult woman's father"): the age parameter again (238:6). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:4 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "FIFTY OF SILVER" — TYRIAN SILVER. (כָּסֶף צוֹרִי "Tyrian silver"): the coin's standard for the fifty as for the hundred (238:4). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:5 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "AND SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE" — HE DRINKS FROM HIS OWN POT: EVEN LAME, BLIND, SMITTEN WITH BOILS; A LEWD MATTER OR UNFIT TO ENTER ISRAEL — MAY HE KEEP HER? "SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE" — A WIFE FIT FOR HIM. (בְּאִשָּׁה הָרְאוּיָה לוֹ "a wife fit for him"): 238:8's forced marriage and its exception for the rapist (Ketubot 3:5). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:6 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "BECAUSE HE HUMBLED HER" — TO INCLUDE THE ORPHAN; HENCE: AN ORPHAN WIDOWED OR DIVORCED — R. ELIEZER SAYS: THE RAPIST IS LIABLE AND THE SEDUCER EXEMPT. (לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַיְּתוֹמָה "to include the orphan"; הָאוֹנֵס חַיָּב וְהַמְפַתֶּה פָּטוּר "the rapist is liable and the seducer exempt"): THE ORPHAN'S FINE HER OWN — the rapist pays her, the seducer nothing (Ketubot 3:6 the answer sheet); the fatherless girl a status arm. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 245:7 — MATERIAL. On 22:29. "HE MAY NOT SEND HER AWAY ALL HIS DAYS" — HE SENDS HER TO THE LEVIR. (מְשַׁלְּחָהּ הוּא לְיָבָם "he sends her to the levir"): 238:10's close for the rapist's wife — the two undivorceable wives released only to the husband's brother (25:5); the chapter's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 246:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:1. "A MAN SHALL NOT TAKE HIS FATHER'S WIFE" (23:1) — HENCE: ONE MARRIES THE KIN OF A WOMAN HE RAPED OR SEDUCED, BUT THE RAPIST OR SEDUCER OF A MARRIED WOMAN'S KIN IS LIABLE; A MAN MARRIES THE WOMAN HIS FATHER OR HIS SON RAPED OR SEDUCED; R. JUDAH FORBIDS HIS FATHER'S. The head row of piska 246 — the chapter's first verse, the English Bible's 22:30 (נוֹשְׂאִים עַל הָאֲנוּסָה וְעַל הַמְּפוּתָּה "one marries the kin of the raped and the seduced"; וְהָאוֹנֵס וְהַמְפַתֶּה עַל הַנְּשׂוּאָה חַיָּב "the rapist and the seducer of the married woman's kin is liable"; רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹסֵר "R. Judah forbids"): THE FATHER'S WIFE the marriage (Leviticus 18:8's twin), not the rape — a rape makes no kinship; Yevamot 11:1 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=", the Hebrew cites no tractate). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 247:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:2. "ONE WITH A CRUSHING WOUND SHALL NOT ENTER" (23:2) — WHAT IS THE CRUSHED? ANY WHOSE TESTICLES WERE WOUNDED, EVEN ONE OF THEM; ONLY ALL OF THEM? WHENCE EVEN PART? THE TEACHING SAYS "CRUSHED". The head row of piska 247 (אֵיזֶהוּ פְּצוּעַ דַּכָּא "what is one with a crushing wound?"; שֶׁנִּפְצְעוּ הַבֵּיצִים שֶׁלּוֹ "whose testicles were wounded"; וַאֲפִלּוּ אַחַת מֵהֶן "even one of them"): THE CLASS DEFINED by the organ and widened to a part — Yevamot 8:2 the answer sheet's row; the compile's status on a class of persons (the design's (c)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 247:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:2. "AND ONE WITH A SEVERED MEMBER" — ANY WHOSE MEMBER IS CUT. (שֶׁנִּכְרַת הַגִּיד "whose member is cut"): the second class of 23:2; Yevamot 8:2 again. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 247:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:2. R. ISHMAEL B. R. YOHANAN B. BEROKA: I HEARD IN THE VINEYARD AT YAVNEH — ONE WITH ONLY ONE TESTICLE IS A EUNUCH OF THE SUN (BORN SO); THE CRUSHED MAY HEAL, THE SEVERED NEVER — THE HEALERS' TRADITION. (שָׁמַעְתִּי בְּכֶרֶם בְּיַבְנֶה "I heard in the vineyard at Yavneh"; סְרִיס חַמָּה "a eunuch of the sun"; זוֹ הִלְכַּת רוֹפְאִים "this is the healers' tradition"): the two classes told apart by prognosis — a PARAMETER the physicians supply, not the verse; the congenital eunuch a third class outside the verse. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 247:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:2. "INTO THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD" — R. JUDAH: FOUR ASSEMBLIES — PRIESTS, LEVITES, ISRAEL, PROSELYTES. (אַרְבַּע קְהִלּוֹת הֵן "there are four assemblies"; קְהַל גֵּרִים "the assembly of proselytes"): the assembly of the LORD (find 8's four seats, all here) partitioned — R. Judah's four. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 247:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:2. THE SAGES SAY: THREE. (וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים שָׁלֹשׁ "and the sages say: three"): the proselytes no assembly of their own — the crushed proselyte may marry a proselyte woman (Kiddushin 4:1 the English's "cf."); a disputed PARAMETER on the classes. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 248:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:3. "NO MAMZER SHALL ENTER THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD" (23:3) — MAN OR WOMAN; "NO MAMZER" — WHOEVER IS ONE. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 257:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:13. "WHEN YOU SIT OUTSIDE" — AND NOT SITTING. (וְלֹא בִּישִׁיבָה "and not in sitting"): the Hebrew's exclusion (the translator reads it as the standing need) — the peg's duty for the one need alone. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 257:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:13. "DIG WITH IT, RETURN AND COVER YOUR EXCREMENT" — DIG WITH ONE AND COVER WITH ANOTHER? THE TEACHING SAYS "DIG WITH IT". (חוֹפֵר בְּאַחַת וּמְכַסֶּה בְּאַחַת "dig with one and cover with one"): one peg for both acts. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 257:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:13. R. SHIMON: WHENCE THAT A MAN NOT TURN HIS LOINS TOWARD THE SOUTH? "RETURN AND COVER". (הוֹפֵךְ מָתְנָיו כְּלַפֵּי דָּרוֹם "turn his loins toward the south"): the sanctuary's direction (from the Galilee) honored — a PLACE parameter the Tosefta names (the English's Megillah 4:26). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 258:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_06_vaetchanan_2026-09-17.md). On 23:15. "FOR THE LORD YOUR GOD WALKS IN THE MIDST OF YOUR CAMP" (23:15) — HENCE: A MAN SHALL NOT RECITE THE SHEMA BESIDE THE LAUNDERERS' VAT, NOR ENTER A BATHHOUSE OR TANNERY WITH SCROLLS AND PHYLACTERIES IN HIS HAND. The head row of piska 258 — READ BEFORE at chapter 6's ledger (the Shema's recital), REREAD WHOLE here (לֹא יִקְרָא אָדָם אֶת שְׁמַע "a man shall not recite the Shema"; מִשְׁרָה שֶׁל כּוֹבְסִים "the launderers' soaking vat"; וּסְפָרִים וּתְפִלִּין בְּיָדוֹ "scrolls and phylacteries in his hand"): the camp's holiness carried to the recital and the holy objects — Berakhot 3:5 the English's "cf."; the Presence walking a heaven entry (the design's (d)). Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 258:2 — CONTEXT. On 23:15. "TO SAVE YOU AND GIVE YOUR ENEMIES BEFORE YOU" — IF YOU DID ALL THAT IS SAID IN THE MATTER, IN THE END HE SAVES YOU. (אִם עָשִׂיתָ כָּל הָאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן "if you did all that is said in the matter"): the promise conditioned on the camp's laws — 200:3's trope (the English). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 258:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:15. "AND YOUR CAMP SHALL BE HOLY" — SANCTIFY IT; HENCE: A MAN SHALL NOT ENTER THE TEMPLE MOUNT WITH HIS STAFF, HIS SHOE, HIS MONEY-BELT OR THE DUST ON HIS FEET. (קַדְּשֵׁהוּ "sanctify it"; לְהַר הַבַּיִת בְּמַקְלוֹ וּבְמִנְעָלוֹ וּבַאֲפֻנְדָּתוֹ "to the Temple Mount with his staff, his shoe and his money-belt"): Berakhot 9:5 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the camp of the Presence (255:5) is the Mount; the verse's "shall be" read as "make it". Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 258:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:15. "THAT HE SEE NO UNSEEMLY THING IN YOU AND TURN FROM YOU" — LEWDNESS REMOVES THE PRESENCE. (שֶׁעֲרָיוֹת מְסַלְּקוֹת אֶת הַשְּׁכִינָה "lewdness removes the Presence"): 254:3's prototype stated for itself — the effect on the heaven ledger; Onkelos's "His Memra turn from doing you good" (23:15's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "DELIVER NOT A SLAVE TO HIS MASTER" (23:16) — HENCE: HE WHO SELLS HIS SLAVE TO GENTILES OR ABROAD — THE SLAVE GOES OUT FREE. The head row of piska 259 (הַמּוֹכֵר עַבְדּוֹ לְגוֹיִם "he who sells his slave to gentiles"; אוֹ לְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ "or abroad"; יָצָא בֶן חוֹרִים "goes out a free man"): THE SLAVE FREED by the sale itself — an effect on his status (the design's (e)); the Hebrew cites no tractate here. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "WHO ESCAPES TO YOU FROM HIS MASTER" — TO INCLUDE THE RESIDENT SOJOURNER. (לְרַבּוֹת גֵּר תּוֹשָׁב "to include the resident sojourner"): the class widened. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "HE SHALL DWELL WITH YOU" (23:17) — NOT IN A CITY BY HIMSELF; "IN YOUR MIDST" — NOT ON THE FRONTIER. (וְלֹא בָּעִיר עַצְמוֹ "and not in a city by himself"; וְלֹא בַּסְּפָר "and not on the frontier"): two PLACE parameters — among Israel, inland. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "IN THE PLACE HE CHOOSES" — WHERE HIS LIVELIHOOD COMES OUT. (שֶׁפַּרְנָסָתוֹ יוֹצְאָה "where his livelihood comes out"): his choice by his trade. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:5 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei_2026-09-24.md). On 23:16. "IN ONE OF YOUR GATES" — IN YOUR GATES AND NOT IN JERUSALEM; "IN ONE OF YOUR GATES" — THAT HE NOT WANDER FROM CITY TO CITY. READ BEFORE at chapters 19-21's ledger (the rebellious son's "anywhere but Jerusalem" — the English's Tosefta note), REREAD WHOLE here (בִּשְׁעָרֶיךָ וְלֹא בִּירוּשָׁלִַם "in your gates and not in Jerusalem"; שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא גּוֹלֶה מֵעִיר לְעִיר "that he not wander from city to city"): Jerusalem no tribe's inheritance, so no gate of his; the parser's [1] "one of your gates" read as ONE city — a status fixed to a place. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "WHERE IT IS GOOD FOR HIM" — FROM A BAD DWELLING TO A FINE ONE. (מִנָּוֶה הָרָע לְנָוֶה הַיָּפֶה "from a bad dwelling to a fine one"): the good his betterment — 251:2's foil. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:7 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. "OPPRESS HIM NOT" — THIS IS OPPRESSION BY WORDS. (זוֹ הוֹנָאַת דְּבָרִים "this is oppression by words"): Bava Metzia 4:10's verbal wrong (the English's "cf." — "remember your fathers' deeds" forbidden) — the effect a prohibition on speech. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 259:8 — MATERIAL. On 23:16. ANOTHER WORD: "DELIVER NOT A SLAVE TO HIS MASTER" — SCRIPTURE SPEAKS OF A GENTILE WHO FLED FROM IDOLATRY.
