(בְּגוֹי שֶׁנִּצַּל מֵעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה "of a gentile who escaped from idolatry"): the second reading — the refugee from the cult; ONKELOS TAKES THIS ONE ("a slave of the nations", 23:16's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 260:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:18. "NO HARLOT OF ISRAEL'S DAUGHTERS" (23:18) — YOU ARE NOT WARNED ABOUT HER AMONG THE NATIONS; "NO MALE OF ISRAEL'S SONS" — NOT WARNED ABOUT HIM AMONG THE NATIONS. THE TWO ARGUMENTS (THE HARLOT THE LIGHTER, THE MALE THE GRAVER) AND THEIR REVERSE; WHY SAY "MALE"? IT NEEDED SAYING — ELSE THE LIGHTER WOULD HAVE TAUGHT THE GRAVER AMONG THE NATIONS. The head row of piska 260 (אִי אַתָּה מֻזְהָר עָלֶיהָ בָּאֻמּוֹת "you are not warned about her among the nations"; קְדֵשָׁה קַלָּה "the harlot the lighter"; קָדֵשׁ חָמוּר "the male the graver"; צָרִיךְ לְאָמְרוֹ "it needed saying"): THE SCOPE Israel alone, both sexes — the redundancy of "male" the block on an a fortiori; Onkelos reads the verse as marriage with slaves (23:18's row), the spine as harlotry. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 260:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:18. ANOTHER WORD: "NO HARLOT" — A WARNING TO THE UNATTACHED WOMAN, AS GENESIS 38:21 "THERE WAS NO HARLOT HERE". (זוֹ אַזְהָרָה לַמֻּפְנָה "this is a warning to the unattached woman (mufnah)"; לֹא הָיְתָה בָזֶה קְדֵשָׁה״ "there was no harlot here"): TAMAR'S VERSE — the tape's Genesis 38 (Judah's levirate, 25:5's readback) cited for the harlot's name; the rare-text row (the English's note). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:19. "BRING NOT A HARLOT'S HIRE" (23:19) — ONLY A HARLOT'S? THE HIRE OF EVERY FORBIDDEN UNION — WHENCE? "HIRE" OF ANY. WHAT IS A HARLOT'S HIRE? "HERE IS THIS LAMB AS YOUR HIRE" — EVEN A HUNDRED, ALL FORBIDDEN; "HERE IS A LAMB, LET YOUR SLAVE-GIRL LIE WITH MY SLAVE" — RABBI: NO HIRE; THE SAGES: A HIRE. The head row of piska 261 (אֶתְנַן כָּל הָעֲרָיוֹת מִנַּיִן "the hire of all the forbidden unions — whence?"; הֵילָךְ טָלֶה זֶה בִּשְׂכָרֵךְ "here is this lamb as your hire"; אֲפִלּוּ מֵאָה כֻּלָּם אֲסוּרִים "even a hundred, all forbidden"; וְתָלִין שִׁפְחָתְךָ אֵצֶל עַבְדִּי "and let your slave-girl lie with my slave"): Temurah 6:2 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the hire's class and the disputed case; the design's DV23-03. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_17_18_shoftim_2026-09-24.md). On 23:19. "OR A DOG'S PRICE" — WHAT IS IT? "HERE IS THIS LAMB IN EXCHANGE FOR THIS DOG"; EVEN IF HE ONLY WALKED IT THROUGH THE COURT, LIABLE? "ABOMINATION" HERE AND "ABOMINATION" THERE (17:1) — AS THERE FOR A SACRIFICE, SO HERE FOR A SACRIFICE. READ BEFORE at chapters 17-18's ledger (17:1's blemished offering), REREAD WHOLE here (אֵיזֶהוּ מְחִיר כֶּלֶב "what is a dog's price?"; תַּחַת כֶּלֶב זֶה "in exchange for this dog"; נֶאֱמַר כָּאן תּוֹעֵבָה וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן תּוֹעֵבָה "'abomination' is said here and said there"; לְשֵׁם זֶבַח "as a sacrifice"): THE ABOMINATION FORMULA'S TWO SEATS (find 5) joined by a verbal analogy — the bar on the altar's offering, not the court's floor; Temurah 6:3. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:19. "A VOW" — EXCLUDING A THING ALREADY VOWED; "ANY VOW" — TO INCLUDE A HIGH PLACE. (פְּרָט לְדָבָר הַנָּדוּר "excluding a thing already vowed"; לְרַבּוֹת בָּמָה "to include a high place (bamah)"): the hire must precede the vow; the private altar's era in (the Tosefta's rows the English names). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:19. "THE HOUSE OF THE LORD YOUR GOD" — EXCLUDING THE PURIFICATION HEIFER, WHICH COMES NOT TO THE HOUSE: R. ELIEZER; THE SAGES: TO INCLUDE THE GOLD PLATES. (פְּרָט לְפָרַת חַטָּאת "excluding the purification heifer"; שֶׁאֵינָהּ בָּאָה לַבַּיִת "which does not come to the House"; לְהָבִיא אֶת הָרִקּוּעִים "to include the gold plates"): Numbers 19's heifer (burned outside) disputed; the sanctuary's plating in — Onkelos's "house of the sanctuary" (23:19's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:19. "ANY VOW" — TO INCLUDE THE BIRD: CONSECRATED BEASTS, WHICH A BLEMISH DISQUALIFIES, TAKE NO HIRE OR PRICE, SO THE BIRD, WHICH NO BLEMISH DISQUALIFIES? "ANY VOW" INCLUDES IT. EVEN THE HIRE OF HER TIME FORBIDDEN? "FOR BOTH OF THEM ARE AN ABOMINATION" — TWO AND NOT FOUR. (לְהָבִיא אֶת הָעוֹף "to include the bird"; שֶׁהַמּוּם פּוֹסֵל בָּהֶם "which a blemish disqualifies"; שְׂכַר פִּקְעָתָהּ "the hire of her time"; שְׁנַיִם וְלֹא אַרְבָּעָה "two and not four"): Temurah 6:4 the answer sheet's row; THE PARSER'S [2] "both of them" (starred) read as a bound — the hire and the price alone, not the harlot's price or the dog's hire. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 261:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:19. "THEM" — AND NOT THEIR OFFSPRING NOR THEIR EXCHANGES. (וְלֹא וְלָדוֹתֵיהֶם וְלֹא חֲלִיפֵיהֶם "not their offspring nor their exchanges"): the bar on the animals themselves — Temurah 6:3's close. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 262:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:20. "BITE NOT YOUR BROTHER" (23:20) — THIS IS THE BORROWER; THE LENDER WHENCE? LEVITICUS 25:36 "TAKE NO BITE OR INCREASE FROM HIM". The head row of piska 262 (זֶה לֹוֶה "this is the borrower"; מַלְוֶה מִנַּיִן "the lender — whence?"): THE TWO PARTIES each under a verse — Deuteronomy the borrower (who pays the bite), Leviticus the lender; the frozen Leviticus 25 cell reused with the borrower's arm (the design's (a)); the design's DV23-03. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 262:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:20. "YOUR SILVER" AND NOT OTHERS' SILVER, "YOUR FOOD" AND NOT OTHERS' FOOD — OR NOT TITHE MONEY, NOT ANIMAL FODDER? "THE BITE OF SILVER" INCLUDES TITHE MONEY, "THE BITE OF FOOD" INCLUDES FODDER; ONLY SILVER AND FOOD? "THE BITE OF ANYTHING THAT BITES". (וְלֹא כֶּסֶף אֲחֵרִים "and not others' silver"; לְרַבּוֹת כֶּסֶף מַעֲשֵׂר "to include tithe money"; לְרַבּוֹת אָכְלֵי בְהֵמָה "to include animal fodder"; מִנַּיִן לְרַבּוֹת כָּל דָּבָר "whence to include everything?"): the three nouns of 23:20 (silver, food, anything) each a widening of Leviticus 25:37's two — the class of the loan's object unbounded. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 262:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:20. R. SHIMON: WHENCE THAT THE LENDER NOT SAY "GO AND ASK AFTER SO-AND-SO", OR "FIND OUT IF SO-AND-SO CAME"? "THE BITE OF ANYTHING THAT BITES". (צֵא וּשְׁאַל שְׁלוֹם פְּלוֹנִי "go and ask after So-and-so's welfare"): an errand is interest — Bava Metzia 5:10's verbal interest (the English's "cf."); the object's class reaches a favor. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 263:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:21. "TO THE FOREIGNER YOU MAY BITE, TO YOUR BROTHER NOT" (23:21) — THE FIRST A POSITIVE COMMANDMENT, THE SECOND A NEGATIVE. The head row of piska 263 (לַנָּכְרִי תַשִּׁיךְ מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "to the foreigner bite — a positive commandment"; מִצְוַת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "a negative commandment"): the foreigner's arm of the interest cell (the design's (a)) — a permission read as a command. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 263:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:21. RABBAN GAMLIEL: WHY "TO YOUR BROTHER YOU SHALL NOT BITE" WHEN 23:20 SAID IT? THERE IS INTEREST IN ADVANCE AND INTEREST AFTER: A GIFT SENT SO THAT HE WILL LEND — IN ADVANCE; REPAID, THEN A GIFT "FOR YOUR MONEY THAT LAY IDLE WITH ME" — AFTER. (יֵשׁ רִבִּית מֻקְדֶּמֶת וְיֵשׁ רִבִּית מְאֻחֶרֶת "there is interest in advance and interest after"; זֶה הוּא רִבִּית מֻקְדֶּמֶת "this is interest in advance"; זֶה הוּא רִבִּית מְאֻחֶרֶת "this is interest after"): Bava Metzia 5:10 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the redundancy of the two verses the two clocks of the gift, before and after the loan. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 263:3 — CONTEXT. On 23:21. "THAT THE LORD YOUR GOD MAY BLESS YOU IN ALL YOU PUT YOUR HAND TO" — SCRIPTURE FIXED HIM A BLESSING IN THE STRETCHING OF THE HAND. (קָבַע לוֹ הַכָּתוּב בְּרָכָה בִּשְׁלִיחוּת יָד "Scripture fixed him a blessing in the stretching of the hand"): the reward trope (64:2's, the English) — the blessing on the work. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 263:4 — CONTEXT. On 23:21. "ON THE LAND YOU ARE COMING TO POSSESS" — AS REWARD FOR COMING YOU WILL POSSESS. (בִּשְׂכַר שֶׁתָּבֹא תִּירַשׁ "as reward for coming you will possess"): 55:3's trope (the English) — the land formula's seat. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "WHEN YOU VOW A VOW TO THE LORD" (23:22) — "VOW" SAID HERE AND SAID THERE (LEVITICUS 7:16): AS THERE THE VOW IS PAIRED WITH THE FREEWILL OFFERING, SO HERE; AS HERE "DELAY NOT TO PAY IT", SO THERE. The head row of piska 264 (נֶאֱמַר כָּאן נֶדֶר וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן נֶדֶר "'vow' is said here and said there"; נֶדֶר וּנְדָבָה "a vow and a freewill offering"; לֹא תְאַחֵר לְשַׁלְּמוֹ "delay not to pay it"): A VERBAL ANALOGY IN BOTH DIRECTIONS — the freewill offering under the delay's bar, the peace offering's vow under it; Numbers 30's vow daemon takes the CLOCK "delay not" (the design's DV23-04; Rosh Hashanah 1:1 the compile's answer sheet, not cited here). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "TO THE LORD YOUR GOD" — THESE ARE VALUATIONS, DEVOTIONS AND CONSECRATIONS. (אֵלּוּ עֲרָכִים וַחֲרָמִים וְהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת "these are valuations, devotions and consecrations"): Leviticus 27's three classes named as the vow's objects — the frozen unit's cells the callees (the design's (e)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "DELAY NOT TO PAY IT" — IT AND NOT ITS EXCHANGE. (הוּא וְלֹא חֲלוּפָיו "it and not its exchanges"): Leviticus 27:10's substitution barred — the vowed beast itself. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "FOR HE WILL SURELY SEEK IT OF YOU" — THESE ARE SIN OFFERINGS AND GUILT OFFERINGS. (אֵלּוּ חַטָּאוֹת וַאֲשָׁמוֹת "these are sin offerings and guilt offerings"): the obligatory offerings under the delay's bar — the infinitive absolute "seek" (find 14's seven) the widening. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "THE LORD YOUR GOD" — THIS IS THE CONSECRATION FOR THE HOUSE'S REPAIR. (זֶה הֶקְדֵּשׁ בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת "this is the consecration for the House's repair"): the sanctuary's fund in. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "OF YOU" — THESE ARE GLEANINGS, THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF AND THE CORNER. (זֶה לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה "gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner"): the poor's dues (24:19-21 ahead, Leviticus 19:9 behind) counted among the delayed — the forgotten sheaf named in chapter 23 before its verse. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 264:7 — MATERIAL. On 23:22. "AND THERE SHALL BE SIN IN YOU" — IN YOU SIN, AND NOT IN YOUR OFFERING. (בְךָ חֵטְא וְלֹא בְּקָרְבָּנְךָ חֵטְא "in you sin and not in your offering"): the delayed offering still fit — the sin the vower's (find 7's formula at its second seat); the effect on the person, not the beast. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "BUT IF YOU REFRAIN FROM VOWING" (23:23) — R. MEIR: ECCLESIASTES 5:4 "BETTER NOT TO VOW THAN TO VOW AND NOT PAY" — BETTER THAN BOTH NOT TO VOW AT ALL; R. JUDAH: BETTER THAN BOTH TO VOW AND PAY. The head row of piska 265 (״טוֹב מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה שֶׁלֹּא תִדֹּר כָּל עִקָּר "better than both not to vow at all"; נוֹדֵר וּמְשַׁלֵּם "one who vows and pays"): the Writings' receipt of 23:22-23 (Ecclesiastes 5:4-5 the verse's twin); the dispute on counsel, not on law — no sin either way (find 7's negative). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "WHAT YOUR LIPS UTTER" (23:24) — A POSITIVE COMMANDMENT. (מִצְוַת עֲשֵׂה "a positive commandment"): 23:24 carries no head — its rows inside 265; the utterance the vow's form (Nedarim 1:1's handles the compile's parameter). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "KEEP" — A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT. (מִצְוַת לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "a negative commandment"): "keep" (shamar) read as a prohibition — the book's usage (4:2). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "AND DO" — A WARNING TO THE COURT TO COMPEL YOU. (אַזְהָרָה לְבֵית דִּין שֶׁיְּעַשּׂוּךָ "a warning to the court to compel you"): THE COURT'S ARM in the vow — the third verb the enforcement (the design's (f)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "AS YOU VOWED" — THIS IS A VOW. (זֶה נֵדֶר "this is a vow"): the first of six words of 23:24 each given a class. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "TO THE LORD YOUR GOD" — VALUATIONS, DEVOTIONS AND CONSECRATIONS. (אֵלּוּ עֲרָכִים וַחֲרָמִים וְהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת "these are valuations, devotions and consecrations"): 264:2 repeated for 23:24. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:7 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "A FREEWILL OFFERING" — THIS IS THE FREEWILL OFFERING. (זוֹ נְדָבָה "this is a freewill offering"): the pair of 264:1. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:8 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "WHICH YOU SPOKE" — THE CONSECRATION FOR THE HOUSE'S REPAIR. (זֶה הֶקְדֵּשׁ בֶּדֶק הַבַּיִת "this is the consecration for the House's repair"): 264:5 repeated. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 265:9 — MATERIAL. On 23:23. "WITH YOUR MOUTH" — THIS IS CHARITY. (זוֹ צְדָקָה "this is charity"): the pledge to the poor a vow the court compels — the last of the six. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. "WHEN YOU COME INTO YOUR FELLOW'S VINEYARD" (23:25) — AT ANY TIME? THE TEACHING SAYS "INTO YOUR VESSEL PUT NOT" — WHILE YOU PUT INTO THE OWNER'S VESSELS. The head row of piska 266 (יָכוֹל לְעוֹלָם "might it be at any time?"; בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאַתָּה נוֹתֵן לְתוֹךְ כֶּלְיוֹ שֶׁל בַּעַל "when you put into the vessels of the owner"): THE LABORER, not the passer-by — the verse's "you" the hired hand at his gathering; Onkelos writes "when you are hired" (23:25's row); the design's DV23-05, Bava Metzia 7 the compile's answer sheet. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. "YOUR FELLOW" — EXCLUDING OTHERS; "YOUR FELLOW" — EXCLUDING THE MOST HIGH'S. (פְּרָט לַאֲחֵרִים "excluding others"; פְּרָט לַגָּבוֹהַּ "excluding the Most High's"): the owner's class — an Israelite, not the sanctuary. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. "AND EAT" — AND NOT SUCKING OUT. (וְלֹא מְמַצֵּץ "and not sucking out"): eating, not pressing the juice — the Tosefta's row (the English). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. "GRAPES" — AND NOT FIGS; HENCE: WORKING AT FIGS HE EATS NO GRAPES, AT GRAPES NO FIGS — BUT HE HOLDS BACK UNTIL HE REACHES THE FINE ONES AND EATS. (וְלֹא תְּאֵנִים "and not figs"; עַד שֶׁמַּגִּיעַ לִמְקוֹם הַיָּפוֹת וְאוֹכֵל "until he reaches the place of the fine ones and eats"): Bava Metzia 7:4 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the crop he works is the crop he eats. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:5 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. R. ELAZAR HISMA: WHENCE THAT A LABORER NOT EAT MORE THAN HIS WAGE? "AS YOUR SOUL"; THE SAGES: "YOUR FILL" — HE EATS MORE THAN HIS WAGE. (שֶׁלֹּא יֹאכַל פּוֹעֵל יוֹתֵר עַל שְׂכָרוֹ "that a laborer not eat more than his wage"; שֶׁאוֹכֵל פּוֹעֵל יוֹתֵר עַל שְׂכָרוֹ "that a laborer eats more than his wage"): THE MEASURE PARAMETER disputed — the wage a cap or no cap; the Tosefta's row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 266:6 — MATERIAL. On 23:25. "INTO YOUR VESSEL PUT NOT" — WHILE YOU PUT INTO THE OWNER'S VESSELS. (בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאַתָּה נוֹתֵן לְתוֹךְ כֶּלְיוֹ "when you put into his vessels"): 266:1's clause for itself — the time of eating the time of gathering. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 267:1 — MATERIAL. On 23:26. "WHEN YOU COME INTO YOUR FELLOW'S STANDING GRAIN" (23:26) — AT ANY TIME? "SWING NO SICKLE" — WHILE YOU SWING THE SICKLE OVER THE OWNER'S GRAIN. The head row of piska 267 (יָכוֹל לְעוֹלָם "might it be at any time?"; בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁאַתָּה מֵנִיף חֶרְמֵשׁ "when you swing the sickle"): the reaper as the vintager — the same shape on the grain. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 267:2 — MATERIAL. On 23:26. "HIS FELLOW" — EXCLUDING OTHERS; "HIS FELLOW" — EXCLUDING THE MOST HIGH'S. (פְּרָט לַאֲחֵרִים "excluding others"; פְּרָט לַגָּבוֹהַּ "excluding the Most High's"): 266:2 again; the export's lemma reads "his fellow" for the verse's "your fellow" (the printed text's variant, the English's note on "Babylonians"). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 267:3 — MATERIAL. On 23:26. "YOU MAY PLUCK EARS WITH YOUR HAND" — THAT YOU NOT REAP WITH A SICKLE. (שֶׁלֹּא תְהֵא קוֹצֵר בְּמַגָּל "that you not reap with a sickle (magal)"): the hand the instrument — Onkelos's very noun (magla, 23:26's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 267:4 — MATERIAL. On 23:26. "SWING NO SICKLE" — WHILE YOU SWING THE SICKLE OVER THE OWNER'S GRAIN. (עַל קָמַת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת "over the owner's standing grain"): 267:1's clause for itself — the chapter's last row; 268:1 opens on 24:1 (the edge clean, the ink's assert). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 268:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:1. "WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE AND HAS HER" (24:1) — THE WOMAN IS ACQUIRED BY MONEY; THE HEBREW SLAVE-GIRL WOULD ARGUE IT, THE LEVIRATE WIDOW REFUTES IT — "TAKES" TEACHES IT. The head row of piska 268 — the edge clean (267:4 stopped at 23:26) (שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה נִקְנֵית בְּכֶסֶף "the woman is acquired by money"; אָמָה הָעִבְרִיָּה "the Hebrew slave-girl"; יְבָמָה תּוֹכִיחַ "the levirate widow proves it"): THE FIRST OF KIDDUSHIN 1:1'S THREE MODES read out of the divorce verse (the English's "cf.") — the marriage the state the bill dissolves (the design's DV24-01). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 268:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:1. "AND HAS HER" — ACQUIRED BY INTERCOURSE; THE LEVIRATE WIDOW WOULD ARGUE IT, THE SLAVE-GIRL REFUTES IT — "HAS HER" TEACHES IT. (שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה נִקְנֵית בִּבְעִילָה "the woman is acquired by intercourse"; אָמָה הָעִבְרִיָּה תּוֹכִיחַ "the Hebrew slave-girl proves it"): the second mode — the two foils swapped, the same shape mirrored (268:1). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 268:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:1. WHENCE ALSO BY A DOCUMENT? THE ARGUMENT FROM MONEY FAILS (MONEY BUYS CONSECRATED THINGS, A DOCUMENT DOES NOT); THE TEACHING: "HE WRITES HER A BILL … AND SHE GOES OUT AND BECOMES ANOTHER'S" — HER BECOMING COMPARED TO HER GOING OUT: AS THE GOING OUT BY A DOCUMENT, SO THE BECOMING. (מִנַּיִן אַף בִּשְׁטָר "whence also by a document?"; מַקִּישׁ הֲוָיָתָהּ לָזֶה לִיצִיאָתָהּ מִזֶּה "it compares her becoming this one's to her going out from that one"): THE COMPARE (hekesh) of 24:1-2 — the bill of marriage from the bill of divorce; the third mode; Kiddushin 1:1 complete in three rows. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "IF SHE FINDS NO FAVOR IN HIS EYES" — HENCE THE SCHOOL OF SHAMMAI: A MAN DIVORCES HIS WIFE ONLY IF HE FOUND LEWDNESS IN HER, "AN UNSEEMLY THING"; THE SCHOOL OF HILLEL: EVEN IF SHE BURNT HIS DISH, "A THING". The head row of the HEADLESS piska 269 (inside 24:1; fourteen rows) (בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים "the school of Shammai say"; אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן מָצָא בָּהּ עֶרְוָה "unless he found lewdness in her"; וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים "and the school of Hillel say"; אֲפִלּוּ הִקְדִּיחָה תַּבְשִׁילוֹ "even if she burnt his dish"): THE GROUNDS OF DIVORCE — Gittin 9:10 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the PARAMETER Scripture leaves open (the design's DV24-01; 254:3's same phrase read at the camp). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). HILLEL'S SCHOOL TO SHAMMAI'S: IF "THING" IS SAID WHY "UNSEEMLINESS", IF "UNSEEMLINESS" WHY "THING"? WITHOUT "UNSEEMLINESS" — SHE WHO GOES OUT FOR A THING MIGHT REMARRY AND SHE WHO GOES OUT FOR LEWDNESS NOT; WITHOUT "THING" — FOR LEWDNESS SHE GOES OUT, FOR A THING NOT. (אִם נֶאֱמַר דָּבָר לָמָּה נֶאֱמַר עֶרְוַת "if 'thing' is said why is 'unseemliness' said?"; הַיּוֹצְאָה מִפְּנֵי דָבָר "she who goes out for a thing"; מִפְּנֵי עֶרְוָה תֵּצֵא "for lewdness she goes out"): the two words each a clause — the ground and the remarriage; Hillel's reading of the pair. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). R. AKIVA: EVEN IF HE FOUND ANOTHER FAIRER THAN SHE — "IF SHE FINDS NO FAVOR IN HIS EYES". (אֲפִלּוּ מָצָא אַחֶרֶת נָאָה הֵימֶנָּה "even if he found another fairer than she"): the third opinion of Gittin 9:10 — the ground the husband's eyes alone. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "HE WRITES FOR HER" — FOR HER NAME; HENCE: ANY BILL NOT WRITTEN FOR THE WOMAN'S NAME IS INVALID. HOW? HE WAS PASSING IN THE MARKET — ETC., THE MISHNAH. (לִשְׁמָהּ "for her name"; כָּל גֵּט שֶׁנִּכְתַּב שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם אִשָּׁה פָּסוּל "any bill not written for a woman's name is invalid"): THE MISHNAH CITED BY ABBREVIATION IN THE HEBREW ("etc., the Mishnah" — Gittin 3:1, the scribes' copy; RUN 1's 228:8 the precedent) — the bill's WRITING an effect with the name its parameter (the design). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "WRITES" — ONLY IN INK? WITH PIGMENT, RED OCHRE, GUM OR VITRIOL WHENCE? "WRITES" — WITH ANYTHING. (כָּתַב בִּדְיוֹ "wrote in ink"; בְּסַם וּבְסִיקְרָא בְּקוֹמוֹס וּבְקַנְקַנְתּוֹם מִנַּיִן "with pigment, red ochre, gum or vitriol — whence?"): the ink's class unbounded (Gittin 2:3's list, the English's Tosefta). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "A DOCUMENT" — ONLY A SCROLL? ON LEAVES OF REED, NUT, OLIVE, CAROB WHENCE? "HE GIVES" — ANYTHING; WHY "DOCUMENT"? AS A SCROLL LASTS, SO ANYTHING LASTING; R. JUDAH B. BETEIRA: AS A SCROLL IS DETACHED FROM THE GROUND, SO ANYTHING DETACHED. (עֲלֵי קָנִים עֲלֵי אֱגוֹז "on reed leaves, on nut leaves"; שֶׁל קַיָּמָא "of permanence"; תָּלוּשׁ מִן הַקַּרְקַע "detached from the ground"): the surface's class by prototype — two predicates disputed (Gittin 2:4 the English's "cf."). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:7 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "CUTTING OFF" — THAT IT BE A CUTTING OFF: "ON CONDITION YOU NEVER GO TO YOUR FATHER'S HOUSE" — NO CUTTING OFF; "FOR THIRTY DAYS" — A CUTTING OFF. "PERMITTED TO EVERY MAN BUT SO-AND-SO" — R. ELIEZER PERMITS, THE SAGES FORBID; AFTER HIS DEATH FOUR ELDERS ENTERED TO ANSWER HIM. (שֶׁיְּהֵא כְּרִיתוּת "that it be a cutting off"; שֶׁלֹּא תֵלְכִי לְבֵית אָבִיךְ לְעוֹלָם "that you never go to your father's house"; אֵין זֶה כְּרִיתוּת "this is no cutting off"; מֻתֶּרֶת לְכָל אָדָם אֶלָּא לִפְלוֹנִי "permitted to every man but So-and-so"; רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַתִּיר וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים "R. Eliezer permits and the sages forbid"; נִכְנְסוּ אַרְבָּעָה זְקֵנִים "four elders entered"): THE BILL'S DEFINITION — a bond that ends; a lasting condition no divorce, a thirty-day one a divorce (a CLOCK); Gittin 9:1 the answer sheet's row (the English's "="). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:8 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). R. TARFON ANSWERED: SHE MARRIED HIS BROTHER, WHO DIED CHILDLESS — HOW DOES SHE CONTRACT THE LEVIRATE? HE STIPULATED AGAINST WHAT IS WRITTEN IN THE TORAH, AND SUCH A CONDITION IS VOID — SO IT WAS NO CUTTING OFF. (נַעֲנָה רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן "R. Tarfon answered"; הֵיאַךְ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת "how does she contract the levirate?"; וְכָל הַמַּתְנֶה עַל מַה שֶׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה תְּנָאוֹ בָּטֵל "whoever stipulates against what is written in the Torah, his condition is void"): THE RULE OF THE VOID CONDITION named in the spine (Ketubot 9:1, Bava Metzia 7:11 the English's "//") — 25:5's levirate inside chapter 24 as the test case. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:9 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). R. YOSE THE GALILEAN: WHERE DO WE FIND IN THE TORAH ONE PERMITTED TO ONE MAN AND FORBIDDEN TO ANOTHER? PERMITTED TO ONE, PERMITTED TO ALL; FORBIDDEN TO ONE, FORBIDDEN TO ALL — NO CUTTING OFF. (הֵיכָן מָצִינוּ בַּתּוֹרָה "where do we find in the Torah"; שֶׁמֻּתֶּרֶת לְאֶחָד וַאֲסוּרָה לְאַחֵר "that she is permitted to one and forbidden to another"): the status indivisible — a woman's marital state one value for all men. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:10 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). R. ELAZAR B. AZARIAH: "CUTTING OFF" — A THING THAT CUTS BETWEEN HIM AND HER; R. YOSE THE GALILEAN: I SEE R. ELAZAR'S WORDS. (דָּבָר הַכּוֹרֵת בֵּינוֹ לְבֵינָהּ "a thing that cuts between him and her"; רוֹאֶה אֲנִי אֶת דִּבְרֵי "I see the words of"): the definition by the word's root — the bond severed whole; the third elder's answer preferred. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:11 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). R. AKIVA: IN WHICH WAS THE TORAH STRICTER, THE DIVORCEE OR THE WIDOW? THE DIVORCEE IS GRAVER; IF THE LIGHTER WIDOW IS FORBIDDEN TO ONE PERMITTED HER (THE LEVIR'S BOND), THE GRAVER DIVORCEE ALL THE MORE TO ONE FORBIDDEN HER — NO CUTTING OFF. (בַּמֶּה הֶחְמִירָה תּוֹרָה "in which was the Torah stricter?"; חֲמוּרָה גְּרוּשָׁה מֵאַלְמָנָה "the divorcee is graver than the widow"): the fourth elder's a fortiori — the levirate widow the lighter case, the ranking of two statuses. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:12 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). ANOTHER WORD: SHE MARRIED THE ONE (SO-AND-SO), BORE HIM SONS, HE DIED, SHE MARRIED ANOTHER — ARE NOT THE FIRST ONE'S SONS MAMZERIM? NO CUTTING OFF. (לֹא נִמְצְאוּ בָנָיו שֶׁל רִאשׁוֹן מַמְזֵרִים "are not the first one's sons mamzerim?"): 248's status (the mamzer) the consequence of a flawed bill — the divorce cell and the assembly cell joined by the child. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:13 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "IN HER HAND" — ONLY HER HAND? HER ROOF, HER COURTYARD, HER RUIN WHENCE? "HE GIVES" — ANYWHERE; WHY "HAND"? AS HER HAND IS HER DOMAIN, SO ANYTHING THAT IS HER DOMAIN. (לְרַבּוֹת גַּגָּהּ חֲצֵרָהּ וְחֻרְבָתָהּ "to include her roof, her courtyard and her ruin"; שֶׁהִיא רְשׁוּתָהּ "which is her domain"): THE HAND IS THE DOMAIN — the delivery's parameter by prototype (Gittin 8:1 the answer sheet). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 269:14 — MATERIAL. On 24:1 (headless — the grounds of divorce, fourteen rows). "GIVES IT IN HER HAND AND SENDS HER FROM HIS HOUSE" — ONCE HE GIVES IT HE SENDS HER; HENCE: HE WHO THROWS A BILL TO HIS WIFE IN HER HOUSE OR HER COURTYARD — SHE IS DIVORCED, ETC., THE MISHNAH; "TAKE IN THIS BOND OF DEBT", OR SHE FOUND IT BEHIND HIM — ETC., THE MISHNAH. (כֵּיוָן שֶׁנּוֹתְנוֹ בְּיָדָהּ מְשַׁלְּחָהּ מִבֵּיתוֹ "once he gives it in her hand he sends her from his house"; הַזּוֹרֵק גֵּט לְאִשְׁתּוֹ "he who throws a bill to his wife"; הֲרֵי זוֹ מְגֹרֶשֶׁת "she is divorced"; כִּנְסִי שְׁטַר חוֹב זֶה "take in this bond of debt"): THE MISHNAH BY ABBREVIATION TWICE in one row (Gittin 8:1-2 — the English supplies the omitted text in braces): the delivery's cases the answer sheet's; the headless piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. "AND SHE GOES OUT OF HIS HOUSE" (24:2) — THE WOMAN GOES OUT FROM BEFORE THE MAN. The head row of piska 270 (שֶׁהָאִשָּׁה יוֹצְאָה מִלִּפְנֵי הָאִישׁ "the woman goes out from before the man"): the direction of the divorce — his house, her going. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. "AND GOES AND BECOMES ANOTHER MAN'S" — THAT SHE NOT MARRY IN HIS NEIGHBORHOOD. (שֶׁלֹּא תִּנָּשֵׂא עִמּוֹ בִּשְׁכוּנָה "that she not marry in his neighborhood"): a PLACE parameter of the remarriage. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:3 — CONTEXT. On 24:2. "ANOTHER" — THE TORAH ALREADY CALLED HIM "OTHER". (כְּבָר קְרָאַתּוֹ הַתּוֹרָה אַחֵר "the Torah already called him 'other'"): the second husband named by the word — a homily on the noun. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:4 — CONTEXT. On 24:2. "AND THE LATTER MAN HATES HER" (24:3) — SCRIPTURE INFORMS YOU THAT YOU WILL COME TO HATE HER. (הַכָּתוּב מְבַשֶּׂרְךָ "Scripture informs you"; עָתִיד לִשְׂנֹאתָהּ "will come to hate her"): the verse read as a forecast — 24:3 carries no head, its rows inside 270. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:5 — CONTEXT. On 24:2. "OR THE LATTER MAN DIES" — SCRIPTURE INFORMS YOU THAT SHE WILL BURY HIM. (שֶׁעֲתִידָה לְקָבְרוֹ "she will bury him"): the second forecast. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. ONLY A DIVORCEE? THE WIDOW WHENCE? "OR THE LATTER MAN DIES"; IF THE WIDOW IS INCLUDED WHY "DIVORCEE"? THE WIDOW IS PERMITTED TO THE LEVIR, THE DIVORCEE FORBIDDEN. (אַלְמָנָה מִנַּיִן "the widow — whence?"; אַלְמָנָה מֻתֶּרֶת לַיָּבָם גְּרוּשָׁה אֲסוּרָה לַיָּבָם "the widow permitted to the levir, the divorcee forbidden to the levir"): the two ways the second marriage ends, and the levirate's bar on the divorced — chapter 25's law read into 24:3-4 a second time (269:8). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:7 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. MIGHT ONE WHO MISCONDUCTED HERSELF AFTER HER DIVORCE BE FORBIDDEN TO RETURN? "HE WRITES HER A BILL … AND SHE GOES OUT" — SHE WHO GOES OUT BY A BILL IS FORBIDDEN TO RETURN, NOT SHE WHO MISCONDUCTED HERSELF. (שֶׁקִּלְקְלָה עַל בַּעְלָהּ "who misconducted herself against her husband"; הַיּוֹצְאָה בְּגֵט תְּהֵא אֲסוּרָה לַחֲזוֹר לוֹ "she who goes out by a bill is forbidden to return to him"): the bar's trigger the second marriage, not misconduct — the status "forbidden to return" set by 24:2's "becomes another's". Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:8 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. WHENCE THAT ONE WHO GIVES A BILL TO HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW MAY NOT TAKE HER BACK? "HER FIRST HUSBAND MAY NOT" (24:4). WHENCE THAT A WOMAN WHOSE HUSBAND WENT OVER THE SEA, WHO WAS TOLD "YOUR HUSBAND IS DEAD" AND REMARRIED, AND HE CAME BACK — GOES OUT FROM BOTH AND NEEDS A BILL FROM BOTH? "HER FIRST HUSBAND MAY NOT TAKE BACK THE ONE HE SENT". (לְנוֹתֵן גֵּט לִיבִמְתּוֹ "one who gives a bill to his levirate widow"; לִמְדִינַת הַיָּם "to a land over the sea"; שֶׁתֵּצֵא מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה "she goes out from this one and that one"; וּצְרִיכָה גֵּט מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה "and needs a bill from this one and that one"): Yevamot 10:1 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the husband over the sea, the walk's case of a false death report; THE ROW THE DUMPS COUNTED AMONG CHAPTER 25'S (the English's "Dt.25:5-10" for the rite of the shoe (chalitzah, the loosening); the Hebrew cites 24:4). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:9 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. "WHOM HE SENT AWAY" — ONLY FROM MARRIAGE TO MARRIAGE? FROM BETROTHAL TO BETROTHAL, BETROTHAL TO MARRIAGE, MARRIAGE TO BETROTHAL WHENCE? "HER FIRST HUSBAND MAY NOT TAKE THE ONE HE SENT". (מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִים לַנִּשּׂוּאִים "from marriage to marriage"; מִן הָאֵרוּסִים לָאֵרוּסִים "from betrothal to betrothal"): the four transitions — betrothal a state the bar covers. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:10 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. R. YOSE B. KIPPAR IN R. ELAZAR B. AZARIAH'S NAME: FROM BETROTHAL PERMITTED, FROM MARRIAGE FORBIDDEN — "AFTER SHE WAS DEFILED"; THE SAGES: BOTH FORBIDDEN; THEN WHY "AFTER SHE WAS DEFILED"? TO INCLUDE THE SOTAH WHO SECLUDED HERSELF. (מִן הָאֵרוּסִים מֻתֶּרֶת "from betrothal permitted"; מִן הַנִּשּׂוּאִים אֲסוּרָה "from marriage forbidden"; לְרַבּוֹת סוֹטָה שֶׁנִּסְתְּרָה "to include the suspected wife who secluded herself"): the disputed parameter; Numbers 5's sotah (the tape's own daemon) barred to her husband by 24:4's word — a CALL edge to be taught (the compile). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:11 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. SO JEREMIAH 3:1 SAYS: "IF A MAN SENDS AWAY HIS WIFE AND SHE GOES FROM HIM". (״הֵן יְשַׁלַּח אִישׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ "if a man sends away his wife"): THE PROPHETS' RECEIPT OF 24:1-4 — Jeremiah cites the law as known ("shall he return to her again?"), the run citation ahead for the compile's readback. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:12 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. "FOR SHE IS AN ABOMINATION" — R. JUDAH: SHE IS AN ABOMINATION, HER CHILD IS NOT. (הִיא תּוֹעֵבָה וְאֵין הַוָּלָד תּוֹעֵבָה "she is an abomination but the child is not an abomination"): the child of the forbidden return NO MAMZER — the abomination formula (find 5) on the woman alone; 24:4 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 270:13 — MATERIAL. On 24:2. "AND MAKE NOT THE LAND SIN" — TO WARN THE COURT ABOUT IT. (לְהַזְהִיר בֵּית דִּין עַל כָּךְ "to warn the court about it"): the court's arm — the land's sin the court's failure to bar the return (Onkelos: "make not the land liable"). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "WHEN A MAN TAKES A NEW WIFE" (24:5) — ONLY A VIRGIN? A WIDOW AND ONE AWAITING THE LEVIR WHENCE? "REJOICE HIS WIFE" — ANY; WHY "NEW"? NEW TO HIM — EXCLUDING ONE WHO TAKES BACK HIS DIVORCEE, A WIDOW TO A HIGH PRIEST, A DIVORCEE OR RELEASED WIDOW TO A COMMON PRIEST, A MAMZERET OR NETINAH TO AN ISRAELITE, AN ISRAELITE'S DAUGHTER TO A MAMZER OR NATIN. The head row of piska 271 (לְרַבּוֹת אַלְמָנָה וְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יָבָם "to include a widow and one awaiting the levir"; מִי שֶׁחֲדָשָׁה לוֹ "one who is new to him"; פְּרָט לְמַחֲזִיר גְּרוּשָׁתוֹ "excluding one who takes back his divorcee"; וְאַלְמָנָה לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל "a widow to a high priest"): THE FORBIDDEN MARRIAGES EXCLUDED FROM THE YEAR — Leviticus 21's priestly bars and 23:3's mamzer the callees; Sotah 8:4's kin (the design's DV24-02). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "HE SHALL NOT GO OUT IN THE HOST" — BUT SUPPLY WEAPONS, WATER AND FOOD? "NOR SHALL ANY MATTER PASS UPON HIM"; THEN EVEN HE WHO BUILT A HOUSE AND DID NOT DEDICATE IT, PLANTED A VINEYARD, BETROTHED A WOMAN? "UPON HIM" — UPON HIM IT DOES NOT PASS, UPON ALL THESE IT PASSES. (אֲבָל יְסַפֵּק כְּלֵי זַיִן וּמַיִם וּמָזוֹן "but supplies weapons, water and food"; בָּנָה בַיִת וְלֹא חֲנָכוֹ "built a house and did not dedicate it"; עָלָיו אֵינוֹ עוֹבֵר "upon him it does not pass"): 20:5-7'S THREE RETURN HOME BUT SERVE SUPPLY (16b's war cells — Sotah 8:2's parameter), THE NEWLYWED FREE OF ALL — the exemption widened (the design's (b)); the Tosefta's row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "FREE SHALL HE BE FOR HIS HOUSE" — THIS IS HIS HOUSE. (זֶה בֵּיתוֹ "this is his house"): the first of four words each a class. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "SHALL HE BE" — THIS IS HIS VINEYARD. (זֶה כַּרְמוֹ "this is his vineyard"): the second. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "AND REJOICE HIS WIFE" — THIS IS HIS WIFE. (זוֹ אִשְׁתּוֹ "this is his wife"): the third — the year for the house, the vineyard and the wife of 20:5-7 gathered. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 271:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:5. "WHOM HE TOOK" — TO INCLUDE HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW. (לְהָבִיא אֶת יְבִמְתּוֹ "to include his levirate widow"): the fourth — 25:5's widow in the year; the parser's [1] "one year" the CLOCK parameter (Sotah 8:4). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 272:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:6. "HE SHALL NOT TAKE IN PLEDGE THE MILL AND THE UPPER STONE" (24:6) — ONLY THESE? ANYTHING WHENCE? "FOR HE TAKES A LIFE IN PLEDGE"; WHY THESE? AS THEY ARE TWO VESSELS SERVING ONE WORK AND HE IS LIABLE FOR EACH BY ITSELF, SO ANY TWO VESSELS SERVING ONE WORK. The head row of piska 272 (מִנַּיִן לְרַבּוֹת כָּל דָּבָר "whence to include everything?"; שְׁנֵי כֵלִים וּמְשַׁמְּשִׁים מְלָאכָה אַחַת "two vessels serving one work"; וְחַיָּב עַל זֶה בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ "liable for this one by itself"): THE LIVELIHOOD'S TOOLS the class, the two stones the prototype counted twice — Bava Metzia 9:13 the compile's answer sheet (the design's DV24-02); the verse with no kin (find 12). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 272:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:6. "FOR HE TAKES A LIFE IN PLEDGE" — TO TELL WHAT CAUSED IT. (לְהַגִּיד מַה גָּרַם "to tell what caused it"): the reason clause named as a reason — the livelihood the life; Onkelos paraphrases "with them food is made for every soul" (24:6's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "IF A MAN BE FOUND" (24:7) — BY WITNESSES. The head row of piska 273 (בְּעֵדִים "by witnesses"): the court's condition — 19:15's two witnesses the callee. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "A MAN" — EXCLUDING A MINOR. (פְּרָט לְקָטָן "excluding a minor"): the thief's age parameter (255:2's form). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "STEALING A SOUL OF HIS BROTHERS" — AND NOT OF OTHERS. (וְלֹא מֵאֲחֵרִים "and not of others"): the victim an Israelite. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL" — TO INCLUDE ONE WHO STEALS HIS OWN SON AND SELLS HIM, LIABLE: R. YOHANAN B. BEROKA; THE SAGES: EXEMPT. (לְהָבִיא הַגּוֹנֵב אֶת בְּנוֹ וּמוֹכְרוֹ "to include one who steals his own son and sells him"; פָּטוּר "exempt"): the father's case disputed — Exodus 21:16's cell (the design's (e)), the Mekhilta's row the English names. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "AND ENSLAVES HIM" — NOT LIABLE UNTIL HE BRINGS HIM INTO HIS DOMAIN; R. JUDAH: UNTIL HE BRINGS HIM IN AND USES HIM — "ENSLAVES HIM AND SELLS HIM". (עַד שֶׁיַּכְנִיסֶנּוּ לִרְשׁוּתוֹ "until he brings him into his domain"; וְיִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ "and uses him"): Sanhedrin 11:1's row (the English's "//") — the domain and the use two conditions, the second disputed. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "AND THAT THIEF SHALL DIE" — AN UNSPECIFIED DEATH IN THE TORAH IS STRANGLING. (בִּסְתַם מִיתָה הָאֲמוּרָה בַתּוֹרָה בְּחֶנֶק "an unspecified death in the Torah is strangling"): THE DEATH'S MODE by the default rule (155:1's, the English) — the sanction's parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:7 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "THAT" — AND NOT ONE WHO STEALS A SLAVE. (וְלֹא הַגּוֹנֵב אֶת הָעֶבֶד "not one who steals a slave"): the victim's class narrowed. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:8 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "THAT" — AND NOT ONE WHO STEALS ONE HALF SLAVE AND HALF FREE. (שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ עֶבֶד וְחֶצְיוֹ בֶּן חוֹרִים "half slave and half free"): the mixed status excluded (Sanhedrin 11:1 the English's "cf."). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 273:9 — MATERIAL. On 24:7. "AND YOU SHALL PURGE THE EVIL FROM YOUR MIDST" — PURGE THE DOERS OF EVIL FROM ISRAEL. (בַּעֵר עוֹשֵׂי הָרָעוֹת מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל "purge the doers of evil from Israel"): THE PURGE FORMULA'S NINTH AND LAST SEAT (find 6; 13:6 the first) — the effect the same at every seat (the English's list of its piskaot); Onkelos "the doer of evil" (24:7's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. "TAKE HEED" (24:8) — A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT. The head row of piska 274 (בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "under a negative commandment"): the chapter's one imperative (find 14) read as a prohibition — the book's "take heed" (4:9). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. "IN THE PLAGUE" — THIS IS THE WHITE HAIR. (זֶה שֵׂעָר לָבָן "this is white hair"): Leviticus 13:3's sign the first — the daemon's parameters cited by name (the design's DV24-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. "OF LEPROSY" — THIS IS THE QUICK FLESH; ONLY BEFORE HE IS BOUND TO UNCLEANNESS? ONCE BOUND, AND AFTER THE RELEASE WHENCE? "TO KEEP DILIGENTLY AND TO DO". (זוֹ מִחְיָה "this is quick flesh"; מִשֶּׁנִּזְקַק לְטֻמְאָה "once bound to uncleanness"; וְאַחַר הַפְּטוּר מִנַּיִן "and after the release — whence?"): the second sign (Leviticus 13:10) and the bar on cutting a sign through the plague's three states — before, during, after. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. ONLY PLAGUES OF MEN? OF GARMENTS AND OF HOUSES WHENCE? "AS THE LEVITICAL PRIESTS TEACH YOU". (נִגְעֵי בְגָדִים וְנִגְעֵי בָתִּים מִנַּיִן "plagues of garments and of houses — whence?"): Leviticus 13:47 and 14:34's two other subjects — the daemon's three ranges. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. ONLY FROM THE CONFIRMED? FROM THE QUARANTINE WHENCE? "AS I COMMANDED THEM". (מִתּוֹךְ הֶחְלֵט "from the confirmed"; מִתּוֹךְ הֶסְגֵּר מִנַּיִן "from the quarantine — whence?"): the two states of the leper (confirmed, shut up) — Onkelos names the plague by the quarantine (24:8's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 274:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:8. ONLY ALL OF THEM? EVEN SOME OF THE PRIESTS WHENCE? "TO KEEP"; "AND TO DO" — YOU GO ON DOING AND NEED NOT FEAR LEST HIS LEPROSY WENT AWAY. (מִנַּיִן אַף מִקְצָתָם "whence even some of them?"; וְאִי אַתָּה חוֹשֵׁשׁ "and you need not fear"; שֶׁמָּא הָלְכָה לָהּ צָרַעְתּוֹ "lest his leprosy went away"): a minority of priests decides; the sign that falls off in the course of work no offense (the Tosefta's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 275:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:9. "REMEMBER WHAT THE LORD YOUR GOD DID TO MIRIAM" (24:9) — WHAT HAS THIS TO DO WITH THAT? PLAGUES COME ONLY FOR SLANDER; AN A FORTIORI: MIRIAM SPOKE OUT OF MOSES' HEARING, FOR HIS GOOD, IN THE OMNIPRESENT'S PRAISE, FOR THE WORLD'S BUILDING — AND WAS PUNISHED; ONE WHO SPEAKS HIS FELLOW'S SHAME IN PUBLIC ALL THE MORE. The head row of piska 275 (מָה עִנְיָן זֶה לָזֶה "what has this to do with that?"; שֶׁאֵין נְגָעִים בָּאִים אֶלָּא עַל לְשׁוֹן הָרָע "plagues come only for slander"; קַל וָחֹמֶר "an a fortiori argument"): THE ADJACENCY READ AS A CAUSE — Numbers 12 the tape's own line (the RUN CITATION behind, the design's readback); the second "remember" (find 2). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 275:2 — CONTEXT. On 24:9. "ON THE WAY" — IN THE HOUR OF YOUR CONFUSION. (בִּשְׁעַת טֵרוּפְכֶם "in the hour of your confusion"): 250:2's gloss repeated for the phrase's second seat. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 275:3 — CONTEXT. On 24:9. "WHEN YOU CAME OUT OF EGYPT" — IN THE HOUR OF YOUR REDEMPTION; SCRIPTURE HUNG IT ON MIRIAM TO TEACH THAT WHEN THE BANNERS JOURNEYED THEY WENT NOT UNTIL MIRIAM WENT BEFORE THEM — MICAH 6:4 "I SENT BEFORE YOU MOSES, AARON AND MIRIAM". (בִּשְׁעַת גְּאֻלַּתְכֶם "in the hour of your redemption"; שֶׁהָיוּ הַדְּגָלִים נוֹסְעִים "when the banners journeyed"; עַד שֶׁמִּרְיָם מַקְדֶּמֶת לִפְנֵיהֶם "until Miriam went before them"): the seven days the camp waited (Numbers 12:15, on the tape) read as Miriam's precedence; Micah the receipt (250:1's twin verse). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 276:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:10. "WHEN YOU LEND YOUR FELLOW" (24:10) — ONLY A LOAN? A HIRELING'S WAGE AND SHOP CREDIT WHENCE? "A LOAN OF ANYTHING". The head row of piska 276 (אֵין לִי אֶלָּא מִלְוָה "only a loan"; לְרַבּוֹת שְׂכַר שָׂכִיר וְהַקָּפַת חֲנוּת "to include a hireling's wage and shop credit"): the debt's class widened — the pledge cell (Exodus 22:24-26, the design's (a)) with every debt as its trigger. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 276:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:10. "COME NOT INTO HIS HOUSE" — NOT FROM INSIDE BUT FROM OUTSIDE? "OUTSIDE YOU SHALL STAND" (24:11); NOT FROM OUTSIDE BUT FROM INSIDE? "OUTSIDE YOU SHALL STAND". (לֹא יְמַשְׁכְּנֶנּוּ מִבִּפְנִים "he shall not take his pledge from inside"; אֲבָל יְמַשְׁכְּנֶנּוּ מִבַּחוּץ "but takes it from outside"): the creditor takes nothing himself — Bava Metzia 9:13 (the English's "cf."). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 276:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:10. WHEN IT SAYS "AND THE MAN" — TO INCLUDE THE COURT'S AGENT. (לְרַבּוֹת שְׁלִיחַ בֵּית דִּין "to include the court's agent"): the one who may enter — the court's arm in the loan; 24:11 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 277:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:12. "AND IF HE IS A POOR MAN" (24:12) — ONLY THE POOR? THE RICH WHENCE? "IF A MAN"; WHY "POOR"? I HASTEN TO EXACT FOR THE POOR MORE THAN FOR THE RICH. The head row of piska 277 (עָשִׁיר מִנַּיִן "the rich — whence?"; מְמַהֵר אֲנִי לִפָּרַע עַל יְדֵי עָנִי "I hasten to exact for the poor"): the rule for all, the poor named for the sanction's speed — the trope of 278:2 and 279:4. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 277:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:12. "LIE NOT DOWN IN HIS PLEDGE" — WOULD YOU THINK HE LIES IN IT? RATHER, LIE NOT DOWN WITH HIS PLEDGE BY YOU. (שֶׁלֹּא תִשְׁכַּב וַעֲבוֹטוֹ אֶצְלְךָ "that you not lie down with his pledge by you"): the night the bound — the pledge back before sleep. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 277:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:12. "SURELY RETURN HIM THE PLEDGE" (24:13) — A DAY VESSEL BY DAY AND A NIGHT VESSEL BY NIGHT: A BLANKET AT NIGHT AND A PLOW BY DAY, NOT THE REVERSE. (כְּלִי יוֹם בַּיּוֹם וּכְלִי לַיְלָה בַּלַּיְלָה "a day vessel by day and a night vessel by night"; סָגוֹס בַּלַּיְלָה וּמַחְרֵשָׁה בַּיּוֹם "a blanket by night and a plow by day"): THE CLOCK OF THE PLEDGE — two returns a day by the vessel's use (Bava Metzia 9:13's row); the sunset of 24:13 the night vessel's hour; 24:13 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 277:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:12. "AND HE LIES IN HIS GARMENT AND BLESSES YOU" — HE IS COMMANDED TO BLESS YOU; IF HE BLESSES YOU ARE BLESSED, IF NOT NOT? "AND IT SHALL BE RIGHTEOUSNESS TO YOU" — OF YOURSELF YOU DO RIGHTEOUSNESS. (שֶׁהוּא מְצֻוֶּה לְבָרֶכְךָ "that he is commanded to bless you"; מֵעַצְמְךָ אַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה צְדָקָה "of yourself you do righteousness"): the debtor's blessing a duty, the creditor's merit independent of it — Onkelos "merit" (zekhuta, 24:13's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 277:5 — CONTEXT. On 24:12. "AND IT SHALL BE RIGHTEOUSNESS TO YOU" — RIGHTEOUSNESS ASCENDS BEFORE THE THRONE OF GLORY; PSALM 85:14 "RIGHTEOUSNESS GOES BEFORE HIM". (שֶׁהַצְּדָקָה עוֹלָה לִפְנֵי כִסֵּא הַכָּבוֹד "righteousness ascends before the throne of glory"): the Writings' verse for the heaven entry — a homily on the reward. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 278:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:14. "OPPRESS NOT A HIRELING POOR AND NEEDY" (24:14) — IS NOT "ROB NOT" (LEVITICUS 19:13) ALREADY SAID? WHOEVER WITHHOLDS A HIRELING'S WAGE TRANSGRESSES FOUR PROHIBITIONS: OPPRESS NOT, ROB NOT, LET NOT THE WAGE STAY OVERNIGHT, AND "ON HIS DAY GIVE HIS WAGE, LET NOT THE SUN SET ON IT". The head row of piska 278 (שֶׁכָּל הַכּוֹבֵשׁ שְׂכַר שָׂכִיר "whoever withholds a hireling's wage"; עוֹבֵר מִשּׁוּם אַרְבָּעָה לָאוִים "transgresses four prohibitions"): THE FOUR SEATS OF ONE WRONG — Leviticus 19:13's three and this verse's one, the frozen Leviticus cells the callees (the design's (a)); "poor and needy" once in the Torah (find 16); the Tosefta's row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 278:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:14. SINCE "TO IT HE LIFTS HIS SOUL" (24:15) — ONLY WORK DONE AT THE RISK OF LIFE? THE WEAVER AND THE CARDER WHENCE? "OPPRESS NOT" — ANY; ONLY THE POOR AND NEEDY? EVERY MAN WHENCE? "OPPRESS NOT" — ANY; WHY "POOR AND NEEDY"? I HASTEN TO EXACT FOR THEM MORE THAN FOR ANY MAN. (גַּרְדִּי וְסוֹרֵק מִנַּיִן "a weaver and a carder — whence?"; מִנַּיִן לְרַבּוֹת כָּל אָדָם "whence to include every man?"; מְמַהֵר אֲנִי לִיפָּרַע "I hasten to exact"): the work's class and the worker's class both unbounded — 277:1's trope. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 278:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:14. "OF YOUR BROTHERS" — AND NOT OF OTHERS. (וְלֹא מֵאֲחֵרִים "and not of others"): the hireling an Israelite. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 278:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:14. "OR OF YOUR SOJOURNER" — THE RIGHTEOUS PROSELYTE: FOR HIM ONE TRANSGRESSES TWO PROHIBITIONS; R. YOSE B. R. JUDAH: ONLY "OPPRESS NOT". (זֶה גֵּר צֶדֶק "this is the righteous proselyte"; שֶׁעוֹבֵר עָלָיו בִּשְׁנֵי לָאוִים "transgresses two prohibitions for him"; מִשּׁוּם בַּל תַּעֲשֹׁק "on account of 'do not oppress'"): the proselyte's count disputed — Onkelos "your proselyte" (24:14's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 278:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:14. "IN YOUR GATES" — THE RESIDENT SOJOURNER; ONLY A MAN'S WAGE? THE HIRE OF A BEAST, OF VESSELS WHENCE? "IN YOUR LAND" — ALL THAT IS IN YOUR LAND. (זֶה גֵּר תּוֹשָׁב "this is the resident sojourner"; שְׂכַר בְּהֵמָה שְׂכַר כֵּלִים מִנַּיִן "the hire of a beast, the hire of vessels — whence?"): the two sojourners of one verse (14:21's lesson); the rent of beasts and tools under the wage's clock (Bava Metzia 9:12 the English's "cf."). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 279:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:15. "ON HIS DAY GIVE HIS WAGE" (24:15) — THE NIGHT'S WAGE IS COLLECTED ALL THE DAY, THE DAY'S WAGE ALL THE NIGHT — WHENCE? LEVITICUS 19:13 "LET NOT THE HIRELING'S WAGE STAY WITH YOU UNTIL MORNING". The head row of piska 279 (שֶׁשְּׂכַר לַיְלָה גּוֹבֶה כָּל הַיּוֹם "the night's wage is collected all the day"; וּשְׂכַר יוֹם גּוֹבֶה כָּל הַלַּיְלָה "the day's wage all the night"): THE WAGE'S CLOCK — two windows from two verses (Bava Metzia 9:11 the answer sheet, the English's "cf."); the design's DV24-04. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 279:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:15. "LET NOT THE SUN SET ON IT, FOR HE IS POOR" — EXCLUDING ONE WHO AGREED OTHERWISE WITH HIM. (פְּרָט לְשֶׁפָּסַק מֵעִמּוֹ "excluding one who agreed otherwise with him"): the sunset waived by agreement — the clock's parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 279:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:15. "AND TO IT HE LIFTS HIS SOUL" — WHY DID HE CLIMB THE RAMP AND HAND YOU HIS LIFE, IF NOT FOR HIS WAGE THAT DAY? WHOEVER WITHHOLDS A HIRELING'S WAGE, SCRIPTURE COUNTS IT AS IF HE TOOK HIS LIFE. (עָלָה זֶה בַּכֶּבֶשׁ "this one climbed the ramp"; כְּאִלּוּ הוּא נוֹטֵל אֶת נַפְשׁוֹ "as if he took his life"): the wage the life — the effect's gravity. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 279:4 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_15_reeh_2026-09-22.md). On 24:15. "THAT HE CRY NOT AGAINST YOU TO THE LORD" — A COMMANDMENT NOT TO CRY? 15:9 "AND HE CRIES AGAINST YOU"; A COMMANDMENT TO CRY? "THAT HE CRY NOT"; SIN ONLY IF HE CRIES? "AND IT SHALL BE SIN IN YOU" — EITHER WAY; WHY "AND HE CRIES"? I HASTEN TO EXACT FOR ONE WHO CRIES MORE THAN ONE WHO DOES NOT. READ BEFORE at chapter 15's ledger (the needy's cry), REREAD WHOLE here (יָכוֹל מִצְוָה שֶׁלֹּא לִקְרוֹת "might it be a commandment not to cry?"; יָכוֹל מִצְוָה לִקְרוֹת "might it be a commandment to cry?"; עַל יְדֵי קוֹרֵא יוֹתֵר מִמִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ קוֹרֵא "for one who cries more than one who does not cry"): THE TWO CRIES (15:9 and 24:15 — find 7's run of six) neither commanded nor forbidden; the sin in you regardless — the same row that closed the release's ledger. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 280:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:16. "FATHERS SHALL NOT BE PUT TO DEATH FOR SONS" (24:16) — WHAT DOES IT TEACH, THAT FATHERS DIE NOT FOR SONS' DEEDS? "EACH FOR HIS OWN SIN" SAYS IT — RATHER, FATHERS NOT BY THE TESTIMONY OF SONS NOR SONS BY THE TESTIMONY OF FATHERS. The head row of piska 280 (שֶׁלֹּא יוּמְתוּ אָבוֹת בְּעֵדוּת בָּנִים "fathers not put to death by the testimony of sons"; וְלֹא בָנִים בְּעֵדוּת אָבוֹת "nor sons by the testimony of fathers"): THE VERSE READ AS A LAW OF WITNESSES, not of guilt — Onkelos writes the same ("by the mouth of sons", 24:16's row); 2 Kings 14:6 quotes the verse in its plain sense (find 1 — the pointer OWED, the design's (f)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 280:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:16. WHEN IT SAYS "AND SONS" — TO INCLUDE THE RELATIVES: HIS BROTHER, HIS FATHER'S BROTHER, HIS MOTHER'S BROTHER, HIS SISTER'S HUSBAND, HIS FATHER'S SISTER'S HUSBAND, HIS MOTHER'S SISTER'S HUSBAND, HIS MOTHER'S HUSBAND, HIS FATHER-IN-LAW AND HIS BROTHER-IN-LAW. (לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַקְּרוֹבִים "to include the relatives"; אָחִיו וַאֲחִי אָבִיו וַאֲחִי אִמּוֹ "his brother, his father's brother, his mother's brother"; וְחָמִיו וְגִיסוֹ "his father-in-law and his brother-in-law"): Sanhedrin 3:4 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — THE KINSHIP PARAMETER of the witness (the design's DV24-05); 19:15's witnesses the callee. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 280:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:16. "EACH FOR HIS OWN SIN SHALL DIE" — FATHERS DIE FOR THEIR OWN INIQUITY AND SONS FOR THEIR OWN. (אָבוֹת מֵתִים בַּעֲוֹן עַצְמָם "fathers die for their own iniquity"): the verse's third clause in its plain sense — Ezekiel 18's kin (find 1). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 281:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_16_reeh_shoftim_2026-09-23.md). On 24:17. "PERVERT NOT THE JUDGMENT OF THE SOJOURNER" (24:17) — WHAT DO I NEED IT FOR, WHEN 16:19 SAYS "PERVERT NOT JUDGMENT, REGARD NOT PERSONS"? WHOEVER PERVERTS A SOJOURNER'S JUDGMENT TRANSGRESSES TWO PROHIBITIONS; IF A SOJOURNER-ORPHAN, THREE. The head row of piska 281 — READ BEFORE at chapter 16's ledger (the courts' bribe and perversion), REREAD WHOLE here (מָה אֲנִי צָרִיךְ "what do I need it for?"; שֶׁכָּל הַמַּטֶּה דִּינוֹ שֶׁל גֵּר "whoever perverts a sojourner's judgment"; עוֹבֵר בִּשְׁנֵי לָאוִים "transgresses two prohibitions"; עוֹבֵר בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה לָאוִים "transgresses three prohibitions"): 16:19'S CELL REUSED with a count of prohibitions by the victim's class (the design's (f)) — the sojourner, the orphan, the sojourner-orphan. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 281:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:17. "AND TAKE NOT A WIDOW'S GARMENT IN PLEDGE" — POOR OR RICH, EVEN MARTHA DAUGHTER OF BOETHUS. (בֵּין עֲנִיָּה בֵּין עֲשִׁירָה "poor or rich"; כְּמָרְתָא בַּת בַּיְתוֹס "as Martha daughter of Boethus"): the widow's status alone the bar, her wealth no parameter — Jerusalem's richest widow the case. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 281:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:17. R. SHIMON: WHAT YOU TAKE IN PLEDGE FROM A MAN YOU DO NOT RETURN TO A WOMAN, LEST YOU GO IN AND OUT AT HER HOUSE AND GIVE HER A BAD NAME. (אֵין אַתָּה מַחֲזִיר לָאִשָּׁה "you do not return to a woman"; לְהַשִּׂיאָהּ שֵׁם רָע "to give her a bad name"): the reason of the bar — the daily return (277:3) would bring the creditor to her door; the chapter's one row citing no verse in either file (the ink). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 282:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:19. "WHEN YOU REAP YOUR HARVEST IN YOUR FIELD" (24:19) — EXCLUDING WHAT ROBBERS REAPED, ANTS GNAWED, THE WIND OR A BEAST BROKE. The head row of piska 282 (פְּרָט לְשֶׁקְּצָרוּהוּ לִיסְטִים "excluding what robbers reaped"; וְקִרְסְמוּהוּ נְמָלִים "and ants gnawed"; וּשְׁבָרַתּוּ הָרוּחַ אוֹ בְהֵמָה "and the wind or a beast broke"): the harvest must be the owner's act (Peah 2:7 the English's "cf."); THE ENGLISH ROW 282:1 CARRIES 282:2'S TEXT TOO (the gentile who converted, R. Judah's sheaf) — the export's misplacement, RUN 1's 227:11 the precedent. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 282:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:19. "YOUR HARVEST" — EXCLUDING WHAT GENTILES REAPED; HENCE: A GENTILE WHO REAPED HIS FIELD AND THEN CONVERTED IS EXEMPT FROM GLEANINGS, THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF AND THE CORNER; R. JUDAH OBLIGATES THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF, FOR FORGETTING IS ONLY AT THE SHEAVING. "YOUR HARVEST" — EXCLUDING OTHERS; EXCLUDING THE CONSECRATED: THE SANCTUARY REAPED AND AN ISRAELITE BOUGHT — EXEMPT; A GENTILE REAPED AND AN ISRAELITE BOUGHT — EXEMPT. (נָכְרִי שֶׁקָּצַר אֶת שָׂדֵהוּ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִתְגַּיֵּר "a gentile who reaped his field and then converted"; פָּטוּר מִן הַלֶּקֶט וּמִן הַשִּׁכְחָה וּמִן הַפֵּאָה "exempt from gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner"; שֶׁאֵין שִׁכְחָה אֶלָּא בִּשְׁעַת הָעִמּוּר "forgetting is only at the sheaving"; פְּרָט לְהֶקְדֵּשׁ "excluding the consecrated"): Peah 4:6 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — THE CLOCK OF THE DUTY: gleanings and corner at the reaping, the forgotten sheaf at the binding; the design's DV24-05 (Leviticus 19:9's cells with the sheaf NEW). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 282:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:19. R. YOSE THE GALILEAN: FROM "WHEN YOU REAP YOUR HARVEST … AND FORGET A SHEAF" — WHENEVER HE HAS A HARVEST HE HAS A SHEAF AND A FORGETTING; NO HARVEST, NO SHEAF, NO FORGETTING: THE SANCTUARY REAPED AND AN ISRAELITE BOUGHT — EXEMPT; A GENTILE REAPED — EXEMPT. (כָּל זְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ קָצִיר "whenever he has a harvest"; יֵשׁ לוֹ עֹמֶר וְיֵשׁ לוֹ שִׁכְחָה "he has a sheaf and he has a forgetting"; קָצַר גּוֹי וְלָקַח יִשְׂרָאֵל פְּטוּרָה "a gentile reaped and an Israelite bought — exempt"): the same two verdicts by a second route — the sheaf conditioned on the harvest's owner. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 282:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:19. "IN YOUR FIELD" — EXCLUDING ONE WHO SHEAVES IN HIS FELLOW'S FIELD: R. MEIR; THE SAGES OBLIGATE; HENCE: A SHEAF THE LABORERS FORGOT AND THE OWNER NOT, OR THE OWNER FORGOT AND THE LABORERS NOT, OR THE POOR STOOD BEFORE IT, OR THEY COVERED IT WITH STRAW — NOT FORGOTTEN. (פְּרָט לִמְעַמֵּר בִּשְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ "excluding one who sheaves in his fellow's field"; שֶׁשְּׁכָחוּהוּ פּוֹעֲלִים וְלֹא שְׁכָחוֹ בַּעַל הַבַּיִת "which the laborers forgot and the owner did not"; עָמְדוּ הָעֲנִיִּים בְּפָנָיו "the poor stood before it"; אוֹ שֶׁחִפּוּהוּ בְקַשׁ "or covered it with straw"; אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה "is not a forgotten sheaf"): Peah 5:7 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — FORGETTING NEEDS BOTH MINDS, the owner's and the laborers'; the poor's own act no forgetting. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). "AND FORGET A SHEAF" — AND NOT THE STACK; EVEN NOT TWO? "FOR THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW LET IT BE"; HENCE: TWO SHEAVES ARE FORGOTTEN, THREE ARE NOT; TWO HEAPS OF OLIVES OR CAROBS, THREE NOT; TWO BERRIES ARE FALLEN GRAPES, THREE NOT. The head row of the HEADLESS piska 283 (inside 24:19; six rows) (וְלֹא הַגָּדִישׁ "and not the stack"; שְׁנֵי עֳמָרִים שִׁכְחָה שְׁלֹשָׁה אֵינָם שִׁכְחָה "two sheaves are forgotten, three are not"; שְׁנֵי גַּרְגְּרִים פֶּרֶט "two berries are fallen grapes"): THE COUNT PARAMETER — two forgotten, three a stack (Peah 6:5 the English's "//"); one rule for the sheaf, the olive, the carob and the grape. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). "IN THE FIELD" — EXCLUDING THE BURIED: R. JUDAH; THE SAGES: "IN THE FIELD" INCLUDES THE BURIED. "IN THE FIELD" — TO INCLUDE STANDING GRAIN: THE A FORTIORI FROM THE SHEAF (WHERE THE POOR MAN'S POWER IS WEAK) ANSWERED — THE SHEAF SAVES NEITHER SHEAF NOR STANDING GRAIN, THE STANDING GRAIN SAVES BOTH — SO THE VERSE TEACHES IT. (פְּרָט לַטְּמוּנִים "excluding the buried"; לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַטְּמוּנִים "to include the buried"; לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַקָּמָה "to include the standing grain"; שֶׁהוּרַע כֹּחַ עָנִי בּוֹ "in which the poor man's power is weak"): Peah 6:10 and 6:8 — the forgotten standing grain a parameter the argument cannot reach and the word supplies. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). "RETURN NOT TO TAKE IT" — EXCLUDING THE HEADS OF ROWS; HENCE: THE HEADS OF ROWS — THE SHEAF OPPOSITE PROVES IT; THE SHEAF HE TOOK HOLD OF TO CARRY TO THE CITY AND FORGOT — THEY AGREE IT IS NOT FORGOTTEN. (פְּרָט לְרָאשֵׁי שׁוּרוֹת "excluding the heads of rows"; שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק בּוֹ לְהוֹלִיכוֹ לָעִיר וּשְׁכָחוֹ "which he took hold of to carry to the city and forgot"; מוֹדִים שֶׁאֵין שִׁכְחָה "they agree it is not forgotten"): Peah 6:2-3 — the return's bar on what was never forgotten in the field's sense; the two schools agreeing. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). "RETURN NOT TO TAKE IT" — ALL OF IT AT ONCE; HOW MUCH? THE SAGES ESTIMATED TWO SEAHS' YIELD; HENCE: A SHEAF OF TWO SEAHS FORGOTTEN IS NOT FORGOTTEN; TWO SHEAVES OF TWO SEAHS TOGETHER — RABBAN GAMLIEL: THE OWNER'S; THE SAGES: THE POOR'S — ETC., THE MISHNAH. (כֻּלּוֹ כְּאֶחָד "all of it at once"; שִׁעֲרוּ חֲכָמִים בְּעוֹשֶׂה סָאתַיִם "the sages estimated at two seahs' yield"; רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר לְבַעַל הַבַּיִת "Rabban Gamliel says: the owner's"; וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים לָעֲנִיִּים "and the sages say: the poor's"): THE MEASURE PARAMETER two seahs and THE MISHNAH BY ABBREVIATION (Peah 6:6 — the English supplies the dialogue in braces); the sheaf too big to forget. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). "RETURN NOT TO TAKE IT" — R. ISHMAEL: AN EAR IN THE HARVEST WHOSE HEAD REACHES THE STANDING GRAIN — IF IT COULD BE REAPED WITH IT, THE OWNER'S; IF NOT, THE POOR'S. AN OWNER WHO CLAIMS FROM THE POOR MUST BRING PROOF, FOR HE WHO CLAIMS FROM HIS FELLOW BEARS THE PROOF. DOUBTFUL GLEANING IS GLEANING, DOUBTFUL SHEAF A SHEAF, DOUBTFUL CORNER A CORNER — "FOR THE SOJOURNER … LET IT BE". (שִׁבֹּלֶת שֶׁבַּקָּצִיר וְרֹאשָׁהּ מַגִּיעַ לַקָּמָה "an ear in the harvest whose head reaches the standing grain"; שֶׁהַמּוֹצִיא מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עָלָיו הָרְאָיָה "he who claims from his fellow bears the proof"; שֶׁסְּפֵק לֶקֶט לֶקֶט "doubtful gleaning is gleaning"): Peah 5:2 and Eduyot 2:4 (the English's "=") — THE BURDEN OF PROOF named as a general rule in the spine, and THE DOUBT RESOLVED FOR THE POOR: two court parameters from one verse. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 283:6 — CONTEXT. On 24:19 (headless — the forgotten sheaf). R. ELAZAR B. AZARIAH: ONE WHO LOSES A SELA FROM HIS HAND, AND A POOR MAN FINDS IT AND LIVES ON IT — SCRIPTURE COUNTS HIM AS IF HE MERITED; AN A FORTIORI FOR ONE WHO MEANS IT. (לִמְאַבֵּד סֶלַע מִתּוֹךְ יָדוֹ "one who loses a sela from his hand"; וּמְצָאָהּ עָנִי "and a poor man found it"; כְּאִלּוּ זָכָה "as if he merited"; קַל וָחֹמֶר "an a fortiori argument"): 252:2's argument on the unmeant good — the forgotten sheaf its Torah seat; the headless piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "WHEN YOU BEAT YOUR OLIVE TREE" (24:20) — THE EARLY ONES BEAT THEIR OLIVES AND DEALT WITH A GENEROUS EYE; HENCE: AN OLIVE TREE STANDING ON THREE ROWS OF TWO BEDS, FORGOTTEN — NOT FORGOTTEN. The head row of piska 284 (הָרִאשׁוֹנִים הָיוּ חוֹבְטִים זֵיתֵיהֶם "the early ones beat their olives"; עַיִן יָפָה "a generous eye"; עַל שָׁלֹשׁ שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל שְׁנֵי מַלְבְּנִים "on three rows of two beds"; אֵינוֹ שִׁכְחָה "is not forgotten"): Peah 7:2 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=", its continuation supplied in braces — the Netofah olive) — the tree too marked to forget, the sheaf's rule on the orchard. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "YOUR OLIVES" — EXCLUDING OTHERS'; EXCLUDING THE CONSECRATED. (פְּרָט לַאֲחֵרִים "excluding others"; פְּרָט לְהֶקְדֵּשׁ "excluding the consecrated"): 282:2's two exclusions on the olive. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "GO NOT OVER THE BOUGHS" — GLORY NOT OVER THE POOR WITH IT; HENCE: HE WHO LETS NOT THE POOR GLEAN, OR LETS ONE AND NOT ANOTHER, OR HELPS ONE OF THEM — ROBS THE POOR; PROVERBS 22:28 "REMOVE NOT THE ANCIENT BOUNDARY". (לֹא תִּתְפָּאֵר לֶעָנִי בּוֹ "glory not over the poor with it"; שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַנִּיחַ אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים לְלַקֵּט "who lets not the poor glean"; הֲרֵי זֶה גּוֹזֵל אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים "he robs the poor"): Peah 5:6 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the rare verb read by its other sense (glory), the owner's interference a robbery; the Writings' verse the seal. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "AFTER YOU" — IT HAS A FORGETTING. (שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שִׁכְחָה "it has a forgetting"): the olive under the sheaf's law. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:5 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "AFTER YOU" — IT HAS A CORNER. (שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ פֵּאָה "it has a corner"): the olive under the corner's law (Leviticus 19:9). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 284:6 — MATERIAL. On 24:20. "FOR THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW" — "SOJOURNER AND ORPHAN" SAID HERE AND SAID THERE (24:19): AS THERE AT TWO SEAHS' YIELD, SO HERE AT TWO SEAHS' YIELD. (נֶאֱמַר כָּאן גֵּר וְיָתוֹם וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן גֵּר וְיָתוֹם "'sojourner and orphan' is said here and said there"; בְּעוֹשֶׂה סָאתַיִם "at two seahs' yield"): THE VERBAL ANALOGY CARRYING THE MEASURE from the sheaf to the olive — 283:4's parameter reused by the shared words. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 285:1 — MATERIAL. On 24:21. "WHEN YOU GATHER YOUR VINEYARD" (24:21) — R. ELIEZER: A VINEYARD ALL SMALL CLUSTERS IS THE OWNER'S; R. AKIVA: THE POOR'S. The head row of piska 285 (כֶּרֶם שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ עוֹלֵלוֹת "a vineyard all small clusters"; לְבַעַל הַבַּיִת "the owner's"; לָעֲנִיִּים "the poor's"): Peah 7:7 — the edge case of a whole vineyard of the poor's share, disputed. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 285:2 — MATERIAL. On 24:21. "GLEAN NOT THE SMALL CLUSTERS" — WHAT IS A SMALL CLUSTER? ONE WITH NEITHER SHOULDER NOR PENDANT; A SHOULDER WITHOUT PENDANT OR A PENDANT WITHOUT SHOULDER — THE OWNER'S; NEITHER — THE POOR'S. (אֵיזוֹ הִיא עוֹלֶלֶת "what is a small cluster?"; לֹא כָתֵף וְלֹא נָטֵף "neither shoulder nor pendant"): Peah 7:4 — the cluster's two shapes the definition; the rare verb (olel) kept by Onkelos (24:21's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 285:3 — MATERIAL. On 24:21. "AFTER YOU" — IT HAS A FORGETTING; "AFTER YOU" — IT HAS A CORNER; HENCE: WHAT IS FORGETTING ON A TRELLIS? ANY CLUSTER HE CANNOT STRETCH HIS HAND AND TAKE; IN TRAILING VINES — ONCE HE PASSES IT. (אֵיזוֹ הִיא שִׁכְחָה בֶּעָרִיס "what is forgetting on a trellis?"; לִפְשֹׁט אֶת יָדוֹ וְלִטְּלָהּ "to stretch his hand and take it"; בָּרַגְלִיּוֹת מִשֶּׁיַּעֲבֹר הֵימֶנָּה "in trailing vines, once he passes it"): Peah 7:8 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — forgetting defined by reach and by passing, two parameters by the vine's training. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 285:4 — MATERIAL. On 24:21. "SOJOURNER, ORPHAN" — SAID HERE AND SAID THERE (24:19): AS THERE AT TWO SEAHS' YIELD, SO HERE. (נֶאֱמַר כָּאן גֵּר וְיָתוֹם "'sojourner and orphan' is said here"; בְּעוֹשֶׂה סָאתַיִם "at two seahs' yield"): 284:6's analogy for the vineyard — the chapter's last row; 286:1 opens on 25:1 (the edge clean, the ink's assert). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_29_separation_promise_2026-08-25.md). On 25:1. "WHEN THERE IS A QUARREL BETWEEN MEN" (25:1) — NO PEACE COMES OUT OF STRIFE; GENESIS 13:7 "THERE WAS A QUARREL BETWEEN THE HERDSMEN OF ABRAM'S CATTLE AND OF LOT'S" — WHAT MADE LOT PART FROM THE RIGHTEOUS ONE? STRIFE; WHAT MADE THIS ONE BE FLOGGED? STRIFE. The head row of piska 286 — the edge clean (285:4 stopped at 24:21) — READ BEFORE at Genesis 29's ledger (the separation of Abram and Lot), REREAD WHOLE here (אֵין שָׁלוֹם יוֹצֵא מִתּוֹךְ מְרִיבָה "no peace comes out of strife"; מִי גָרַם לְלוֹט לִפָּרֵשׁ מִן הַצַּדִּיק "what caused Lot to part from the righteous one?"; מִי גָרַם לָזֶה לִלְקוֹת "what caused this one to be flogged?"): the tape's own Genesis 13 read back as the cause of every court case — the quarrel the trigger of the lashes' cell (the design's DV25-01). Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "BETWEEN MEN" — ONLY MEN? A MAN WITH A WOMAN, A WOMAN WITH A MAN, TWO WOMEN — WHENCE? "THEY COME TO JUDGMENT" — ANYONE. (אִישׁ עִם אִשָּׁה וְאִשָּׁה עִם אִישׁ "a man with a woman and a woman with a man"; וּשְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים זוֹ עִם זוֹ "and two women with each other"): the parties' class every person — 249:1's sex parameter reversed. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "AND THEY JUDGE THEM" — AGAINST THEIR WILL. (בְּעַל כָּרְחָם "against their will"): the court's jurisdiction not consensual. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "AND JUSTIFY THE RIGHTEOUS" — ARE ALL THE GUILTY FLOGGED? "IF THE WICKED DESERVES FLOGGING" (25:2) — SOMETIMES YES, SOMETIMES NO; WHICH? "MUZZLE NOT AN OX" (25:4): AS THE MUZZLE IS A NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT AND FLOGGED, SO EVERY NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT; OR EVERY ONE WITH A "RISE AND DO"? AS THE MUZZLE HAS NO "RISE AND DO" AND IS FLOGGED, SO EVERY NEGATIVE COMMANDMENT WITHOUT A REMEDY. R. SHIMON: JUSTIFY HIM SO HE NOT BE FLOGGED? "IF HE DESERVES FLOGGING" — SOMETIMES. (יָכוֹל כָּל הַמֻּרְשָׁעִים לוֹקִים "might all the guilty be flogged?"; פְּעָמִים לוֹקֶה פְּעָמִים אֵינוֹ לוֹקֶה "sometimes flogged, sometimes not"; מַה חֲסִימַת שׁוֹר מְיֻחֶדֶת "as the muzzling of the ox is distinctive"; שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ קוּם עֲשֵׂה "which has no 'rise and do'"; צַדְּקֵהוּ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא לוֹקֶה "justify him so that he not be flogged"): THE CLASS OF THE FLOGGABLE fixed by the muzzle's prototype — a prohibition with no positive remedy (Makkot 3's principle, the Tosefta the English cites); 25:4's verse read into 25:1-3 by adjacency — the muzzle the lashes' key. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "AND THE JUDGE LAYS HIM DOWN" (25:2) — THEY DO NOT FLOG ONE STANDING. (אֵין מַלְקִים מֵעוֹמֵד "they do not flog one standing"): the posture; 25:2 carries no head — its rows inside 286. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:6 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "AND FLOGS HIM" — A THIRD BEFORE HIM AND TWO PARTS BEHIND. (שְׁלִישׁ לְפָנָיו וּשְׁתֵּי יָדוֹת מֵאַחֲרָיו "a third before him and two parts behind him"): Makkot 3:13's row (the English's "//") — the blows' division a parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:7 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "BEFORE HIM" — FLOGGED WITH THE JUDGE'S EYES ON HIM, NOT ELSEWHERE; FLOGGED AND KILLED? "AS HIS WICKEDNESS DESERVES" — NOT FLOGGED AND KILLED; FLOGGED AND PAYING? — NOT FLOGGED AND PAYING. (וְעֵינָיו בּוֹ "with his eyes on him"; אֵין לוֹקֶה וָמֵת "not flogged and killed"; אֵין לוֹקֶה וּמְשַׁלֵּם "not flogged and paying"): THE ONE-SANCTION RULE — lashes exclude death and exclude payment (Makkot 1:2 and Ketubot 3:1's principle, the answer sheet's) — the compile's parameter on every fine beside a flogging (22:19's hundred, 22:29's fifty). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:8 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "FORTY" (25:3) — A FULL FORTY? "BY NUMBER, FORTY" — A COUNT THAT BORDERS ON FORTY; R. JUDAH: A FULL FORTY — WHERE THE EXTRA ONE? BETWEEN HIS SHOULDERS. (יָכוֹל אַרְבָּעִים שְׁלֵמוֹת "might it be a full forty?"; מִנְיָן שֶׁהוּא סוֹכֵם אֶת הָאַרְבָּעִים "a count that borders on the forty"; הֵיכָן הוּא לוֹקֶה אֶת הַיְּתֵרָה "where does he take the extra one?"; בֵּין כְּתֵפָיו "between his shoulders"): THE PARSER'S [40] READ AS THIRTY-NINE by the two verses' seam ("by number" closing 25:2, "forty" opening 25:3 — the English's note on the order) — Makkot 3:10 the answer sheet's parameter (the design's DV25-01), R. Judah's forty the dissent. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:9 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "HE SHALL STRIKE HIM" — NOT THE GROUND; NOT HIS GARMENT; NOT TWO AS ONE. (וְלֹא עַל הַקַּרְקַע "and not on the ground"; וְלֹא עַל כְּסוּתוֹ "and not on his garment"; אֵין מַלְקִים שְׁנַיִם כְּאֶחָד "they do not flog two as one"): the pronoun's three exclusions — the body, bare, one at a time. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:10 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "HE SHALL NOT ADD" — IF HE ADDED HE TRANSGRESSES A PROHIBITION; ONLY OVER THE COUNT? OVER ANY ESTIMATE THE COURT ESTIMATED — "LEST HE ADD". (אִם הוֹסִיף עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "if he added he transgresses a prohibition"; עַל כָּל אֹמֶד וְאֹמֶד שֶׁאֲמָדוּהוּ בֵּית דִּין "over any estimate the court estimated for him"): THE ESTIMATE (omed) — the count set below forty by the court's judgment of his strength (Makkot 3:11) — a PARAMETER per case; the officer's excess an offense. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:11 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "A GREAT BLOW" — ONLY A GREAT ONE? A LIGHT ONE WHENCE? "OVER THESE"; WHY "GREAT"? THE FIRST IS NOT A GREAT BLOW. (מַכָּה מוּעֶטֶת מִנַּיִן "a light blow — whence?"; שֶׁאֵין הָרִאשׁוֹנָה מַכָּה רַבָּה "the first is not a great blow"): the blows equal, none heavier. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:12 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. "AND YOUR BROTHER IS DEGRADED BEFORE YOUR EYES" — HENCE: IF HE BEFOULED HIMSELF WITH EXCREMENT OR URINE HE IS EXEMPT; R. JUDAH: THE MAN WITH EXCREMENT, THE WOMAN WITH URINE. (נִתְקַלְקֵל בֵּין בִּרְעִי בֵּין בְּמַיִם פָּטוּר "if he befouled himself with excrement or with urine, he is exempt"; הָאִישׁ בִּרְעִי וְהָאִשָּׁה בְּמַיִם "the man with excrement, the woman with urine"): Makkot 3:14 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — the degradation the sentence's end, the rest remitted; 25:3 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:13 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. ANOTHER WORD: "YOUR BROTHER IS DEGRADED" — ONCE FLOGGED HE IS YOUR BROTHER; HENCE: ALL LIABLE TO EXCISION WHO WERE FLOGGED ARE FREED OF THEIR EXCISION. (מִשֶּׁלָּקָה הֲרֵי הוּא אָחִיךָ "once flogged he is your brother"; כָּל חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת שֶׁלָּקוּ נִפְטְרוּ מִידֵי כְּרֵתָם "all liable to excision who were flogged are freed of their excision"): Makkot 3:15 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — THE STATUS EFFECT of the lashes: the wicked becomes the brother, the heavenly sanction lifted — the compile's effect on the person (the design's (f)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:14 — MATERIAL. On 25:1. R. HANANIAH B. GAMLIEL: ALL THE DAY SCRIPTURE CALLS HIM WICKED — "IF THE WICKED DESERVES FLOGGING"; ONCE FLOGGED IT CALLS HIM YOUR BROTHER — "YOUR BROTHER IS DEGRADED". (כָּל הַיּוֹם הַכָּתוּב קוֹרֵא אוֹתוֹ רָשָׁע "all the day Scripture calls him wicked"; אֲבָל מִשֶּׁלָּקָה הַכָּתוּב קוֹרֵא אוֹתוֹ אָחִיךָ "but once flogged Scripture calls him your brother"): the two names of one man in two verses — the status change read from the nouns (Makkot 3:15). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:15 — CONTEXT. On 25:1. R. SHIMON: FROM ITS OWN PLACE IT IS DECIDED — LEVITICUS 18:29 "CUT OFF" AND 18:5 "LIVE BY THEM"; AN A FORTIORI: IF THE LESSER MEASURE OF PUNISHMENT JOINS ONE TO THE TRANSGRESSORS' JUDGMENT, ONE WHO JOINS THE DOERS OF A COMMANDMENT ALL THE MORE. (מִמְּקוֹמוֹ הוּא מֻכְרָע "from its own place it is decided"; קַל וָחֹמֶר "an a fortiori argument"; הַנִּטְפָּל לְעוֹשֵׂי מִצְוָה "he who joins the doers of a commandment"): the homily on the lifted excision — Makkot 3:15's continuation. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:16 — CONTEXT (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_12_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 25:1. R. SHIMON B. RABBI: "ONLY BE STRONG NOT TO EAT THE BLOOD" (12:23) — IF ONE WHO KEEPS FROM BLOOD, WHICH A MAN'S SOUL RECOILS FROM, IS REWARDED, ONE WHO KEEPS FROM ROBBERY AND FORBIDDEN UNIONS, WHICH THE SOUL CRAVES, ALL THE MORE — FOR HIM AND HIS GENERATIONS TO THE END. READ BEFORE at chapter 12's ledger (the blood), REREAD WHOLE here (מָה הַדָּם שֶׁנַּפְשׁוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם חָתָה מִמֶּנּוּ "as the blood, from which a man's soul recoils"; גֵּזֶל וַעֲרָיוֹת "robbery and forbidden unions"; עַד סוֹף כָּל הַדּוֹרוֹת "to the end of all the generations"): Makkot 3:15's a fortiori on abstention — the blood's verse the measure. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 286:17 — CONTEXT. On 25:1. RABBI: PSALM 15:1-5 "WHO SHALL DWELL IN YOUR TENT … HIS SILVER HE GAVE NOT AT INTEREST" AND EZEKIEL 18:5-9 "A MAN WHO IS RIGHTEOUS … HE SHALL SURELY LIVE" — WHAT DID THIS ONE DO? WHOEVER SITS AND DOES NOT TRANSGRESS IS REWARDED AS ONE WHO DOES A COMMANDMENT. (וְכִי מַה עָשָׂה זֶה "and what did this one do?"; כָּל הַיּוֹשֵׁב וְאֵינוֹ עוֹבֵר עֲבֵרָה "whoever sits and does not transgress"; נוֹתְנִים לוֹ שָׂכָר כְּעוֹשֶׂה מִצְוָה "they give him reward as one who does a commandment"): Makkot 3:16's close (the English's "cf.") — Ezekiel 18 the kin of 24:16 (find 1) cited for the righteous man; the interest of 23:20 in the psalm; the piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 287:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:4. "MUZZLE NOT AN OX" (25:4) — ONLY AN OX? OTHER CATTLE, BEASTS AND BIRDS AS THE OX WHENCE? "MUZZLE NOT" — ANY; WHY "OX"? AN OX YOU MAY NOT MUZZLE, A MAN YOU MAY. The head row of piska 287 (שְׁאָר בְּהֵמָה חַיָּה וָעוֹף "other cattle, beasts and birds"; שׁוֹר אִי אַתָּה חוֹסֵם חוֹסֵם אַתָּה אֶת הָאָדָם "an ox you may not muzzle; a man you may muzzle"): the class every working animal (Bava Kamma 5:7 the English's "cf."), the laborer of 23:25 excluded — he may waive by agreement (Bava Metzia 7:6); the design's DV25-02. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 287:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:4. "IN ITS THRESHING" — ONLY THRESHING? OTHER LABORS WHENCE? "MUZZLE NOT" — ANY; WHY "THRESHING"? AS THRESHING IS OF WHAT GROWS FROM THE EARTH, DETACHED FROM THE GROUND, AT THE WORK'S COMPLETION, AND THE WORKER EATS — SO EVERY SUCH LABOR. (לְרַבּוֹת שְׁאָר עֲבוֹדוֹת "to include other labors"; שֶׁגִּדּוּלוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ וְתָלוּשׁ מִן הַקַּרְקַע "which grows from the earth and is detached from the ground"; בִּשְׁעַת גְּמַר מְלָאכָה "at the time of the work's completion"): THE FOUR CONDITIONS of the eating labor — Bava Metzia 7:2's row for the laborer (23:25-26) and the ox alike: one rule for the man's mouth and the beast's. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 287:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:4. R. YOSE B. R. JUDAH: AS THRESHING IS DONE WITH HANDS, FEET AND BODY — EXCLUDING WORK WITH THE HANDS BUT NOT THE FEET, THE FEET BUT NOT THE BODY. (בְּיָדָיו וּבְרַגְלָיו וּבְגוּפוֹ "with his hands, his feet and his body"; יָצָא הָעוֹשֶׂה בְּיָדָיו אֲבָל לֹא בְּרַגְלָיו "excluding one who works with his hands but not with his feet"): a narrower prototype disputed (the Tosefta's row the English names); one of chapter 25's two rows citing no verse (the ink). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "WHEN BROTHERS DWELL TOGETHER" (25:5) — EXCLUDING THE WIFE OF A BROTHER WHO WAS NOT IN HIS WORLD; HENCE: TWO BROTHERS, ONE DIED, A BROTHER WAS BORN TO THEM, THEN THE SECOND TOOK HER — ETC., THE MISHNAH. The head row of piska 288 (פְּרָט לְאֵשֶׁת אָחִיו שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּעוֹלָמוֹ "excluding the wife of a brother who was not in his world"; וְנוֹלַד לָהֶם אָח "and a brother was born to them"; וְאַחַר כָּךְ יִבֵּם הַשֵּׁנִי "and then the second took her in levirate"): THE FIRST CONDITION OF THE LEVIRATE DAEMON — the brothers alive together; THE MISHNAH BY ABBREVIATION (Yevamot 2:1 — the English supplies the case's end in braces), the sitting's fifth; the design's DV25-03. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "TOGETHER" — EXCLUDING HIS BROTHER FROM THE MOTHER; SINCE THE TORAH HAS BROTHERS WHERE THE MOTHER'S BROTHER IS AS THE FATHER'S, HERE TOO? "TOGETHER" — THE MOTHER'S EXCLUDED. (פְּרָט לְאָחִיו מֵאִמּוֹ "excluding his brother from the mother"; אָח מִן הָאֵם כְּאָח מִן הָאָב "a brother from the mother as a brother from the father"): the paternal line the parameter — "together" in one inheritance. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "AND ONE OF THEM DIES" — ONLY TWO BROTHERS? MANY — WHENCE? "ONE OF THEM"; ALL DIED? "ONE OF THEM"; WHY "ONE"? THE WIFE OF ONE IS TAKEN, NOT THE WIFE OF TWO; HENCE: THREE BROTHERS MARRIED TO THREE UNRELATED WOMEN, ONE DIED, THE SECOND MADE HER A DECLARATION AND DIED — THEY LOOSEN THE SHOE AND ARE NOT TAKEN. "AND HE HAS NO SON" — A SON'S SON, A SON'S DAUGHTER, A DAUGHTER'S SON, A DAUGHTER'S DAUGHTER WHENCE? "NO SON" — NONE AT ALL; WHY "SON"? EXCLUDING A CHILD BY THE SLAVE-GIRL OR THE GENTILE WOMAN. (אֵשֶׁת אֶחָד מִתְיַבֶּמֶת וְלֹא אֵשֶׁת שְׁנַיִם "the wife of one is taken in levirate, and not the wife of two"; שְׁלֹשָׁה אַחִים נְשׂוּאִים שָׁלֹשׁ נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֹת "three brothers married to three unrelated women"; חוֹלְצוֹת וְלֹא מִתְיַבְּמוֹת "they loosen the shoe and are not taken"; בֵּן הַבֵּן וּבַת הַבֵּן וּבֶן הַבַּת וּבַת הַבַּת "a son's son, a son's daughter, a daughter's son, a daughter's daughter"; מִן הַשִּׁפְחָה וּמִן הַנָּכְרִית "from the slave-girl or from the gentile woman"): THE PARSER'S [1] "ONE OF THEM" read as ONE BOND — a widow bound to two dead brothers is freed by the shoe (Yevamot 3:9 the answer sheet's row, the English's "="); "no son" means no descendant of any degree, the slave's and the gentile's children not counted — the daemon's second condition with its class. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "THE DEAD MAN'S WIFE SHALL NOT BE OUTSIDE TO A STRANGER" — WHY NEEDED? LEST "THE WIFE OF ONE, NOT OF TWO" BE READ HERE; WHAT DOES SHE DO? SHE LOOSENS THE SHOE OR IS TAKEN. ONE WHO GIVES A BILL TO HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW DISQUALIFIES HER FOR HIMSELF AND FOR THE BROTHERS; DOES THE BILL FREE HER? THE ARGUMENT FROM THE SHOE SAYS YES — THE TEACHING SAYS "NOT OUTSIDE TO A STRANGER" EXCEPT BY THE SHOE-LOOSENING. (אוֹ חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת "either she loosens the shoe or is taken"; הַנּוֹתֵן גֵּט לִיבִמְתּוֹ "he who gives a bill to his levirate widow"; פְּסָלָהּ לְעַצְמוֹ וּפְסָלָהּ עַל יְדֵי אַחִים "disqualified her for himself and for the brothers"; אֶלָּא בַּחֲלִיצָה "only by the shoe-loosening"): THE TWO EXITS ONLY — the marriage or the rite; the divorce bill (24:1) no release from the levirate bond though it disqualifies (270:8's twin); THE ENGLISH ROW 288:4 CARRIES A FIFTH SECTION (the declaration, ma'amar — "penetration concludes the acquisition, the declaration does not") WITH NO HEBREW IN THE EXPORT'S ROW — the third misplacement of the sitting. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "HER LEVIR SHALL COME TO HER" — IN ERROR OR WITH INTENT, BY FORCE OR WILLINGLY, EVEN HE IN ERROR AND SHE WITH INTENT OR THE REVERSE. (בֵּין בְּשׁוֹגֵג בֵּין בְּמֵזִיד בֵּין בְּאֹנֶס בֵּין בְּרָצוֹן "whether in error or with intent, whether by force or willingly"): Yevamot 6:1's row (the English's "cf.") — the act alone acquires, intention no parameter; Onkelos writes the verb plain (25:5's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 288:6 — MATERIAL. On 25:5. "AND TAKE HER … AND MARRY HER" — NOT "TAKE" AND "MARRY" — EXCLUDING THE RIVAL WIFE OF ANY FORBIDDEN KIN; HENCE: FIFTEEN WOMEN FREE THEIR RIVALS AND THEIR RIVALS' RIVALS FROM THE SHOE-LOOSENING AND THE LEVIRATE. (פְּרָט לְצָרַת כָּל הָעֲרָיוֹת "excluding the rival wife of any forbidden kin"; חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה נָשִׁים פּוֹטְרוֹת צָרוֹתֵיהֶן "fifteen women free their rivals"; מִן הַחֲלִיצָה וּמִן הַיִּבּוּם "from the shoe-loosening and from the levirate"): YEVAMOT 1:1 — THE TRACTATE'S OPENING ROW IN THE SPINE (the English's "//"): the pronoun suffixes ("her", "her") the exclusion's hook; the forbidden kin's rival freed — the daemon's exception table (the design). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "AND THE FIRSTBORN SHE BEARS" (25:6) — IF HIS NAME WAS YOSE IS THE CHILD CALLED YOSE, YOHANAN YOHANAN? "SHALL STAND ON HIS BROTHER'S NAME" — ANY NAME; WHY "THE FIRSTBORN"? THE COMMANDMENT IS ON THE ELDEST TO TAKE HER.
