The head row of piska 289 (אִם הָיָה שְׁמוֹ יוֹסֵי יִקָּרֵא שְׁמוֹ יוֹסֵי "if his name was Yose, is he called Yose?"; שֶׁמִּצְוָה בַּגָּדוֹל לְיַבֵּם "the commandment is on the eldest to take her in levirate"): THE FIRSTBORN IS THE ELDEST BROTHER, not the child, and the name no literal name — Yevamot 2:8 and 4:5 the answer sheet's rows (the English's "="); Genesis 38's Judah read back (Er, Onan, Shelah in order). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "SHE BEARS" — EXCLUDING THE BARREN WOMAN (AYLONIT), WHO IS NOT FIT TO BEAR. (פְּרָט לְאַיְלוֹנִית "excluding the barren woman (aylonit)"; שֶׁאֵינָהּ רְאוּיָה לֵילֵד "who is not fit to bear"): the one fertility exclusion (289:7 keeps the rest). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "SHALL STAND ON HIS BROTHER'S NAME" — AND NOT ON HIS FATHER'S BROTHER'S. (וְלֹא עַל שֵׁם אֲחִי אָבִיו "and not on his father's brother's name"): the bond one generation deep. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "THE DEAD" — SINCE "THE WIFE OF ONE, NOT OF TWO", WHENCE THAT IF THE FIRST DIED THE SECOND TAKES HER, THE SECOND DIED THE THIRD? "THE DEAD", "THE DEAD" — IT INCLUDES. (מֵת רִאשׁוֹן יְיַבֵּם שֵׁנִי "the first died, the second takes her"; מֵת שֵׁנִי יְיַבֵּם שְׁלִישִׁי "the second died, the third takes her"; הַמֵּת הַמֵּת רִבָּה "'the dead', 'the dead' — it includes"): THE SUCCESSION by the word said in both verses (25:5-6) — Judah's three sons the tape's own case (Genesis 38:8-11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "THAT HIS NAME BE NOT BLOTTED OUT OF ISRAEL" — EXCLUDING THE EUNUCH, WHOSE NAME IS ALREADY BLOTTED. (פְּרָט לְסָרִיס "excluding a eunuch"; שֶׁכְּבָר שְׁמוֹ מָחוּי "whose name is already blotted"): Yevamot 8:4 (the English's "cf.") — "blot out" (machah) the verb of Amalek's memory at 25:19 and Exodus 17:14: the same root for the childless name and the enemy's record. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:6 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "IF THE MAN DOES NOT DESIRE" (25:7) — AND NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT DOES NOT DESIRE HER: THE KIN LIABLE TO DEATH BY THE COURT EXCLUDED; THOSE LIABLE TO EXCISION? "MY LEVIR REFUSES" — NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT REFUSES; THOSE UNDER A PROHIBITION? "HE WILL NOT CONSENT" — NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT DOES NOT CONSENT. (וְלֹא שֶׁלֹּא חָפֵץ בָּהּ הַמָּקוֹם "and not when the Omnipresent does not desire her"; מִיתַת בֵּית דִּין "death by the court"; כָּרֵת בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם "excision by the hand of Heaven"; שֶׁהֵן בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה "which are under a prohibition"): THE THREE SANCTION CLASSES (248:2-4's ladder) each excluded from the levirate by one of the verse's three refusal clauses — the forbidden widow exempt, the exception table's ground (288:6); 25:7 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:7 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "TO TAKE HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW" — WHY NEEDED? SINCE "THE FIRSTBORN SHE BEARS", MIGHT I EXCLUDE THE BARREN, THE OLD AND THE MINOR, UNFIT TO BEAR? "HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW", "HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW" — IT INCLUDES. (עֲקָרָה וּזְקֵנָה וּקְטַנָּה "a barren woman, an old woman and a minor"; יְבִמְתּוֹ יְבִמְתּוֹ רִבָּה "'his levirate widow', 'his levirate widow' — it includes"): the doubled noun includes what 289:2 seemed to exclude — only the aylonit out, the old and the minor in; the fertility parameter narrowed to one class. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:8 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "AND HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW GOES UP TO THE GATE" — THE COMMANDMENT IS WITH THE COURT, THE HIGHEST IN THE CITY, WITH ELDERS. (מִצְוָה בְּבֵית דִּין "the commandment is with the court"; בַּגָּבוֹהַּ שֶׁבָּעִיר "at the highest in the city"; בִּזְקֵנִים "with elders"): THE COURT'S ARM in the rite — "goes up" the height; Onkelos "the gate of the court" (25:7's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:9 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "MY LEVIR REFUSES" — NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT REFUSES; "TO RAISE UP FOR HIS BROTHER" — EXCLUDING THE EUNUCH, WHO IF HE WISHED COULD NOT; "A NAME" — R. JUDAH: "NAME" SAID HERE AND THERE (25:6) — AS THERE IT IS INHERITANCE, SO HERE; AS HERE IT IS SEED, SO THERE. (שֶׁאִם רָצָה לְהָקִים אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל "who, if he wished to raise up, could not"; נֶאֱמַר כָּאן שֵׁם וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן שֵׁם "'name' is said here and said there"; נַחֲלָה "inheritance"; זֶרַע "seed"): THE NAME IS THE INHERITANCE AND THE SEED — a verbal analogy in both directions (264:1's form): THE LEVIR'S FIRSTBORN INHERITS THE DEAD BROTHER'S ESTATE — the daemon's effect on the ledger (the design's (b)). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:10 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "IN ISRAEL" — AND NOT AMONG PROSELYTES; HENCE: TWO PROSELYTE BROTHERS CONCEIVED OUTSIDE HOLINESS AND BORN IN HOLINESS ARE EXEMPT FROM THE SHOE-LOOSENING AND THE LEVIRATE. (וְלֹא בְּגֵרִים "and not among proselytes"; שֶׁהָיְתָה הוֹרָתָם שֶׁלֹּא בִּקְדֻשָּׁה וְלֵידָתָם בִּקְדֻשָּׁה "whose conception was not in holiness and whose birth was in holiness"; פְּטוּרִים מִן הַחֲלִיצָה וּמִן הַיִּבּוּם "exempt from the shoe-loosening and from the levirate"): the brotherhood dated from the conversion — a status by the mother's state at conception (Yevamot 11:2's case; the English's "cf. 8:2" the cite to check at the compile). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 289:11 — MATERIAL. On 25:6. "HE WILL NOT CONSENT TO TAKE ME" — AND NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT DOES NOT CONSENT TO HER. (וְלֹא שֶׁלֹּא אָבָה בָהּ הַמָּקוֹם "and not when the Omnipresent did not consent to her"): the third refusal clause for itself (289:6) — the piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 290:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:8. "AND THE ELDERS OF HIS CITY CALL HIM" (25:8) — THE COMMANDMENT IS WITH THE ELDERS OF HIS CITY. The head row of piska 290 (מִצְוָה בְּזִקְנֵי עִירוֹ "the commandment is with the elders of his city"): the summons the court's — his city's, not hers. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 290:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:8. "AND SPEAK TO HIM" — OF WHAT IS FITTING FOR HIM: IF HE IS A BOY AND SHE OLD, OR HE OLD AND SHE A GIRL, THEY SAY "GO TO ONE LIKE YOURSELF; WHY BRING STRIFE INTO YOUR HOUSE?" (בְּהוֹגֶנֶת לוֹ "about what is fitting for him"; הוּא יֶלֶד וְהִיא זְקֵנָה "he a boy and she old"; כַּלֵּךְ אֵצֶל כְּמוֹתְךָ "go to one like yourself"; לְהַכְנִיס קְטָטָה לְבֵיתְךָ "to bring strife into your house"): THE ELDERS' COUNSEL against an unfit match — Yevamot 12:6's row (the English's "//"); the court may advise the refusal. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 290:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:8. "AND HE STANDS AND SAYS" — HE SAYS HIS WORDS ONLY STANDING. (אֶלָּא בַּעֲמִידָה "only standing"): the posture of the declaration (the Tosefta's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 290:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:8. "I DO NOT DESIRE TO TAKE HER" — AND NOT WHEN THE OMNIPRESENT DOES NOT DESIRE HER. (וְלֹא שֶׁלֹּא חָפֵץ בָּהּ הַמָּקוֹם "and not when the Omnipresent does not desire her"): the refusal formula's fourth seat (289:6, 289:9, 289:11) — his words, not Heaven's bar. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "AND HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW COMES NEAR HIM BEFORE THE ELDERS' EYES AND SPITS BEFORE HIM" (25:9) — SPITTLE VISIBLE TO THE ELDERS; "BEFORE THE EYES" — SHE IS TOGETHER WITH HIM BEFORE THEM. The head row of piska 291 (רֹק הַנִּרְאֶה לְעֵינֵי הַזְּקֵנִים "spittle visible to the elders' eyes"; שֶׁמִּתְיַחֶדֶת עִמּוֹ "that she is together with him"): the rite's witnesses — the court sees the spittle and the pair. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "AND DRAWS OFF HIS SHOE" — ONLY HIS OWN? OTHERS' WHENCE? "THE HOUSE OF THE LOOSED SHOE" (25:10) — ANY; WHY "HIS SHOE"? EXCLUDING ONE TOO BIG TO WALK IN, TOO SMALL TO COVER MOST OF THE FOOT, OR A SANDAL WITHOUT A HEEL — HER LOOSENING INVALID. (שֶׁל אֲחֵרִים מִנַּיִן "of others — whence?"; פְּרָט לְגָדוֹל שֶׁאֵין יָכוֹל לְהַלֵּךְ בּוֹ "excluding one too big to walk in"; וְסוֹלְיָיס שֶׁאֵין לוֹ עָקֵב "and a sandal without a heel"; שֶׁחֲלִיצָתָהּ פְּסוּלָה "her loosening is invalid"): THE SHOE'S PARAMETERS — Yevamot 12:1-2 (the English's "cf.") — the shoe an EFFECT with a fitness test (the design's DV25-03). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "FROM HIS FOOT" — "FOOT" SAID HERE AND THERE (LEVITICUS 14:14): AS THERE THE RIGHT, SO HERE THE RIGHT; "FROM HIS FOOT" — FROM THE KNEE DOWN VALID, FROM THE KNEE UP INVALID. (יְמָנִית "the right"; מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַטָּה חֲלִיצָתָהּ כְּשֵׁרָה "from the knee down her loosening is valid"; מִן הָאַרְכֻּבָּה וּלְמַעְלָה חֲלִיצָתָהּ פְּסוּלָה "from the knee up her loosening is invalid"): THE LEPER'S RIGHT FOOT (Leviticus 14:14 — 24:8's daemon) lends the rite its side by a verbal analogy; Yevamot 12:1's knee (the English's "//"). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "AND SPITS BEFORE HIM" — LITERALLY IN HIS FACE? "BEFORE THE ELDERS' EYES" — VISIBLE TO THEM; HENCE: LOOSENED AND SPAT AND DID NOT RECITE — VALID; RECITED AND SPAT AND DID NOT LOOSEN — INVALID; LOOSENED AND RECITED AND DID NOT SPIT — R. ELIEZER INVALID, R. AKIVA VALID. (יָכוֹל בְּפָנָיו מַמָּשׁ "might it be literally in his face?"; חָלְצָה וְרָקְקָה וְלֹא קָרְאתָה "she loosened and spat and did not recite"; קָרְאתָה וְרָקְקָה וְלֹא חָלְצָה "she recited and spat and did not loosen"; חָלְצָה וְקָרְאתָה וְלֹא רָקְקָה "she loosened and recited and did not spit"): Yevamot 12:3 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=") — THE RITE'S THREE ACTS AND THEIR NECESSITY: the shoe essential, the recital not, the spittle disputed — the compile's effects table (the shoe and the spittle EFFECTS, the design). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "AND ANSWERS AND SAYS" — "ANSWERING" SAID HERE AND THERE (27:15): AS THERE IN THE HOLY TONGUE, SO HERE IN THE HOLY TONGUE. (נֶאֶמְרָה כָּאן עֲנִיָּה וְנֶאֶמְרָה לְהַלָּן עֲנִיָּה "'answering' is said here and said there"; בִּלְשׁוֹן הַקֹּדֶשׁ "in the holy tongue"): THE RECITAL IN HEBREW by analogy to the Levites' answer of 27:15 — A VERSE AHEAD (the pointer for 27:15 owed with 24:16's and 25:19's); Sotah 7:2's list of recitals (the English's "cf."). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:6 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "THUS SHALL BE DONE TO THE MAN WHO WILL NOT BUILD HIS BROTHER'S HOUSE" — R. ELIEZER: WHO DID NOT BUILD AND WILL NOT BUILD. (אֲשֶׁר לֹא בָנָה וַאֲשֶׁר לֹא עָתִיד לִבְנוֹת "who did not build and who will not build"): after the shoe no levirate ever — the refusal final (the status the rite sets). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:7 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. R. ELIEZER: "THUS SHALL BE DONE" — A THING WHOSE ACT IS ESSENTIAL; R. AKIVA: FROM THERE A PROOF? "THUS SHALL BE DONE TO THE MAN" — WHAT IS DONE TO THE MAN; R. SHIMON: THE LOOSENING ESSENTIAL AND THE SPITTING ESSENTIAL; "HIS NAME SHALL BE CALLED IN ISRAEL" (25:10) — R. ISHMAEL: THE LOOSENING FOR THE LYING, THE SPITTING FOR THE SEED. (בְּדָבָר שֶׁמַּעֲשֶׂה מְעַכֵּב "a thing whose act is essential"; מִשָּׁם רְאָיָה "from there a proof?"; חֲלִיצָה מְעַכֶּבֶת וּרְקִיקָה מְעַכֶּבֶת "the loosening is essential and the spitting is essential"; חֲלִיצָה בִּשְׁכִיבָה וּרְקִיקָה כְּדֵי שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע "the loosening for the lying, the spitting for the seed"): 291:4's dispute argued from the verse's "thus"; R. Ishmael's reading of the two acts as signs of the refused marriage; 25:10 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:8 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "HIS NAME SHALL BE CALLED IN ISRAEL" — "IN ISRAEL" SAID HERE AND THERE (25:7): AS THERE IT EXCLUDES A COURT OF PROSELYTES, SO HERE. (פְּרָט לְבֵית דִּין שֶׁל גֵּרִים "excluding a court of proselytes"): the analogy inside the chapter — 289:10's "not among proselytes" carried to the judges: the court's composition a parameter. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 291:9 — MATERIAL. On 25:9. "THE HOUSE OF THE LOOSED SHOE" — THE COMMANDMENT IS ON THE JUDGES, NOT ON THE DISCIPLES; R. JUDAH: ON ALL WHO STAND THERE TO SAY "LOOSED OF THE SHOE, LOOSED OF THE SHOE"; R. JUDAH: ONCE WE SAT BEFORE R. TARFON AND HE SAID "ANSWER, ALL OF YOU: LOOSED OF THE SHOE!" (מִצְוָה בַּדַּיָּנִים וְלֹא מִצְוָה בַּתַּלְמִידִים "the commandment is on the judges and not on the disciples"; חֲלוּץ הַנָּעַל חֲלוּץ הַנָּעַל "loosed of the shoe, loosed of the shoe"; הָיִינוּ יוֹשְׁבִים לִפְנֵי רַבִּי טַרְפוֹן "we were sitting before R. Tarfon"): THE SHOUT the rite's close — the name given aloud (Yevamot 12:6's row; the English's "M. Yev. 12:10" a cite past the tractate's twelfth chapter's count — to check at the compile); the piska's and the levirate's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 292:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in gen_29_separation_promise_2026-08-25.md). On 25:11. "WHEN MEN STRUGGLE TOGETHER" (25:11) — NO PEACE COMES OUT OF STRUGGLE; GENESIS 13:7 AGAIN — WHAT MADE LOT PART FROM THAT RIGHTEOUS ONE? STRIFE; "WHEN THERE IS A QUARREL" (25:1) — WHAT MADE THIS ONE BE FLOGGED? STRIFE. The head row of piska 292 — READ BEFORE at Genesis 29's ledger, REREAD WHOLE here (אֵין שָׁלוֹם יוֹצֵא מִתּוֹךְ נִצּוּת "no peace comes out of struggle"; מִי גָרַם לְלוֹט שֶׁיִּפְרוֹשׁ מֵאוֹתוֹ צַדִּיק "what caused Lot to part from that righteous one?"; הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר זוֹ מְרִיבָה "say: it is strife"): 286:1's homily repeated for the chapter's second quarrel verse — Abram and Lot the prototype of both. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 292:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:11. "MEN" — ONLY MEN? A MAN WITH A WOMAN, A WOMAN WITH A MAN — WHENCE? "TOGETHER" — ANYONE. (אִישׁ עִם אִשָּׁה אִשָּׁה עִם אִישׁ מִנַּיִן "a man with a woman, a woman with a man — whence?"; יַחְדָּו מִכָּל מָקוֹם "'together' — anyone"): 286:2's widening for the wrestlers. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 292:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:11. "A MAN AND HIS BROTHER" — EXCLUDING SLAVES, WHO HAVE NO BROTHERHOOD. (פְּרָט לַעֲבָדִים שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם אַחְוָה "excluding slaves, who have no brotherhood"): the parties free men — the slave outside the shame payment (the Tosefta's row the English names); one of the chapter's rows citing no verse in the Hebrew. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 292:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:11. "AND THE WIFE OF THE ONE DRAWS NEAR" — NOT THE WIFE OF THE COURT'S AGENT; "TO SAVE HER HUSBAND" — RABBI: SINCE THE TORAH HAS DAMAGES WHERE THE UNINTENDING IS AS THE INTENDING, HERE TOO? "AND SEIZES HIS SECRETS" — NOT LIABLE FOR THE SHAME UNTIL HE INTENDS. (וְלֹא אֵשֶׁת שְׁלִיחַ בֵּית דִּין "and not the wife of the court's agent"; שֶׁאֵין מִתְכַּוֵּין כְּמִתְכַּוֵּין "the unintending as the intending"; שֶׁאֵינוֹ חַיָּב עַל הַבֹּשֶׁת עַד שֶׁיִּתְכַּוֵּין "not liable for the shame until he intends"): THE INTENT PARAMETER of the shame payment (Bava Kamma 8:3's row, the English's "//"; the design's DV25-04 with Bava Kamma 8:1) — the parser's [1] "the one" (starred) read as whose wife; the court's agent beaten with no claim. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 292:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:11. "HIS SECRETS" — ONLY THESE? ANYTHING WITH DANGER IN IT WHENCE? "AND SEIZES" — ANY; WHY "HIS SECRETS"? AS THEY HOLD DANGER TO LIFE AND STAND UNDER "CUT OFF HER HAND", SO ANYTHING WITH DANGER TO LIFE. (לְרַבּוֹת דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ סַכָּנָה "to include anything with danger in it"; שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ סַכָּנַת נְפָשׁוֹת "with danger to life in it"): the class by prototype — the life-threatening grip, 293:1's rescue its consequence. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 293:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:12. "AND YOU SHALL CUT OFF HER HAND" (25:12) — YOU ARE BOUND TO SAVE HIM BY HER HAND; IF YOU CANNOT SAVE HIM BY HER HAND, SAVE HIM BY HER LIFE — "YOUR EYE SHALL NOT PITY". The head row of piska 293 (שֶׁאַתָּה חַיָּב לְהַצִּילָהּ בְּכַפָּהּ "you are bound to save by her hand"; הַצִּילָהּ בְּנַפְשָׁהּ "save by her life"): THE PURSUER'S LAW read into the verse (243:2's kin at the betrothed girl) — the rescuer stops the attacker by a limb, else by her life; the first of the verse's two readings. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 293:2 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei_2026-09-24.md). On 25:12. R. JUDAH: "YOUR EYE SHALL NOT PITY" SAID HERE AND THERE (19:21): AS THERE IT IS MONEY, SO HERE IT IS MONEY. READ BEFORE at chapters 19-21's ledger (the plotting witnesses' "eye for eye"), REREAD WHOLE here (הָאָמוּר לְהַלָּן מָמוֹן "said there — money"; הָאָמוּר כָּאן מָמוֹן "said here — money"): THE PITY FORMULA'S OPERATOR — "cut off her hand" IS THE HAND'S VALUE IN MONEY by the verbal analogy to 19:21 (16b's operator; the design's DV25-04); the verse's second reading — the compile takes the payment, 293:1 the rescue. Reread.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. "YOU SHALL NOT HAVE IN YOUR BAG A STONE AND A STONE" (25:13) — NO LITRA, HALF LITRA AND QUARTER LITRA? "GREAT AND SMALL" — A GREAT ONE THAT BELIES THE SMALL, TAKING WITH THE GREAT AND RETURNING WITH THE SMALL. The head row of piska 294 (לִיטְרָא וַחֲצִי לִיטְרָא וּרְבִיעַ לִיטְרָא "a litra, a half litra and a quarter litra"; גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁהִיא מַכְחֶשֶׁת אֶת הַקְּטַנָּה "a great one that belies the small"; נוֹטֵל בַּגְּדוֹלָה וּמַחֲזִיר בַּקְּטַנָּה "takes with the great and returns with the small"): the bar not on a set of weights but on two of one name — "a stone and a stone" Proverbs 20:10's phrase (find 16); the design's DV25-04 with Bava Batra 5:10-11. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. R. AKIVA: WHENCE THAT ONE MAKES NO SELA LESS THAN A SHEKEL, NO DINAR LESS THAN A HALF-DINAR? "YOU SHALL NOT HAVE". (סֶלַע פָּחוּת מִשֶּׁקֶל "a sela less than a shekel"; וְלֹא דִּינָר פָּחוּת מִטַּרְפָּעִיקָא "nor a dinar less than a tarpe'ik (a half-dinar)"): THE COIN'S WEAR — a worn coin a false weight; the tolerable loss the Tosefta's parameter (the English's Bava Metzia rows). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. R. YOSE B. R. JUDAH: IF YOU KEPT IT, IT IS UNDER "YOU SHALL NOT HAVE". (אִם קִיַּמְתָּ "if you kept it"; הֲרֵי הוּא בְּלֹא יִהְיֶה לְךָ "it is under 'you shall not have'"): possession the offense without use — the verb "have" the whole law; the second row of the chapter citing nothing (the ink). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. "YOU SHALL NOT HAVE IN YOUR HOUSE AN EPHAH AND AN EPHAH" (25:14) — NO TARKAV, HALF AND QUARTER? "GREAT AND SMALL" — THE GREAT THAT BELIES THE SMALL. (תַּרְקָב וַחֲצִי תַּרְקָב וּרְבִיעַ תַּרְקָב "a tarkav (two kav), a half and a quarter"; גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁהִיא מַכְחֶשֶׁת אֶת הַקְּטַנָּה "a great one that belies the small"): 294:1's form for the dry measure; 25:14 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:5 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. R. ELIEZER: WHENCE THAT A MAN MAKES NO FOUR-KAV MEASURE TO MEASURE WITH IN HIS HOUSE? "YOU SHALL NOT HAVE". (מִדָּה בַּת אַרְבַּעַת קַבִּים "a measure of four kav"; מוֹדֵד בָּהּ בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ "measuring with it in his house"): a non-standard size barred even at home. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:6 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. WHENCE THAT ONE WEIGHS NOT EXACTLY WHERE THEY TIP THE SCALE, NOR TIPS WHERE THEY WEIGH EXACTLY? "A WHOLE STONE" (25:15); EVEN IF HE SAID "I WEIGH EXACTLY WHERE THEY TIP, TO LOWER THE PRICE"? "AND JUST SHALL YOU HAVE". (שֶׁאֵין מְעַיְּנִים בִּמְקוֹם שֶׁמַּכְרִיעִים "one does not weigh exactly where they tip the scale"; לִפְחוֹת לוֹ מִן הַדָּמִים "to lower the price for him"; וָצֶדֶק יִהְיֶה לָּךְ "and just shall you have"): THE LOCAL CUSTOM the parameter — the market's way binds even with the buyer's consent and to his gain (Bava Batra 5:11's row). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:7 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. WHENCE THAT ONE HEAPS NOT WHERE THEY LEVEL, NOR LEVELS WHERE THEY HEAP? "A WHOLE STONE"; EVEN "I LEVEL WHERE THEY HEAP, TO LOWER THE PRICE" — THEY DO NOT LISTEN TO HIM? "AND JUST SHALL YOU HAVE". (שֶׁלֹּא יִגְדּוֹשׁ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁמּוֹחֲקִים "that one heap not where they level"; וְלֹא יִמְחוֹק בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁגּוֹדְשִׁים "nor level where they heap"; שֶׁאֵין שׁוֹמְעִים לוֹ "they do not listen to him"): 294:6's rule for the dry measure — heaped or level by the place (Bava Batra 5:11). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:8 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. "SHALL YOU HAVE" — APPOINT A MARKET INSPECTOR FOR IT; HENCE: THE WHOLESALER WIPES HIS MEASURES ONCE IN THIRTY DAYS, THE HOUSEHOLDER ONCE IN TWELVE MONTHS — ETC., THE MISHNAH. (מַנֵּה אַגְרוֹנוֹמוֹס עַל כָּךְ "appoint a market inspector (agronomos) for it"; הַסִּיטוֹן מְקַנֵּחַ מִדּוֹתָיו אַחַת לִשְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם "the wholesaler wipes his measures once in thirty days"; אַחַת לִשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר חֹדֶשׁ "once in twelve months"): Bava Batra 5:10 the answer sheet's row (the English's "=", its rest supplied in braces) — THE MISHNAH BY ABBREVIATION (the sitting's sixth), THE GREEK LOANWORD agronomos (the market officer; kruspedin the tassels' Greek name in RUN 1), and two CLOCKS (thirty days, twelve months) — an office the verse's "you shall have" creates. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 294:9 — MATERIAL. On 25:13. ELAZAR B. HANANIAH B. HEZEKIAH B. GARON: EZEKIEL 46:11 "AN EPHAH FOR THE BULL, AN EPHAH FOR THE RAM, AN EPHAH FOR THE LAMB" — IS THEIR MEASURE ONE? NUMBERS 29:3 SAYS THREE TENTHS, TWO, ONE — SO A GREAT EPHAH AND A SMALL EPHAH ARE BOTH CALLED EPHAH. (וְכִי מִדַּת פָּרִים וְאֵילִים וּכְבָשִׂים אַחַת הִיא "is the measure of bulls, rams and lambs one?"; שֶׁאֵיפָה גְּדוֹלָה וְאֵיפָה קְטַנָּה קְרוּיָה אֵיפָה "a great ephah and a small ephah are both called ephah"): the word's two sizes proved from the tape's own Numbers 29:3 against Ezekiel's temple — the noun a class, not a quantity; the piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 295:1 — CONTEXT. On 25:15 (headless — the weights' reward). "THAT YOUR DAYS MAY BE LONG" (25:15) — ONE OF THE TORAH'S COMMANDMENTS WITH ITS REWARD BESIDE IT; AN A FORTIORI: IF A DRIED FIG, ONE HUNDREDTH OF AN ISSAR, EARNS LENGTH OF DAYS, THE COMMANDMENTS THAT COST THE PURSE ALL THE MORE. The head row of the HEADLESS piska 295 (inside 25:15; four rows) (שֶׁמַּתַּן שְׂכָרָהּ בְּצִדָּהּ "whose reward is given beside it"; גְּרוֹגֶרֶת שֶׁאֵינָהּ אֶלָּא אֶחָד מִמֵּאָה בְּאִסָּר "a dried fig, only one hundredth of an issar"; קַל וָחֹמֶר "an a fortiori argument"): the reward formula (find 9's run with 5:16 and 4:40) — the weights and the nest (22:7) the two laws paid beside their verse; a homily on the least weight. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 295:2 — MATERIAL. On 25:15 (headless — the weights' reward). "FOR AN ABOMINATION OF THE LORD YOUR GOD IS EVERYONE WHO DOES THESE" (25:16) — NOT LIABLE UNTIL HE DOES ALL OF THEM? "EVERYONE WHO DOES THESE" — EVEN ONE. (עַד שֶׁיַּעֲבֹר עַל כֻּלָּם "until he transgresses all of them"; אֲפִלּוּ אַחַת מֵהֶם "even one of them"): the abomination formula's seventh Deuteronomy seat (find 5; 22:5's run of seven) — "these" any one of the four; 25:16 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 295:3 — MATERIAL. On 25:15 (headless — the weights' reward). "EVERYONE WHO DOES WRONG" — HENCE: ONE DOES NOT MIX PRODUCE WITH PRODUCE, EVEN NEW WITH OLD, EVEN A SEAH AT A DINAR WITH A SEAH WORTH A DINAR AND A TRESSIS, TO SELL AT A DINAR. (אֵין מְעָרְבִין פֵּרוֹת בְּפֵרוֹת "one does not mix produce with produce"; וַאֲפִלּוּ חֲדָשִׁים בִּישָׁנִים "even new with old"; סְאָה בְּדִינָר "a seah at a dinar"): Bava Metzia 4:11's bar on blending (the English's "//") — even to the seller's loss; the wrong the deception, not the price. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 295:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:15 (headless — the weights' reward). "EVERYONE WHO DOES WRONG" — CALLED BY FIVE NAMES: CONDEMNED, ABOMINATION, DETESTABLE, HATED, PERVERSE. (קָרוּי חֲמִשָּׁה שֵׁמוֹת "called by five names"; חֵרֶם תּוֹעֵבָה מְשֻׁקָּץ שָׂנוּי עַוָּל "condemned, abomination, detestable, hated, perverse"): 226:3's five epithets again (RUN 1, the garments) — the abomination formula's subject named the same at 22:5 and 25:16, the run of seven words the two verses share (find 5); the headless piska's last row. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:1 — MATERIAL. On 25:17. "REMEMBER" (25:17) — WITH THE MOUTH; "FORGET NOT" (25:19) — IN THE HEART; SO EXODUS 15:14 "THE PEOPLES HEARD AND TREMBLED". The head row of piska 296 (זָכוֹר בַּפֶּה "remember — with the mouth"; אַל תִּשְׁכָּח בַּלֵּב "forget not — in the heart"): THE TWO VERBS TWO ORGANS — the reading aloud and the memory (find 10's "you shall not forget", twice in the Torah); the Song's verse the tape's own line before Amalek's attack (Exodus 15:14, 17:8) — the design's DV25-05. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:2 — CONTEXT. On 25:17. "ON THE WAY" — IN THE HOUR OF YOUR CONFUSION. (בִּשְׁעַת טֵרוּפְכֶם "in the hour of your confusion"): the phrase's third seat glossed as at 250:2 and 275:2. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:3 — CONTEXT. On 25:17. "WHEN YOU CAME OUT OF EGYPT" — IN THE HOUR OF YOUR REDEMPTION. (בִּשְׁעַת גְּאֻלַּתְכֶם "in the hour of your redemption"): find 2's three remembrances (23:5, 24:9, 25:17) glossed alike by the spine — one gloss for one phrase. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:4 — MATERIAL. On 25:17. "WHO MET YOU" (25:18) — "MET" MEANS ONLY "HAPPENED UPON YOU". (אֵין קָרְךָ אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּזְדַּמֵּן לְךָ "'met you' means only 'happened upon you'"): the chance encounter — the root of 23:11's "chance of the night" (mikreh), a homograph of the camp's law in the war's enemy; 25:18 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:5 — CONTEXT. On 25:17. "AND CUT OFF THE STRAGGLERS BEHIND YOU" — HE KILLED ONLY THOSE DRAWN FROM THE OMNIPRESENT'S WAYS, WHO FELL OUT FROM UNDER THE CLOUD'S WINGS. (שֶׁנִּמְשְׁכוּ מִדַּרְכֵי מָקוֹם "who were drawn from the ways of the Omnipresent"; וְנֶחְשְׁלוּ מִתַּחַת כַּנְפֵי הֶעָנָן "and fell out from under the cloud's wings"): the Mekhilta's reading of Exodus 17:8 (the English) — the tape's own attack retold; a REFERENCE ROW for the readback (the design). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:6 — CONTEXT. On 25:17. "AND YOU WERE WEARY AND FAINT AND FEARED NOT GOD" — BY THE MEASURE YOU MEASURED THEY MEASURED TO YOU: AS YOU WERE WEARY AND FAINT AND FEARED NOT GOD, SO HE WAS WEARY AND FAINT AND FEARED NOT GOD. (בַּמִּדָּה שֶׁמָּדַדְתָּ בָּהּ מָדְדוּ לְךָ "by the measure you measured they measured to you"; אַף הוּא עָיֵף וְיָגֵעַ "he too was weary and faint"): MEASURE FOR MEASURE (Sotah 1:7's principle) — the verse's subject read both ways, Israel's and Amalek's (Onkelos gives it to Amalek, 25:18's row); "God" (Elohim) the chapter's one seat (find 14). Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:7 — MATERIAL. On 25:17. "WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU REST FROM ALL YOUR ENEMIES AROUND" (25:19) — THAT NO BAND GATHER AGAINST YOU. (שֶׁלֹּא תֵאָגֵד עָלֶיךָ אֲגֻדָּה "that no band gather against you"): THE CONDITION OF THE COMMAND — the rest from every enemy before Amalek's war: a STATE the command waits on (the design's DV25-05 — the king's war of 1 Samuel 15 the receipt ahead, the pointer OWED); 25:19 carries no head. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:8 — MATERIAL. On 25:17. "IN THE LAND THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU FOR AN INHERITANCE TO POSSESS" — WHAT YOU WILL INHERIT YOU WILL CONQUER. (מַה שֶּׁתִּירַשׁ תִּכְבֹּשׁ "what you will inherit you will conquer"): the gift formula's seat (find 9 — 15:4 and 25:19 the only two "for an inheritance to possess it") — 180:1's and 297:1's trope (the English): the land's possession by war. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 296:9 — MATERIAL. On 25:17. "BLOT OUT THE MEMORY OF AMALEK" — AND THERE HE SAYS (EXODUS 17:14) "I WILL SURELY BLOT OUT THE MEMORY OF AMALEK", AND (17:16) "A HAND ON THE THRONE OF THE LORD: WAR FOR THE LORD WITH AMALEK FROM GENERATION TO GENERATION". (וּלְהַלָּן הוּא אוֹמֵר "and there He says"; מִלְחָמָה בַּעֲמָלֵק מִדֹּר דֹּר״ "war with Amalek from generation to generation"): THE TWO BLOTTINGS — the LORD's promise (Exodus 17:14, the tape's own line; find 10's run of five) and Israel's command, the same verb; the war "from generation to generation" the memory never wholly blotted (the English's note) — the chapter's, the portion's and the sitting's last row; 297:1 opens on 26:1 (the edge clean, the ink's assert). Fresh.

## Sifrei Devarim rows citing chapters 22-25 from outside the spine (14 rows found by the union of both files' citations over the four dumps, each read whole in both files; 10 read before and reread whole; 4 EXCLUDED after the whole read — 352:8, 352:9, 352:10, 352:17, the English's "Dt.23:12" a misprint for 33:12; not in the cite index)
- Sifrei Devarim 41:3 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_11_ekev_reeh_2026-09-20.md, deu_15_reeh_2026-09-22.md). On 11:13; cites 24:19. ONLY THE COMMANDMENTS BEFORE THEY CONQUERED AND SETTLED? THOSE AFTER — GLEANINGS, THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF, THE CORNER, TITHES, THE HEAVE-OFFERING, SABBATICAL YEARS AND JUBILEES — WHENCE? "IF YOU HEARKEN TO MY COMMANDMENTS" (11:13): TO ADD OTHER COMMANDMENTS TO THEM. The row on 11:13 (לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה "gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner"): THE FORGOTTEN SHEAF (24:19) named among the land's commandments learned after the settlement — the English's citation; the compile's daemon on the harvest's three gifts. REREAD WHOLE (chapters 11 and 15, deu_11_ekev_reeh and deu_15_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 63:5 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_12_reeh_2026-09-20.md). On 12:5; cites 23:22. IF ONE FESTIVAL PASSED AND HE DID NOT BRING IT, DOES HE TRANSGRESS "DELAY NOT"? "THESE YOU SHALL DO TO THE LORD AT YOUR APPOINTED TIMES" (Numbers 29:39) — HE DOES NOT TRANSGRESS "DELAY NOT" UNTIL THE FESTIVALS OF A WHOLE YEAR PASS OVER HIM. The row on 12:5 (בַּל תְּאַחֵר "delay not"): THE VOW'S CLOCK — 23:22's "delay not to pay it" measured by the three festivals (Rosh Hashanah 1:1 the answer sheet); the compile's clock parameter for chapter 23. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 12, deu_12_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 87:3 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_13_reeh_2026-09-21.md). On 13:7; cites 24:16. FROM "FATHERS SHALL NOT BE PUT TO DEATH FOR SONS" (24:16) WE LEARNED THAT THEY DO NOT TESTIFY FOR ONE ANOTHER; AS THEY DO NOT TESTIFY, DO THEY NOT INCITE ONE ANOTHER? "IF YOUR BROTHER, YOUR MOTHER'S SON, INCITES YOU" (13:7). The row on 13:7 (שֶׁאֵין מְעִידִים זֶה לָזֶה "that they do not testify for one another"): 24:16 READ AS THE KINSMEN'S TESTIMONY — the fathers-and-sons verse the ground that relatives cannot witness (Sanhedrin 3:4 the answer sheet), the compile's witness parameter for chapter 24 already cited at the seducer. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 13, deu_13_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 94:3 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_13_reeh_2026-09-21.md). On 13:16; cites 24:16. HENCE: THE CHILDREN OF THE CONDEMNED CITY ARE NOT SPARED; ABBA HANAN SAYS: "FATHERS SHALL NOT BE PUT TO DEATH FOR SONS" (24:16) — OF THE CONDEMNED CITY THE VERSE SPEAKS; "THE INHABITANTS OF THAT CITY" — NOT ANOTHER CITY'S; THE ASS-TRAIN AND THE CAMEL-TRAIN PASSING THROUGH SAVE IT; "BY THE SWORD'S EDGE" — NOT TO DISFIGURE THEM. The row on 13:16 (בְּעִיר הַנִּדַּחַת הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר "of the condemned city the verse speaks"): Abba Hanan's reading of 24:16 as the condemned city's children (against the row's first ruling) — a second seat of the verse in chapter 13. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 13, deu_13_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 110:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_14_reeh_2026-09-21.md). On 14:29; cites 24:17. "THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW IN YOUR GATES" (14:29) — NEEDY OR NOT? DO NOT WONDER: "TAKE NOT A WIDOW'S GARMENT IN PLEDGE" (24:17) — POOR OR RICH! "THE POOR" (Leviticus 19:10) — AS THE POOR MAN IS NEEDY, SO ALL THESE. The row on 14:29 (בֵּין עֲנִיָּה וּבֵין עֲשִׁירָה "whether poor or rich"): 24:17's WIDOW RICH OR POOR — the pledge's bar not conditioned on need, against the tithe's widow who must be needy; the compile's predicate for 24:17. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 14, deu_14_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 117:5 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_15_reeh_2026-09-22.md). On 15:9; cites 24:15. "AND HE CRIES AGAINST YOU TO THE LORD" (15:9) — A COMMANDMENT NOT TO CRY? "HE CRIES"; A COMMANDMENT TO CRY? "HE SHALL NOT CRY AGAINST YOU" (24:15); SIN ONLY IF HE CRIES? "IT SHALL BE SIN IN YOU" ANYWAY; WHY "HE CRIES"? I HASTEN TO REPAY THROUGH THE CRIER MORE THAN THROUGH THE SILENT. The row on 15:9 (מְמַהֵר אֲנִי לִפָּרַע "I hasten to repay"): 24:15'S CRY read with 15:9's — the hireling's and the needy's one heaven entry, the sin regardless, the cry the speed (the ink's find 7). REREAD WHOLE (chapter 15, deu_15_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 122:1 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_15_reeh_2026-09-22.md). On 15:17; cites 24:1. "YOU TAKE THE AWL" (15:17) — THE THORN, THE GLASS, THE REED: "YOU TAKE", R. YOSE B. R. JUDAH; RABBI: THE AWL — METAL; HENCE R. ISHMAEL: IN THREE PLACES THE LAW GOES AROUND SCRIPTURE — "COVER IT WITH DUST" (Leviticus 17:13), THE LAW: ANYTHING THAT GROWS PLANTS; "HE WRITES HER A BILL OF CUTTING-OFF" (24:1), THE LAW: ON ANYTHING DETACHED; "WITH THE AWL", THE LAW: WITH ANYTHING; RABBI: STANDING. The row on 15:17 (בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת הֲלָכָה עוֹקֶפֶת הַמִּקְרָא "in three places the law goes around Scripture"; בְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא בְּתָלוּשׁ "on anything that is detached"): THE BILL'S MATERIAL — 24:1's "bill" widened by the law to anything detached from the ground (Gittin 2:4 the answer sheet): one of R. Ishmael's three; the compile's parameter on the divorce's writing. REREAD WHOLE (chapter 15, deu_15_reeh).
- Sifrei Devarim 155:9 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_17_18_shoftim_2026-09-24.md). On 17:12; cites 22:1. "AND PURGE THE EVIL FROM ISRAEL" (17:12) — PURGE THE DOERS OF EVIL FROM ISRAEL. The row on 17:12 (בַּעֵר עוֹשֵׂי הָרָעוֹת מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל "purge the doers of evil from Israel"): the formula's third seat read as persons — 240:5 and 241:7 its words in chapter 22; the row sits among this ledger's outside rows by the translator's footnote alone ("Dt.22:1" for the formula's later seats), the Hebrew citing 17:12. REREAD WHOLE (chapters 17-18, deu_17_18_shoftim).
- Sifrei Devarim 190:14 — MATERIAL (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei_2026-09-24.md). On 19:17; cites 25:12. WHENCE THAT THE SHAMER PAYS MONEY? "YOUR EYE SHALL NOT PITY" HERE (19:21) AND "YOUR EYE SHALL NOT PITY" THERE (25:12): AS THERE MONEY, SO HERE MONEY. The row on 19:17 (שֶׁהַמְבַיֵּשׁ מְשַׁלֵּם מָמוֹן "that the shamer pays money"): 25:12'S "CUT OFF HER HAND" IS MONEY — the pity formula's analogy (I2) between the talion and the wrestler's wife: the compile's operator for chapter 25 (Bava Kamma 8:1 the answer sheet), read already at the talion. REREAD WHOLE (chapters 19-21, deu_19_21_shoftim_ki_teitzei).
- Sifrei Devarim 336:2 — MATERIAL. On 32:47; cites 22:7. "AND BY THIS THING YOU SHALL PROLONG DAYS" (32:47) — ONE OF THE THINGS WHOSE DOER EATS THEIR FRUIT IN THIS WORLD AND HAS LENGTH OF DAYS IN THE WORLD TO COME: HERE THE STUDY OF TORAH; HONORING FATHER AND MOTHER — "THAT YOUR DAYS BE LONG" (Exodus 20:12); SENDING THE NEST — "SURELY SEND THE MOTHER … THAT YOUR DAYS BE LONG" (22:7); BRINGING PEACE — "GREAT PEACE FOR YOUR CHILDREN" (Isaiah 54:13). The row on 32:47 (בְּשִׁלּוּחַ הַקֵּן כָּתוּב "of the sending of the nest it is written"): THE NEST'S REWARD in the list of Peah 1:1 with the parents' and peace's — 22:7's clause the ground; a heaven entry's formula. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 352:8 — EXCLUDED. On 33:11; cites 23:12. "AND BETWEEN HIS SHOULDERS HE DWELLS" (33:12) — WHETHER IN RUIN OR NOT IN RUIN; SO "ALL THE KINGDOMS OF THE EARTH THE LORD GOD OF HEAVEN GAVE ME" (2 Chronicles 36:23). The row on 33:11-12 (בֵּין חָרֵב בֵּין שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָרֵב "whether in ruin or not in ruin"): the Temple's place holy in ruin — THE ENGLISH CITES "Dt.23:12" FOR THE BLESSING OF BENJAMIN'S 33:12, a misprint; the Hebrew cites no verse of chapter 23: EXCLUDED from this ledger's coverage, read whole. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 352:9 — EXCLUDED (REREAD WHOLE — read before in deu_17_18_shoftim_2026-09-24.md). On 33:11; cites 23:12. ANOTHER WORD: "BETWEEN HIS SHOULDERS HE DWELLS" — AS THE OX HAS NOTHING HIGHER THAN ITS SHOULDERS, SO THE TEMPLE IS HIGHER THAN ALL THE WORLD: "YOU SHALL ARISE AND GO UP TO THE PLACE" (17:8), "MANY PEOPLES SHALL GO: COME, LET US GO UP" (Isaiah 2:3) — NOT GAD FROM THE EAST AND DAN FROM THE WEST, BUT "MANY PEOPLES". The row on 33:11-12 (גָּבוֹהַּ מִכָּל הָעוֹלָם "higher than all the world"): the Temple's height from 17:8's "go up" — the English's "Dt.23:12" the same misprint: EXCLUDED from this ledger's coverage. REREAD WHOLE (chapters 17-18, deu_17_18_shoftim).
- Sifrei Devarim 352:10 — EXCLUDED. On 33:11; cites 23:12. RABBI SAYS: OF EVERY BORDER IT SAYS "THE BORDER WENT DOWN", "WAS DRAWN", AND HERE "THE BORDER WENT UP THE VALLEY OF BEN-HINNOM TO THE SHOULDER OF THE JEBUSITE ON THE SOUTH — JERUSALEM" (Joshua 15:8): THE CHOSEN HOUSE WAS BUILT IN BENJAMIN'S PORTION; R. MEIR: IN BENJAMIN'S, AND A STRIP WENT OUT INTO JUDAH'S — "BETWEEN HIS SHOULDERS"; "THE SCEPTER SHALL NOT DEPART FROM JUDAH" (Genesis 49:10) — THE CHAMBER OF HEWN STONE IN JUDAH'S; THE GREAT HOUSE IN JOSEPH'S AT SHILOH; [R. JUDAH: IN JUDAH'S,] "BETHLEHEM EPHRATAH" (Micah 5:1), EPHRATH IS BETHLEHEM (Genesis 35:19). The row on 33:11-12 (בְּחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל בִּנְיָמִין "in Benjamin's portion"): the Temple's tribe — the English's "Dt.23:12" the misprint: EXCLUDED from this ledger's coverage, read whole. Fresh.
- Sifrei Devarim 352:17 — EXCLUDED. On 33:11; cites 23:12. ANOTHER WORD: WHY DID BENJAMIN MERIT THE PRESENCE IN HIS PORTION? A KING WITH MANY SONS — EACH GROWN SEIZED HIS PLACE; THE YOUNGEST HIS FATHER LOVED, ATE AND DRANK WITH HIM, LEANED ON HIM GOING OUT AND COMING IN; SO BENJAMIN THE RIGHTEOUS, THE YOUNGEST OF THE TRIBES, ON WHOM JACOB LEANED: WHERE THIS RIGHTEOUS ONE LEANED HIS HANDS, THERE I SET MY PRESENCE — "BETWEEN HIS SHOULDERS HE DWELLS". The row on 33:11-12 (קָטָן שֶׁבַּשְּׁבָטִים הָיָה "he was the youngest of the tribes"): the parable of the leaning father — the English's "Dt.23:12" the misprint: EXCLUDED from this ledger's coverage, read whole. Fresh.

## Onkelos Deuteronomy 22:1-29 (29 DB verses read whole — the export's 29 rows, the identity)
- Onkelos Deut 22:1 — MATERIAL. YOU SHALL NOT SEE YOUR BROTHER'S OX OR HIS SHEEP STRAYING AND HIDE YOURSELF FROM THEM; YOU SHALL SURELY RETURN THEM TO YOUR BROTHER. "לֹֽא תִרְאֶה אֶת שׁוֹר אָחִיךָ" (you shall not see your brother's ox — 22:4's opening word for word) "הָשֵׁב תְּשִׁיבֵם לְאָחִֽיךָ" (surely return them to your brother) אוֹ יָת אִמְּרֵיהּ דְּטָעָן (OR HIS SHEEP THAT STRAYED — the participle a relative clause) וְתִתְכְּבֵשׁ מִנְּהוֹן (and hide yourself from them) THE LOST THING'S DUTY: the piska reads it against Exodus 23:4 (222:4-5). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:2 — MATERIAL. IF YOUR BROTHER IS NOT NEAR YOU AND YOU KNOW HIM NOT, GATHER IT INTO YOUR HOUSE, AND IT STAYS WITH YOU UNTIL YOUR BROTHER SEEKS IT, AND YOU RETURN IT TO HIM. "עַד דְּרֹשׁ אָחִיךָ אֹתוֹ" (until your brother seeks it — 223:3's marks and the fraud) עַד דְּיִתְבַּע אֲחוּךְ יָתֵיהּ (UNTIL YOUR BROTHER CLAIMS IT — seek rendered claim (tava), the legal word) THE KEEPING until the claim. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:3 — MATERIAL. SO SHALL YOU DO WITH HIS ASS, HIS GARMENT, EVERY LOST THING OF YOUR BROTHER'S WHICH IS LOST FROM HIM AND YOU FOUND; YOU MAY NOT HIDE YOURSELF. "לְכָל אֲבֵדַת אָחִיךָ" (every lost thing of your brother — 224:5's widening) "לֹא תוּכַל לְהִתְעַלֵּֽם" (you may not hide yourself) לֵית לָךְ רְשׁוּ לְאִתְכַּסָּאָה (YOU HAVE NO PERMISSION TO HIDE YOURSELF — 'may not' rendered 'no permission' (reshu), the Aramaic's modal for the book's 'cannot' (22:19, 22:29, 24:4)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:4 — MATERIAL. YOU SHALL NOT SEE YOUR BROTHER'S ASS OR HIS OX FALLEN ON THE WAY AND HIDE YOURSELF; YOU SHALL SURELY RAISE WITH HIM. "נֹפְלִים בַּדֶּרֶךְ" (fallen on the way — 225:3-4's two conditions) "הָקֵם תָּקִים עִמּֽוֹ" (surely raise with him — 225:6's owner's part) רָמָן בְּאָרְחָא (LYING ON THE ROAD) אֲקָמָא תְקִים עִמֵּיהּ (surely raise with him) THE LOADING (222:3) beside Exodus 23:5's unloading. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:5 — MATERIAL. NO MAN'S GEAR SHALL BE ON A WOMAN, NOR SHALL A MAN WEAR A WOMAN'S GARMENT, FOR AN ABOMINATION OF THE LORD YOUR GOD IS EVERYONE WHO DOES THESE. "לֹא יִהְיֶה כְלִי גֶבֶר עַל אִשָּׁה" (no man's gear on a woman) "כִּי תוֹעֲבַת יְהוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ כָּל עֹשֵׂה אֵֽלֶּה" (for an abomination of the LORD your God is everyone who does these — the run of seven shared with 25:16) לָא יְהֵי תִקּוּן זֵין דִּגְבַר עַל אִתְּתָא (NO ARMAMENT OF A MAN ON A WOMAN — the gear rendered weapons (R. Eliezer b. Jacob's reading, 226:2)) וְלָא יְתַקֵּן גְּבַר בְּתִקּוּנֵי אִתְּתָא (nor shall a man ADORN himself with a woman's ADORNMENTS — the garment rendered ornaments (226:1's rule)) אֲרֵי מְרָחָק קֳדָם יְיָ (for it is DISTANCED before the LORD — the abomination formula's rendering, eleven seats) ONKELOS WRITES THE SIFREI'S TWO RULES INTO THE VERSE; the Name's one seat in the chapter. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:6 — MATERIAL. IF A BIRD'S NEST CHANCE BEFORE YOU ON THE WAY, IN ANY TREE OR ON THE GROUND, FLEDGLINGS OR EGGS, AND THE MOTHER SITTING ON THE FLEDGLINGS OR ON THE EGGS — TAKE NOT THE MOTHER WITH THE YOUNG. "כִּי יִקָּרֵא קַן צִפּוֹר לְפָנֶיךָ בַּדֶּרֶךְ" (if a bird's nest chance before you on the way — 227:1's chance, 227:3-4's before you and the way) "לֹא תִקַּח הָאֵם עַל הַבָּנִֽים" (take not the mother with the young — 227:10-11) אֲרֵי יְעָרַע קִנָּא דְצִפְּרָא קֳדָמָךְ בְּאָרְחָא (IF A BIRD'S NEST HAPPEN BEFORE YOU ON THE ROAD) וְאִמָא רְבֵעָא עַל אֶפְרֹחִין (and the mother sitting on the fledglings — 227:8's posture) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:7 — MATERIAL. SURELY SEND THE MOTHER AWAY AND TAKE THE YOUNG FOR YOURSELF, THAT IT MAY BE WELL WITH YOU AND YOUR DAYS LONG. "שַׁלֵּחַ תְּשַׁלַּח אֶת הָאֵם" (surely send the mother — 228:1's positive commandment) "לְמַעַן יִיטַב לָךְ וְהַאֲרַכְתָּ יָמִֽים" (that it be well with you and your days long — the parents' reward formula (5:16), 336:2's list) בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לָךְ וְתוֹרִיךְ יוֹמִין (SO THAT IT BE WELL WITH YOU AND YOU PROLONG DAYS) THE REWARD CLAUSE on the cheapest commandment (228:8's a fortiori). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:8 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU BUILD A NEW HOUSE, MAKE A PARAPET FOR YOUR ROOF, AND PUT NOT BLOOD IN YOUR HOUSE WHEN THE FALLER FALLS FROM IT. "כִּי תִבְנֶה בַּיִת חָדָשׁ" (when you build a new house — 20:5's phrase, 229:1) "וְלֹֽא תָשִׂים דָּמִים בְּבֵיתֶךָ" (and put not blood in your house — 229:2-4's widening) "כִּי יִפֹּל הַנֹּפֵל מִמֶּֽנּוּ" (when the faller falls from it — 229:7-8) וְתַעְבֵּד תְּיָקָא לְאִגְּרָךְ (AND MAKE A FENCE FOR YOUR ROOF) וְלָא תְשַׁוִּי חוֹבַת דִּין דִקְטוֹל בְּבֵיתָךְ (AND PUT NOT THE LIABILITY OF A DEATH SENTENCE IN YOUR HOUSE — 'blood' rendered as the court's liability (the phrase again at 22:26)) ONKELOS MAKES THE BLOOD A LIABILITY — the parapet's omission a capital guilt in the Aramaic. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:9 — MATERIAL. SOW NOT YOUR VINEYARD WITH MIXED SEED, LEST THE FULLNESS OF THE SEED YOU SOW AND THE PRODUCE OF THE VINEYARD BE SANCTIFIED. "לֹא תִזְרַע כַּרְמְךָ כִּלְאָיִם" (sow not your vineyard with mixed seed — 230:1's second prohibition) "פֶּן תִּקְדַּשׁ הַֽמְלֵאָה הַזֶּרַע" (lest the fullness of the seed be sanctified — 230:3-4, 230:12) לָא תִזְרַע כַּרְמָךְ עֵרוּבִין (SOW NOT YOUR VINEYARD WITH MIXTURES) דִּילְמָא תִסְתָּאַב דִּמְעַת זַרְעָא (LEST THE FULLNESS OF THE SEED BE DEFILED — 'sanctified' rendered 'defiled' (tistaav): the forbidden as the unclean, R. Josiah's 'lest it be forbidden' (230:12) in the Aramaic's word) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:10 — MATERIAL. PLOW NOT WITH AN OX AND AN ASS TOGETHER. "לֹֽא תַחֲרֹשׁ בְּשׁוֹר וּבַחֲמֹר יַחְדָּֽו" (plow not with an ox and an ass together — 231:1's pair, 230:10's each alone) לָא תִרְדֵּי בְתוֹרָא וּבַחֲמָרָא כַּחֲדָא (PLOW NOT WITH THE OX AND THE ASS AS ONE) the five-word verse with no kin in the Bible (the ink's find 12). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:11 — MATERIAL. WEAR NOT MINGLED STUFF, WOOL AND LINEN TOGETHER. "לֹא תִלְבַּשׁ שַֽׁעַטְנֵז צֶמֶר וּפִשְׁתִּים יַחְדָּֽו" (wear not mingled stuff, wool and linen together — 232:1's acronym, 232:3's together) לָא תִלְבַּשׁ שַׁעַטְנֵזָא עֲמַר וְכִתָּן מְחֻבַּר כַּחֲדָא (WEAR NOT MINGLED STUFF, WOOL AND LINEN JOINED AS ONE — 'joined' (mechabar) added: the joining the mixture (232:4)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:12 — MATERIAL. TASSELS MAKE FOR YOURSELF ON THE FOUR CORNERS OF YOUR COVERING WITH WHICH YOU COVER YOURSELF. "גְּדִלִים תַּעֲשֶׂה לָּךְ" (tassels make for yourself — 234:1-3's threads; the word the consonants of 'great') "עַל אַרְבַּע כַּנְפוֹת כְּסוּתְךָ" (on the four corners of your covering — 234:4-7's exclusions) כְּרֻסְפְּדִין תַּעְבֶּד לָךְ עַל אַרְבַּע כַּנְפֵי כְסוּתָךְ (KRUSPEDIN MAKE FOR YOURSELF ON THE FOUR CORNERS — the Greek word for fringe (kraspedon) in the Aramaic) THE GREEK LOANWORD for the tassels. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:13 — MATERIAL. WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE AND GOES IN TO HER AND HATES HER. "כִּֽי יִקַּח אִישׁ אִשָּׁה וּבָא אֵלֶיהָ וּשְׂנֵאָֽהּ" (when a man takes a wife and goes in to her and hates her — 235:1's condition, 24:1's opening) אֲרֵי יִסַּב גְּבַר אִתְּתָא וְיֵעוּל לְוָתַהּ וְיִסְנִנַּהּ (WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE AND GOES IN TO HER AND HATES HER) the seven-word verse opening the bride's case. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:14 — MATERIAL. AND LAYS A CHARGE OF WORDS AGAINST HER AND BRINGS ON HER AN EVIL NAME, AND SAYS: THIS WOMAN I TOOK AND CAME NEAR TO HER AND FOUND NO TOKENS IN HER. "וְשָׂם לָהּ עֲלִילֹת דְּבָרִים" (and lays a charge of words — 235:3's analogy with 22:17) "וְלֹא מָצָאתִי לָהּ בְּתוּלִֽים" (and found no tokens in her — 235:5's witnesses) וִישַׁוִּי לַהּ תַּסְקוֹפֵי מִלִּין (AND LAYS ON HER ACCUSATIONS OF WORDS (taskufei)) וְיַפֵּק עֲלַהּ שׁוּם בִּישׁ (and brings out on her an evil name) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:15 — MATERIAL. THE FATHER OF THE GIRL AND HER MOTHER TAKE AND BRING OUT THE GIRL'S TOKENS TO THE ELDERS OF THE CITY AT THE GATE. "וְלָקַח אֲבִי הנער וְאִמָּהּ" (the father of the girl and her mother take — 'the girl' written as a boy (the store one token more)) "אֶל זִקְנֵי הָעִיר הַשָּֽׁעְרָה" (to the elders of the city, at the gate) וְיִסַּב אֲבוּהָא (THE FATHER OF THE GIRL (ulemta) AND HER MOTHER TAKE — the export's 'another reading' mark (nusach acher: her father / his father) inside the Aramaic — the parenthesized mark no word of the verse, the cut goes around it) דְעוּלֶמְתָּא וְאִמַּהּ (of the girl (ulemta) and her mother) לָקֳדָם סָבֵי קַרְתָּא לִתְרַע בֵּית דִּין אַתְרָא (BEFORE THE ELDERS OF THE CITY, TO THE GATE OF THE COURT OF THE PLACE — the gate rendered the court's gate) THE VARIANT APPARATUS IN THE TEXT and the court named. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:16 — MATERIAL. AND THE FATHER OF THE GIRL SAYS TO THE ELDERS: MY DAUGHTER I GAVE TO THIS MAN FOR A WIFE, AND HE HATES HER. "וְאָמַר אֲבִי הנער אֶל הַזְּקֵנִים" (and the father of the girl says to the elders — 235:10 the father speaks, 236:3 first) "אֶת בִּתִּי נָתַתִּי לָאִישׁ הַזֶּה לְאִשָּׁה" (my daughter I gave to this man for a wife — 235:11's right over the minor) וְיֵימַר אֲבוּהָא דְעוּלֶמְתָּא לְסָבַיָּא (AND THE GIRL'S FATHER SAYS TO THE ELDERS) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:17 — MATERIAL. AND BEHOLD, HE LAYS A CHARGE OF WORDS, SAYING: I FOUND NO TOKENS IN YOUR DAUGHTER — AND THESE ARE MY DAUGHTER'S TOKENS; AND THEY SPREAD THE GARMENT BEFORE THE ELDERS OF THE CITY. "וְאֵלֶּה בְּתוּלֵי בִתִּי" (and these are my daughter's tokens — 236:1's counter-witnesses) "וּפָֽרְשׂוּ הַשִּׂמְלָה לִפְנֵי זִקְנֵי הָעִֽיר" (and they spread the garment before the elders — 237's three readings) וְיִפְרְסוּן שׁוֹשִׁיפָא קֳדָם סָבֵי קַרְתָּא (AND THEY SPREAD THE SHEET (shoshipa) BEFORE THE ELDERS OF THE CITY — the garment a specific cloth in the Aramaic, R. Eliezer b. Jacob's letter (237:3)) the chapter's one "saying". Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:18 — MATERIAL. AND THE ELDERS OF THAT CITY TAKE THE MAN AND CHASTISE HIM. "וְלָֽקְחוּ זִקְנֵי הָֽעִיר הַהִוא אֶת הָאִישׁ" (and the elders of that city take the man — 238:1 not the minor) "וְיִסְּרוּ אֹתֽוֹ" (and chastise him — 21:18's verb for the son) וְיַלְקוּן יָתֵיהּ (AND THEY FLOG HIM — chastise rendered flog: Onkelos and 238:2 agree, the lashes) ONKELOS AND THE SIFREI AGREE ON THE LASHES. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:19 — MATERIAL. AND FINE HIM A HUNDRED OF SILVER AND GIVE TO THE GIRL'S FATHER, FOR HE BROUGHT AN EVIL NAME ON A VIRGIN OF ISRAEL; AND SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE — HE MAY NOT SEND HER AWAY ALL HIS DAYS. "וְעָנְשׁוּ אֹתוֹ מֵאָה כֶסֶף" (and fine him a hundred of silver — 238:3-4 money, Tyrian) "וְנָתְנוּ לַאֲבִי הַֽנַּעֲרָה" (and give to the girl's father — THE GIRL WRITTEN IN FULL, the one seat of twelve) "לֹא יוּכַל לְשַּׁלְּחָהּ כָּל יָמָֽיו" (he may not send her away all his days — with the lamed; 22:29 without it) וְיִגְבּוּן מִנֵּיהּ מְאָה סִלְעִין דִּכְסָף (AND THEY EXACT FROM HIM A HUNDRED SELA OF SILVER — the coin named) לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ לְמִפְטְרַהּ כָּל יוֹמוֹהִי (HE HAS NO PERMISSION TO RELEASE HER ALL HIS DAYS — 'send away' rendered release (patar — the bill's root, 24:1's get piturin)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:20 — MATERIAL. BUT IF THIS THING WAS TRUE — NO TOKENS WERE FOUND FOR THE GIRL. "וְאִם אֱמֶת הָיָה הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה" (but if this thing was true — 239:1's 'was' and 'this') "לֹא נִמְצְאוּ בְתוּלִים לנער" (no tokens were found for the girl — 239:2 no refuting witnesses) וְאִם קֻשְׁטָא הֲוָה פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין (AND IF THE WORD WAS TRUE (kushta)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:21 — MATERIAL. THEY BRING THE GIRL OUT TO THE DOOR OF HER FATHER'S HOUSE, AND THE MEN OF HER CITY STONE HER WITH STONES AND SHE DIES, FOR SHE DID FOLLY IN ISRAEL TO PLAY THE HARLOT IN HER FATHER'S HOUSE; AND YOU PURGE THE EVIL FROM YOUR MIDST. "אֶל פֶּתַח בֵּית אָבִיהָ" (to the door of her father's house — 239:3, 235:6's place) "כִּֽי עָשְׂתָה נְבָלָה בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל" (for she did folly in Israel — Achan's and Jeremiah's phrase) "וּבִֽעַרְתָּ הָרָע מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ" (and purge the evil from your midst — the formula's sixth seat) אֲרֵי עָבְדַת קְלָנָא בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל (FOR SHE DID A DISGRACE IN ISRAEL (kelana)) וּתְפַלֵּי עָבֵד דְּבִישׁ מִבֵּינָךְ (AND REMOVE THE DOER OF EVIL FROM AMONG YOU — the formula as a person, 240:5's reading) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:22 — MATERIAL. IF A MAN BE FOUND LYING WITH A WOMAN MARRIED TO A HUSBAND, BOTH OF THEM SHALL DIE — THE MAN LYING WITH THE WOMAN AND THE WOMAN; AND YOU PURGE THE EVIL FROM ISRAEL. "אִשָּׁה בְעֻֽלַת בַּעַל" (a woman married to a husband — Sarah's phrase (Genesis 20:3); 236:4's levirate widow) "וּמֵתוּ גַּם שְׁנֵיהֶם" (both of them shall die — 241:2 by strangling, 241:3-6's arms) עִם אִתְּתָא אִתַּת גְּבַר (WITH A WOMAN, A MAN'S WIFE — the rare phrase made plain) וְיִתְקַטְּלוּן אַף תַּרְוֵיהוֹן (THEY SHALL BE KILLED, EVEN BOTH OF THEM — 'die' rendered 'be executed') Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:23 — MATERIAL. IF THERE BE A VIRGIN GIRL BETROTHED TO A MAN, AND A MAN FINDS HER IN THE CITY AND LIES WITH HER. "כִּי יִהְיֶה נער בְתוּלָה מְאֹרָשָׂה לְאִישׁ" (if there be a virgin girl betrothed to a man — 242:1's three conditions; the girl written bare (na'ar)) "וּמְצָאָהּ אִישׁ בָּעִיר וְשָׁכַב עִמָּֽהּ" (and a man finds her in the city and lies with her — 242:2-3) אֲרֵי תְהֵי עוּלֶמְתָּא בְתֻלְתָּא דִּמְאָרְסָא לִגְבַר (IF THERE BE A GIRL, A VIRGIN, BETROTHED TO A MAN) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:24 — MATERIAL. YOU BRING BOTH OF THEM OUT TO THE GATE OF THAT CITY AND STONE THEM WITH STONES AND THEY DIE — THE GIRL BECAUSE SHE DID NOT CRY OUT IN THE CITY, AND THE MAN BECAUSE HE HUMBLED HIS NEIGHBOR'S WIFE; AND YOU PURGE THE EVIL FROM YOUR MIDST. "וְהוֹצֵאתֶם אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם אֶל שַׁעַר הָעִיר הַהִוא" (you bring both out to the gate of that city — 242:4's gate where found; the plural 'you' the chapter's one) "עַל דְּבַר אֲשֶׁר לֹא צָעֲקָה בָעִיר" (because she did not cry out in the city — 242:6's warning) "אֲשֶׁר עִנָּה אֶת אֵשֶׁת רֵעֵהוּ" (he humbled his neighbor's wife — the betrothed already a wife) עַל עֵסַק דִּי לָא צְוָחַת בְּקַרְתָּא (ON THE MATTER THAT SHE DID NOT CRY OUT IN THE CITY (esak)) דְּעַנִּי יָת אִתַּת חַבְרֵיהּ (that he humbled his fellow's wife) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:25 — MATERIAL. BUT IF IN THE FIELD THE MAN FINDS THE BETROTHED GIRL AND THE MAN SEIZES HER AND LIES WITH HER, THE MAN WHO LAY WITH HER SHALL DIE ALONE. "וְֽאִם בַּשָּׂדֶה יִמְצָא הָאִישׁ" (but if in the field the man finds — 242:8's second arm, 243:3's collapse) "וְהֶחֱזִֽיק בָּהּ הָאִישׁ וְשָׁכַב עִמָּהּ" (and the man seizes her and lies with her — 242:9's holder) "וּמֵת הָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר שָׁכַב עִמָּהּ לְבַדּֽוֹ" (the man who lay with her shall die alone — 242:10's stoning) וְאִם בְּחַקְלָא יַשְׁכַּח גְּבַר (BUT IF IN THE FIELD A MAN FINDS) וְיִתְקְטֵל גַּבְרָא דְּשָׁכֵב עִמַּהּ בִּלְחוֹדוֹהִי (THE MAN WHO LAY WITH HER SHALL BE KILLED ALONE) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:26 — MATERIAL. AND TO THE GIRL DO NOTHING; THE GIRL HAS NO SIN WORTHY OF DEATH, FOR AS A MAN RISES AGAINST HIS NEIGHBOR AND MURDERS HIM, SO IS THIS THING. "ולנער לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה דָבָר" (and to the girl do nothing — the girl written twice as a boy (the store two tokens more)) "אֵין לנער חֵטְא מָוֶת" (the girl has no sin worthy of death — 243:1's three exemptions) "כַּאֲשֶׁר יָקוּם אִישׁ עַל רֵעֵהוּ וּרְצָחוֹ נֶפֶשׁ" (as a man rises against his neighbor and murders him — 243:2's pursuer) לֵית לְעוּלֶמְתָּא חוֹבַת דִּין דִּקְטוֹל (THE GIRL HAS NO LIABILITY OF A DEATH SENTENCE — 22:8's phrase for 'blood', the chapter's second seat) כֵּן פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין (so is this word) THE ARAMAIC'S ONE PHRASE FOR THE PARAPET'S BLOOD AND THE GIRL'S SIN. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:27 — MATERIAL. FOR IN THE FIELD HE FOUND HER; THE BETROTHED GIRL CRIED OUT AND THERE WAS NONE TO SAVE HER. "צָעֲקָה הנער הַמְאֹרָשָׂה" (the betrothed girl cried out — 243:4's 'let him be') "וְאֵין מוֹשִׁיעַ לָֽהּ" (and there was none to save her — 243:3 the savior the condition) צְוָחַת עוּלֶמְתָּא דִּמְאָרְסָא וְלֵית דְּפָרִיק לַהּ (THE BETROTHED GIRL CRIED OUT AND THERE WAS NONE WHO REDEEMS HER — save rendered redeem (parik)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:28 — MATERIAL. IF A MAN FINDS A VIRGIN GIRL WHO IS NOT BETROTHED AND SEIZES HER AND LIES WITH HER, AND THEY ARE FOUND. "נער בְתוּלָה אֲשֶׁר לֹא אֹרָשָׂה" (a virgin girl who is not betrothed — Exodus 22:15's run of four; 244:1's three a fortiori) "וּתְפָשָׂהּ וְשָׁכַב עִמָּהּ וְנִמְצָֽאוּ" (and seizes her and lies with her and they are found — 244:3 by witnesses) וְיֶחֱדִנַּהּ וְיִשְׁכּוּב עִמַּהּ וְיִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן (AND SEIZES HER AND LIES WITH HER AND THEY ARE FOUND) THE RAPIST beside Exodus's seducer. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 22:29 — MATERIAL. THE MAN WHO LAY WITH HER GIVES THE GIRL'S FATHER FIFTY OF SILVER, AND SHE SHALL BE HIS WIFE BECAUSE HE HUMBLED HER; HE MAY NOT SEND HER AWAY ALL HIS DAYS. "לַאֲבִי הנער חֲמִשִּׁים כָּסֶף" (to the girl's father fifty of silver — 245:2-4) "וְלֽוֹ תִהְיֶה לְאִשָּׁה תַּחַת אֲשֶׁר עִנָּהּ" (and she shall be his wife because he humbled her — 22:19's run of three; 245:5-6) "לֹא יוּכַל שַׁלְּחָה כָּל יָמָֽיו" (he may not send her away all his days — without the lamed, 22:19's twin close) חַמְשִׁין סִלְעִין דִּכְסַף (FIFTY SELA OF SILVER) חֳלַף דִּי עַנְיַהּ (in exchange that he humbled her) לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ לְמִפְטְרַהּ כָּל יוֹמוֹהִי (HE HAS NO PERMISSION TO RELEASE HER ALL HIS DAYS — 22:19's clause word for word) the chapter's close; the levir the one release (245:7). Fresh.

## Onkelos Deuteronomy 23:1-26 (26 DB verses read whole — the export's 26 rows, the identity)
- Onkelos Deut 23:1 — MATERIAL. A MAN SHALL NOT TAKE HIS FATHER'S WIFE, NOR UNCOVER HIS FATHER'S SKIRT. "לֹא יִקַּח אִישׁ אֶת אֵשֶׁת אָבִיו" (a man shall not take his father's wife — 246:1's head) לָא יִסַּב גְּבַר יָת אִתַּת אֲבוּהִי (A MAN SHALL NOT MARRY HIS FATHER'S WIFE — take rendered marry (nasav)) כַּנְפָא דַאֲבוּהִי (the skirt of his father) the English Bible's 22:30, the Hebrew's 23:1 — the export with the Hebrew (the ink). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:2 — MATERIAL. ONE CRUSHED OR CUT SHALL NOT ENTER THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD. "פְצֽוּעַ דַּכָּא וּכְרוּת שָׁפְכָה" (crushed and cut — 247:1-2's two classes) לָא יִדְכֵּי דְפָסִיק וְדִמְחַבַּל (HE SHALL NOT BE FIT (yidkei) — THE CUT AND THE MAIMED: 'shall not enter' rendered 'shall not be fit to enter', the ink's find 15) לְמֵעַל בִּקְהָלָא דַיְיָ (to enter the assembly of the LORD) fitness (dakhi, the root of clean) the Aramaic's word for the ban — the same root at 23:11's "clean". Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:3 — MATERIAL. NO MAMZER SHALL ENTER THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD; EVEN THE TENTH GENERATION SHALL NOT ENTER. "לֹא יָבֹא מַמְזֵר בִּקְהַל יְהוָה" (no mamzer shall enter — 248:1) "גַּם דּוֹר עֲשִׂירִי" (even the tenth generation — 248:5; the parser's ordinal [10]) לָא יִדְכֵּי מַמְזֵרָא (THE MAMZER SHALL NOT BE FIT — the word kept in the Aramaic) דָּרָא עֲשִׂירָאָה (the tenth generation) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:4 — MATERIAL. NO AMMONITE OR MOABITE SHALL ENTER THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD; EVEN THE TENTH GENERATION SHALL NOT ENTER, FOR EVER. "עַמּוֹנִי וּמוֹאָבִי" (Ammonite and Moabite — 249:1's males) "עַד עוֹלָֽם" (for ever — 249:4's free word for the analogy) לָא יִדְכּוּן עַמּוֹנָאֵי וּמוֹאָבָאֵי (AMMONITES AND MOABITES SHALL NOT BE FIT — the plural) עַד עֳלָם (for ever) find 8's run of seven with 23:3. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:5 — MATERIAL. BECAUSE THEY MET YOU NOT WITH BREAD AND WATER ON THE WAY WHEN YOU CAME OUT OF EGYPT, AND BECAUSE HE HIRED BALAAM SON OF BEOR FROM PETHOR OF ARAM-NAHARAIM AGAINST YOU TO CURSE YOU. "עַל דְּבַר אֲשֶׁר לֹא קִדְּמוּ אֶתְכֶם" (because they met you not — 250:1's 'also the counsel') "בַּדֶּרֶךְ בְּצֵאתְכֶם מִמִּצְרָיִם" (on the way when you came out of Egypt — the three remembrances' phrase, find 2) עַל עֵסַק (BECAUSE OF THE MATTER) אֲרַם דִּי עַל פְּרָת (ARAM WHICH IS ON THE EUPHRATES — Aram of the two rivers named by its river; the store's '?' gloss on Aram (the measure)) the plural "you" (find 14). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:6 — MATERIAL. THE LORD YOUR GOD WOULD NOT LISTEN TO BALAAM, AND TURNED THE CURSE INTO A BLESSING FOR YOU, BECAUSE THE LORD YOUR GOD LOVED YOU. "וַיַּהֲפֹךְ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְּךָ אֶת הַקְּלָלָה לִבְרָכָה" (turned the curse to a blessing — 250:5) יָת לְוָטִין לְבִרְכָן (THE CURSES INTO BLESSINGS — the plural for the singular: Balaam's several oracles (Numbers 23-24 on the tape)) אֲרֵי רָחֲמָךְ (because He loved you) no head on 23:6 — 250:5 its row. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:7 — MATERIAL. SEEK NOT THEIR PEACE NOR THEIR GOOD ALL YOUR DAYS FOR EVER. "לֹא תִדְרֹשׁ שְׁלֹמָם וְטֹבָתָם" (seek not their peace nor their good — 251:1-2) לָא תִתְבַּע שְׁלָמְהוֹן וְטָבָתְהוֹן (CLAIM NOT (tava) THEIR PEACE AND THEIR GOOD) the verse with no kin of two words (find 12). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:8 — MATERIAL. ABHOR NOT AN EDOMITE, FOR HE IS YOUR BROTHER; ABHOR NOT AN EGYPTIAN, FOR YOU WERE A SOJOURNER IN HIS LAND. "לֹֽא תְתַעֵב אֲדֹמִי כִּי אָחִיךָ הוּא" (abhor not the Edomite, he is your brother — 252:1) "כִּי גֵר הָיִיתָ בְאַרְצֽוֹ" (you were a sojourner in his land — 252:2) לָא תְרַחַק אֱדוֹמָאָה (DISTANCE NOT THE EDOMITE — abhor rendered distance (rachak), the abomination formula's verb (find 5)) דַיָּר הֲוֵיתָא בְאַרְעֵיהּ (a dweller you were in his land) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:9 — MATERIAL. SONS BORN TO THEM IN THE THIRD GENERATION SHALL ENTER THE ASSEMBLY OF THE LORD. "בָּנִים אֲשֶׁר יִוָּלְדוּ לָהֶם" (sons born to them — 253:1) "דּוֹר שְׁלִישִׁי" (the third generation — the parser's ordinal [3]; 253:3) דָּרָא תְלִיתָאָה יִדְכֵּי לְהוֹן (THE THIRD GENERATION SHALL BE FIT FOR THEM) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:10 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU GO OUT AS A CAMP AGAINST YOUR ENEMIES, KEEP YOURSELF FROM EVERY EVIL THING. "כִּֽי תֵצֵא מַחֲנֶה עַל אֹיְבֶיךָ" (when you go out as a camp — 254:1-2) "וְנִשְׁמַרְתָּ מִכֹּל דָּבָר רָֽע" (keep from every evil thing — 254:3-4) עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבָךְ (AGAINST THE MASTERS OF YOUR ENMITY — the Aramaic's idiom for enemies) מִכֹּל מִדַּעַם בִּישׁ (from every evil thing) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:11 — MATERIAL. IF THERE BE AMONG YOU A MAN NOT CLEAN FROM A CHANCE OF THE NIGHT, HE SHALL GO OUTSIDE THE CAMP AND NOT COME WITHIN IT. "מִקְּרֵה לָיְלָה" (from a chance of the night — 255:3's usual case) "וְיָצָא אֶל מִחוּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶה" (he goes outside the camp — 255:4-5's two commandments) לָא יְהֵי דְכֵי מִקְּרֵה לֵילְיָא (NOT CLEAN FROM A NIGHT'S CHANCE — clean (dakhei) the root of 'fit' at 23:2-4) לָא יֵעוּל לְגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָא (he shall not enter within the camp) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:12 — MATERIAL. TOWARD EVENING HE BATHES IN WATER, AND AT SUNSET HE COMES WITHIN THE CAMP. "לִפְנֽוֹת עֶרֶב יִרְחַץ בַּמָּיִם" (toward evening he bathes — 256:1) "וּכְבֹא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ" (and at sunset — 256:2's clock) לְמִפְנֵי רַמְשָׁא יַסְחֵי בְמַיָּא (TOWARD EVENING HE SHALL BATHE) וּכְמֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא (and when the sun comes in) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:13 — MATERIAL. A HAND YOU SHALL HAVE OUTSIDE THE CAMP, AND GO OUT THERE. "וְיָד תִּהְיֶה לְךָ מִחוּץ לַֽמַּחֲנֶה" (a hand you shall have outside the camp — 257:1's 'hand is a place') וַאֲתַר מְתַקָּן (A PREPARED PLACE — Onkelos writes 257:1's gloss into the verse) וְתִפּוֹק תַּמָּן לְבָרָא (and go out there outside) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:14 — MATERIAL. A PEG YOU SHALL HAVE ON YOUR GEAR; WHEN YOU SIT OUTSIDE, DIG WITH IT, RETURN AND COVER YOUR EXCREMENT. "וְיָתֵד תִּהְיֶה לְךָ עַל אֲזֵנֶךָ" (a peg on your gear — 257:3) "וְחָפַרְתָּה בָהּ וְשַׁבְתָּ וְכִסִּיתָ" (dig with it, return and cover — 257:5's one peg, 257:6's south) וְסִכְּתָא תְּהֵי לָךְ עַל זֵינָךְ (A PEG YOU SHALL HAVE ON YOUR WEAPONS — the Sifrei's gloss 'the place of your weapons' (257:3) is Onkelos's noun zinakh) וּתְכַסֵּי יָת מִפְּקָתָךְ (and cover your excrement) 23:14 carries no head — 257:3-6 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:15 — MATERIAL. FOR THE LORD YOUR GOD WALKS IN THE MIDST OF YOUR CAMP TO SAVE YOU AND GIVE YOUR ENEMIES BEFORE YOU; YOUR CAMP SHALL BE HOLY, THAT HE SEE NO UNSEEMLY THING IN YOU AND TURN FROM YOU. "מִתְהַלֵּךְ בְּקֶרֶב מַחֲנֶךָ" (walks in the midst of your camp — 258:1) "וְהָיָה מַחֲנֶיךָ קָדוֹשׁ" (your camp shall be holy — 258:3) "עֶרְוַת דָּבָר" (an unseemly thing — 258:4; 24:1's phrase) שְׁכִנְתֵּיהּ מְהַלְּכָא בְּגוֹ מַשְׁרִיתָךְ (HIS SHEKHINAH (indwelling presence) WALKS WITHIN YOUR CAMP — find 15) עֲבֵרַת פִּתְגָּם (A TRANSGRESSION OF A WORD — the unseemly thing, as at 24:1) וִיתוּב מֵימְרֵיהּ מִלְּאוֹטָבָא לָךְ (AND HIS MEMRA (word) TURN FROM DOING YOU GOOD) the heaven entry of the camp (the design's (d)). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:16 — MATERIAL. DELIVER NOT A SLAVE TO HIS MASTER WHO ESCAPES TO YOU FROM HIS MASTER. "לֹא תַסְגִּיר עֶבֶד אֶל אֲדֹנָיו" (deliver not a slave to his master — 259:1, 259:8) לָא תִמְסַר עֶבֶד עַמְמִין (DELIVER NOT A SLAVE OF THE NATIONS — Onkelos writes 259:8's gentile into the verse) לְיַד רִבּוֹנֵיהּ (into the hand of his master) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:17 — MATERIAL. WITH YOU HE SHALL DWELL, IN YOUR MIDST, IN THE PLACE HE CHOOSES IN ONE OF YOUR GATES WHERE IT IS GOOD FOR HIM; OPPRESS HIM NOT. "בְּאַחַד שְׁעָרֶיךָ" (in one of your gates — the parser's [1]; 259:5) "בַּטּוֹב לוֹ לֹא תּוֹנֶֽנּוּ" (where it is good for him, oppress him not — 259:6-7) בַּחֲדָא מִן קִרְוָיךְ (IN ONE OF YOUR CITIES — gates rendered cities) לָא תוֹנִנֵּיהּ (oppress him not) 23:17 carries no head — 259:3-7 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:18 — MATERIAL. THERE SHALL BE NO HARLOT OF ISRAEL'S DAUGHTERS, NOR A MALE OF ISRAEL'S SONS. "לֹא תִהְיֶה קְדֵשָׁה מִבְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל" (no harlot of Israel's daughters — 260:1) "וְלֹֽא יִהְיֶה קָדֵשׁ מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל" (nor a male of Israel's sons) אִתְּתָא מִבְּנַת יִשְׂרָאֵל לִגְבַר עָבֵד (A WOMAN OF ISRAEL'S DAUGHTERS TO A SLAVE MAN — the marriage with slaves, the Aramaic's reading (find 15)) אִתְּתָא אָמָא (a slave woman) the spine reads harlotry (260:1-2), the translation marriage — two laws from one verse. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:19 — MATERIAL. BRING NOT A HARLOT'S HIRE OR A DOG'S PRICE INTO THE HOUSE OF THE LORD FOR ANY VOW, FOR BOTH ARE AN ABOMINATION OF THE LORD YOUR GOD. "אֶתְנַן זוֹנָה וּמְחִיר כֶּלֶב" (a harlot's hire and a dog's price — 261:1-2) "גַּם שְׁנֵיהֶֽם" (even both of them — the parser's [2] starred; 261:5's two not four) אֲגַר זַנִּיתָא וְחוּלְפַן כַּלְבָּא (A HARLOT'S HIRE AND A DOG'S EXCHANGE — price as exchange (chulfan), 261:2's 'in exchange for this dog') לְבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁא (TO THE HOUSE OF THE SANCTUARY) מְרָחָק קֳדָם יְיָ (DISTANCED BEFORE THE LORD — the abomination formula's rendering) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:20 — MATERIAL. BITE NOT YOUR BROTHER — THE BITE OF SILVER, OF FOOD, OF ANYTHING THAT BITES. "לֹא תַשִּׁיךְ לְאָחִיךָ" (bite not your brother — 262:1's borrower) "נֶשֶׁךְ כָּל דָּבָר אֲשֶׁר יִשָּֽׁךְ" (the bite of anything that bites — 262:2-3) לָא תְרַבֵּי לַאֲחוּךְ רִבִּית כְּסַף (INCREASE NOT TO YOUR BROTHER THE INTEREST OF SILVER — the bite (neshekh) rendered increase (ribbit), Leviticus 25:36's second word) רִבִּית עִבוּר (the interest of grain — food as grain) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:21 — MATERIAL. TO THE FOREIGNER YOU MAY BITE, TO YOUR BROTHER NOT, THAT THE LORD YOUR GOD MAY BLESS YOU IN ALL YOU PUT YOUR HAND TO ON THE LAND YOU ARE COMING TO POSSESS. "לַנָּכְרִי תַשִּׁיךְ וּלְאָחִיךָ לֹא תַשִּׁיךְ" (to the foreigner bite, to your brother not — 263:1's two commandments, 263:2's redundancy) "בְּכֹל מִשְׁלַח יָדֶךָ" (in all you put your hand to — 263:3) לְבַר עַמְמִין תְּרַבֵּי (TO A SON OF THE NATIONS INCREASE) בְּכֹל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדָךְ (in all the stretching of your hand) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:22 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU VOW A VOW TO THE LORD YOUR GOD, DELAY NOT TO PAY IT, FOR HE WILL SURELY SEEK IT OF YOU, AND THERE SHALL BE SIN IN YOU. "לֹא תְאַחֵר לְשַׁלְּמוֹ" (delay not to pay it — 264:1, 264:3) "וְהָיָה בְךָ חֵֽטְא" (there shall be sin in you — 264:7; find 7's three seats) קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ (BEFORE THE LORD YOUR GOD — 'to' rendered 'before') וִיהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא (THERE SHALL BE GUILT (chova) IN YOU — sin as guilt, find 15) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:23 — MATERIAL. BUT IF YOU REFRAIN FROM VOWING, THERE SHALL BE NO SIN IN YOU. "וְכִי תֶחְדַּל לִנְדֹּר" (if you refrain from vowing — 265:1) "לֹֽא יִהְיֶה בְךָ חֵֽטְא" (no sin in you — find 7's negative) וַאֲרֵי תִתִּמְנַע לְמִנְדַּר (IF YOU HOLD BACK FROM VOWING) לָא יְהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא (no guilt in you) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:24 — MATERIAL. WHAT YOUR LIPS UTTER KEEP AND DO, AS YOU VOWED TO THE LORD YOUR GOD, A FREEWILL OFFERING YOU SPOKE WITH YOUR MOUTH. "מוֹצָא שְׂפָתֶיךָ תִּשְׁמֹר וְעָשִׂיתָ" (what your lips utter keep and do — 265:2-4's three) "נְדָבָה אֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתָּ בְּפִֽיךָ" (a freewill offering you spoke — 265:7-9) אַפָּקוּת סִפְוָתָךְ תִּטַּר (THE ISSUE OF YOUR LIPS KEEP) דִי מַלֶּלְתָּא בְּפוּמָךְ (which you spoke with your mouth) 23:24 carries no head — 265:2-9 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:25 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU COME INTO YOUR FELLOW'S VINEYARD, EAT GRAPES AS YOU WILL, YOUR FILL, BUT PUT NONE INTO YOUR VESSEL. "כִּי תָבֹא בְּכֶרֶם רֵעֶךָ" (when you come into your fellow's vineyard — 266:1's laborer) "כְּנַפְשְׁךָ שָׂבְעֶךָ" (as your soul, your fill — 266:5's dispute) "וְאֶֽל כֶּלְיְךָ לֹא תִתֵּֽן" (into your vessel put not — 266:6) אֲרֵי תִתָּגַר בְּכַרְמָא דְחַבְרָךְ (WHEN YOU ARE HIRED IN YOUR FELLOW'S VINEYARD — come rendered are hired (titgar): the laborer written in, 266:1's reading as the verse's own word) וּלְמָאנָךְ לָא תִתֵּן (into your vessel put not) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 23:26 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU COME INTO YOUR FELLOW'S STANDING GRAIN, PLUCK EARS WITH YOUR HAND, BUT SWING NO SICKLE OVER IT. "וְקָטַפְתָּ מְלִילֹת בְּיָדֶךָ" (pluck ears with your hand — 267:3) "וְחֶרְמֵשׁ לֹא תָנִיף" (swing no sickle — 267:1, 267:4) אֲרֵי תִתָּגַר בְּקַמְתָא דְחַבְרָךְ (WHEN YOU ARE HIRED IN YOUR FELLOW'S STANDING GRAIN — the hireling again) וּמַגְלָא לָא תְרִים (A SICKLE (magla) LIFT NOT — 267:3's 'reap with a magal', the Sifrei's noun) the chapter's last verse; 24:1 next. Fresh.

## Onkelos Deuteronomy 24:1-22 (22 DB verses read whole — the export's 22 rows, the identity)
- Onkelos Deut 24:1 — MATERIAL. WHEN A MAN TAKES A WIFE AND HAS HER, AND SHE FINDS NO FAVOR IN HIS EYES BECAUSE HE FOUND IN HER AN UNSEEMLY THING, HE WRITES HER A BILL OF CUTTING OFF, GIVES IT IN HER HAND AND SENDS HER FROM HIS HOUSE. "כִּֽי יִקַּח אִישׁ אִשָּׁה וּבְעָלָהּ" (when a man takes a wife and has her — 268:1-2's two acquisitions) "עֶרְוַת דָּבָר" (an unseemly thing — 269:1's schools) "וְכָתַב לָהּ סֵפֶר כְּרִיתֻת וְנָתַן בְּיָדָהּ וְשִׁלְּחָהּ" (writes her a bill of cutting off, gives it in her hand and sends her — find 4's run of eight with 24:3) עֲבֵרַת פִּתְגָּם (A TRANSGRESSION OF A WORD — 23:15's phrase again) גֵּט פִּטּוּרִין (A BILL OF RELEASE (get piturin) — the answer sheet's own word for the bill, find 4) וְיִפְטְרִנַּהּ מִבֵּיתֵיהּ (and release her from his house) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:2 — MATERIAL. AND SHE GOES OUT OF HIS HOUSE AND GOES AND BECOMES ANOTHER MAN'S. "וְיָצְאָה מִבֵּיתוֹ" (she goes out of his house — 270:1) "וְהָיְתָה לְאִישׁ אַחֵֽר" (and becomes another man's — 270:2-3) וּתְהֵי לִגְבַר אָחֳרָן (AND BE TO ANOTHER MAN) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:3 — MATERIAL. AND THE LATTER MAN HATES HER AND WRITES HER A BILL OF CUTTING OFF, GIVES IT IN HER HAND AND SENDS HER FROM HIS HOUSE; OR THE LATTER MAN WHO TOOK HER TO WIFE DIES. "וּשְׂנֵאָהּ הָאִישׁ הָאַחֲרוֹן" (the latter man hates her — 270:4) "אוֹ כִי יָמוּת הָאִישׁ הָאַחֲרוֹן" (or the latter man dies — 270:5-6) וְיִסְנִנַּהּ גַּבְרָא בַתְרָאָה (THE LATTER MAN HATES HER) גֵּט פִּטּוּרִין (the bill of release again) no head on 24:3 — 270:4-7 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:4 — MATERIAL. HER FIRST HUSBAND WHO SENT HER AWAY MAY NOT TAKE HER AGAIN TO BE HIS WIFE AFTER SHE WAS DEFILED, FOR IT IS AN ABOMINATION BEFORE THE LORD; MAKE NOT THE LAND SIN WHICH THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU FOR AN INHERITANCE. "לֹא יוּכַל בַּעְלָהּ הָרִאשׁוֹן" (her first husband may not — 270:8-9; the parser's ordinal [1]) "אַחֲרֵי אֲשֶׁר הֻטַּמָּאָה" (after she was defiled — 270:10's sotah) "כִּֽי תוֹעֵבָה הִוא לִפְנֵי יְהוָה" (for it is an abomination before the LORD — 270:12; find 5) לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ (HE HAS NO PERMISSION — 'may not' as 'no permission', 22:3's idiom) מְרָחָקָא הִיא קֳדָם יְיָ (IT IS DISTANCED BEFORE THE LORD) וְלָא תְחַיַּב יָת אַרְעָא (AND MAKE NOT THE LAND LIABLE — sin as liability (chov)) the gift formula's seat (find 9); no head on 24:4. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:5 — MATERIAL. WHEN A MAN TAKES A NEW WIFE HE SHALL NOT GO OUT IN THE HOST NOR SHALL ANY MATTER PASS UPON HIM; FREE SHALL HE BE FOR HIS HOUSE ONE YEAR AND REJOICE HIS WIFE WHOM HE TOOK. "אִשָּׁה חֲדָשָׁה" (a new wife — 271:1's 'new to him') "נָקִי יִהְיֶה לְבֵיתוֹ שָׁנָה אֶחָת" (free for his house one year — the parser's [1]; 271:3-5) לָא יִפּוֹק בְּחֵילָא (HE SHALL NOT GO OUT IN THE HOST) פָּנֵי יְהֵי לְבֵיתֵיהּ שַׁתָּא חֲדָא (free he shall be for his house one year) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:6 — MATERIAL. HE SHALL NOT TAKE IN PLEDGE THE MILL AND THE UPPER STONE, FOR HE TAKES A LIFE IN PLEDGE. "לֹא יַחֲבֹל רֵחַיִם וָרָכֶב" (take not in pledge the mill and the upper stone — 272:1) "כִּי נֶפֶשׁ הוּא חֹבֵֽל" (for he takes a life in pledge — 272:2) אֲרֵי בְהוֹן מִתְעֲבֵד מָזוֹן לְכָל נְפַשׁ (FOR WITH THEM FOOD IS MADE FOR EVERY SOUL — the reason paraphrased: the livelihood, not the life) the verse with no kin (find 12). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:7 — MATERIAL. IF A MAN BE FOUND STEALING A SOUL OF HIS BROTHERS OF THE CHILDREN OF ISRAEL, AND ENSLAVES HIM AND SELLS HIM, THAT THIEF SHALL DIE; PURGE THE EVIL FROM YOUR MIDST. "גֹּנֵב נֶפֶשׁ מֵאֶחָיו" (stealing a soul of his brothers — 273:3-4) "וְהִתְעַמֶּר בּוֹ וּמְכָרוֹ" (and enslaves him and sells him — 273:5) "וּבִֽעַרְתָּ הָרָע מִקִּרְבֶּֽךָ" (purge the evil from your midst — the formula's last seat, find 6; 273:9) וְיִתָּגַר בֵּיהּ (AND TRADES IN HIM — enslaving as trading) וְיִתְקְטֵל גַּנָּבָא הַהוּא (THAT THIEF SHALL BE KILLED) וּתְפַלֵּי עָבֵד דְּבִישׁ (AND PUT AWAY THE DOER OF EVIL — the formula's rendering, 'the evil' a person) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:8 — MATERIAL. TAKE HEED IN THE PLAGUE OF LEPROSY TO KEEP DILIGENTLY AND TO DO ALL THAT THE LEVITICAL PRIESTS TEACH YOU; AS I COMMANDED THEM YOU SHALL KEEP TO DO. "הִשָּׁמֶר בְּנֶֽגַע הַצָּרַעַת" (take heed in the plague of leprosy — 274:1-3; the one imperative, find 14) "כְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יוֹרוּ אֶתְכֶם הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם" (as the levitical priests teach you — 274:4) בְּמַכְתַּשׁ סְגִירוּ (IN THE PLAGUE OF SHUTTING-UP — leprosy named by its quarantine (segiru), 274:5's state) דִּי יַלְּפוּן יָתְכוֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא (which the priests teach you) the plural "you" (find 14). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:9 — MATERIAL. REMEMBER WHAT THE LORD YOUR GOD DID TO MIRIAM ON THE WAY WHEN YOU CAME OUT OF EGYPT. "זָכוֹר אֵת אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה יְהוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְמִרְיָם" (remember what the LORD did to Miriam — 275:1; the infinitive absolute, find 2) "בַּדֶּרֶךְ בְּצֵאתְכֶם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם" (on the way when you came out of Egypt — 275:2-3) הֲוֵי דְכִיר (BE REMEMBERING — the infinitive absolute as 'be mindful', Exodus 20:8's form) Numbers 12 the tape's line behind. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:10 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU LEND YOUR FELLOW ANYTHING, COME NOT INTO HIS HOUSE TO TAKE HIS PLEDGE. "כִּֽי תַשֶּׁה בְרֵֽעֲךָ מַשַּׁאת מְאוּמָה" (when you lend your fellow anything — 276:1) "לֹא תָבֹא אֶל בֵּיתוֹ" (come not into his house — 276:2) אֲרֵי תַרְשֵׁי בְחַבְרָךְ רְשׁוּ מִדָּעַם (WHEN YOU HAVE A CLAIM ON YOUR FELLOW OF ANYTHING) לְמִסַּב מַשְׁכּוֹנֵיהּ (to take his pledge) the verse with no kin (find 12). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:11 — MATERIAL. OUTSIDE YOU SHALL STAND, AND THE MAN YOU LEND TO SHALL BRING THE PLEDGE OUT TO YOU. "בַּחוּץ תַּעֲמֹד" (outside you shall stand — 276:2) "וְהָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה נֹשֶׁה בוֹ" (and the man you lend to — 276:3's court agent) בְּבָרָא תְּקוּם (OUTSIDE YOU SHALL STAND) יַפֵּק לְוָתָךְ יָת מַשְׁכּוֹנָא (shall bring out the pledge to you) no head on 24:11. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:12 — MATERIAL. AND IF HE IS A POOR MAN, LIE NOT DOWN IN HIS PLEDGE. "וְאִם אִישׁ עָנִי הוּא" (and if he is a poor man — 277:1) "לֹא תִשְׁכַּב בַּעֲבֹטֽוֹ" (lie not down in his pledge — 277:2) גְּבַר מִסְכֵּן (A POOR MAN (miskena)) לָא תִשְׁכּוּב בְּמַשְׁכּוֹנֵיהּ (lie not down with his pledge) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:13 — MATERIAL. SURELY RETURN HIM THE PLEDGE AT SUNSET, THAT HE LIE IN HIS GARMENT AND BLESS YOU; AND IT SHALL BE RIGHTEOUSNESS TO YOU BEFORE THE LORD YOUR GOD. "הָשֵׁב תָּשִׁיב לוֹ אֶֽת הַעֲבוֹט" (surely return him the pledge — 277:3; the infinitive absolute) "כְּבֹא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ" (at sunset — the clock, 23:12's phrase) "וּלְךָ תִּהְיֶה צְדָקָה" (and it shall be righteousness to you — 277:4-5) כְּמֵעַל שִׁמְשָׁא (AT THE SUN'S COMING IN) וְלָךְ תְּהֵי זְכוּתָא (AND IT SHALL BE MERIT (zekhuta) TO YOU — righteousness as merit, find 15) no head on 24:13. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:14 — MATERIAL. OPPRESS NOT A HIRELING POOR AND NEEDY OF YOUR BROTHERS OR OF YOUR SOJOURNER IN YOUR LAND IN YOUR GATES. "לֹא תַעֲשֹׁק שָׂכִיר עָנִי וְאֶבְיוֹן" (oppress not a hireling poor and needy — 278:1-2; find 16) "אוֹ מִגֵּרְךָ" (or your sojourner — 278:4-5) אֲגִירָא עַנְיָא וּמִסְכֵּנָא (A HIRELING POOR AND NEEDY) מִגִּיּוֹרָךְ (YOUR PROSELYTE — the sojourner as proselyte (giyora), 278:4's righteous proselyte) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:15 — MATERIAL. ON HIS DAY GIVE HIS WAGE, AND LET NOT THE SUN SET ON IT, FOR HE IS POOR AND TO IT HE LIFTS HIS SOUL; THAT HE CRY NOT AGAINST YOU TO THE LORD AND IT BE SIN IN YOU. "בְּיוֹמוֹ תִתֵּן שְׂכָרוֹ" (on his day give his wage — 279:1) "וְֽלֹא תָבוֹא עָלָיו הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ" (and let not the sun set on it — 279:2) "וְֽלֹא יִקְרָא עָלֶיךָ אֶל יְהוָה וְהָיָה בְךָ" (that he cry not against you — 279:4; find 7's run of six with 15:9) וְלֵיהּ הוּא מְסַר יָת נַפְשֵׁיהּ (AND TO IT HE GIVES OVER HIS LIFE — 279:3's ramp) וִיהֵי בָךְ חוֹבָא (and there shall be guilt in you) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:16 — MATERIAL. FATHERS SHALL NOT BE PUT TO DEATH FOR SONS, NOR SONS FOR FATHERS; EACH FOR HIS OWN SIN SHALL DIE. "לֹֽא יוּמְתוּ אָבוֹת עַל בָּנִים" (fathers shall not die for sons — 280:1; find 1's 2 Kings 14:6) "אִיש בְּחֶטְאוֹ יוּמָֽתוּ" (each for his own sin — 280:3) עַל פּוּם בְּנִין (BY THE MOUTH OF SONS — Onkelos writes 280:1's testimony into the verse: 'for' rendered 'by the mouth of') אֱנַשׁ בְּחוֹבֵיהּ יְמוּתוּן (each in his guilt shall die) the pointer OWED (2 Kings 14:6 ahead). Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:17 — MATERIAL. PERVERT NOT THE JUDGMENT OF THE SOJOURNER OR THE ORPHAN, AND TAKE NOT A WIDOW'S GARMENT IN PLEDGE. "לֹא תַטֶּה מִשְׁפַּט גֵּר יָתוֹם" (pervert not the judgment of sojourner and orphan — 281:1; 16:19's twin) "וְלֹא תַחֲבֹל בֶּגֶד אַלְמָנָֽה" (take not a widow's garment in pledge — 281:2-3) לָא תַצְלֵי דִין גִּיּוֹרָא וְיִתַּמָּא (BEND NOT THE JUDGMENT OF THE PROSELYTE AND THE ORPHAN) לְבוּשׁ אַרְמְלָא (a widow's garment) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:18 — MATERIAL. REMEMBER THAT YOU WERE A SLAVE IN EGYPT AND THE LORD YOUR GOD REDEEMED YOU FROM THERE; THEREFORE I COMMAND YOU TO DO THIS THING. "וְזָכַרְתָּ כִּי עֶבֶד הָיִיתָ בְּמִצְרַיִם" (remember you were a slave in Egypt — find 3's refrain, 15:15's fourteen words) "אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ" (I command you — the store's '?' gloss on 'I' (anokhi), the measure) וּפָרְקָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ מִתַּמָּן (AND THE LORD YOUR GOD REDEEMED YOU FROM THERE) אֲנָא מְפַקְּדָךְ (I command you) no head on 24:18. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:19 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU REAP YOUR HARVEST IN YOUR FIELD AND FORGET A SHEAF IN THE FIELD, RETURN NOT TO TAKE IT; FOR THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW LET IT BE, THAT THE LORD YOUR GOD MAY BLESS YOU IN ALL THE WORK OF YOUR HANDS. "כִּי תִקְצֹר קְצִֽירְךָ בְשָׂדֶךָ" (when you reap your harvest in your field — 282:1-4) "וְשָֽׁכַחְתָּ עֹמֶר בַּשָּׂדֶה" (and forget a sheaf in the field — 283:1-2) "לֹא תָשׁוּב לְקַחְתּוֹ" (return not to take it — 283:3-5) וְתִנְשֵׁי עֻמְרָא בְחַקְלָא (AND FORGET A SHEAF IN THE FIELD) לְגִיּוֹרָא לְיִתַּמָּא וּלְאַרְמְלָא יְהֵי (for the proselyte, the orphan and the widow let it be) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:20 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU BEAT YOUR OLIVE TREE, GO NOT OVER THE BOUGHS AFTER YOU; FOR THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW LET IT BE. "כִּי תַחְבֹּט זֵֽיתְךָ" (when you beat your olive tree — 284:1) "לֹא תְפָאֵר אַחֲרֶיךָ" (go not over the boughs after you — 284:3-5) לָא תְפַלֵּי בַּתְרָךְ (SEARCH NOT AFTER YOU — the rare verb (pa'er) rendered 'search out' (pali), the same Aramaic root as 24:7's 'put away' the evil: one Aramaic verb for two Hebrew ones) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:21 — MATERIAL. WHEN YOU GATHER YOUR VINEYARD, GLEAN NOT THE SMALL CLUSTERS AFTER YOU; FOR THE SOJOURNER, THE ORPHAN AND THE WIDOW LET IT BE. "כִּי תִבְצֹר כַּרְמְךָ" (when you gather your vineyard — 285:1) "לֹא תְעוֹלֵל אַחֲרֶיךָ" (glean not the small clusters after you — 285:2-3) לָא תְעָלֵל בַּתְרָךְ (GLEAN NOT AFTER YOU — the Hebrew verb kept (olel)) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 24:22 — MATERIAL. REMEMBER THAT YOU WERE A SLAVE IN THE LAND OF EGYPT; THEREFORE I COMMAND YOU TO DO THIS THING. "וְזָכַרְתָּ כִּי עֶבֶד הָיִיתָ בְּאֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם" (remember you were a slave in the land of Egypt — the chapter's second seal, find 3) "אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ" (I command you — the second '?' gloss) וְתִדְכַּר אֲרֵי עַבְדָּא הֲוֵיתָא (REMEMBER THAT A SLAVE YOU WERE) יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין (this thing) no head on 24:22; the chapter's last verse — 25:1 next. Fresh.

## Onkelos Deuteronomy 25:1-19 (19 DB verses read whole — the export's 19 rows, the identity)
- Onkelos Deut 25:1 — MATERIAL. WHEN THERE IS A QUARREL BETWEEN MEN AND THEY COME TO JUDGMENT, THE JUDGES JUSTIFY THE RIGHTEOUS AND CONDEMN THE WICKED. "כִּֽי יִהְיֶה רִיב בֵּין אֲנָשִׁים" (when there is a quarrel between men — 286:1-2) "וְהִצְדִּיקוּ אֶת הַצַּדִּיק וְהִרְשִׁיעוּ אֶת הָרָשָֽׁע" (justify the righteous and condemn the wicked — 286:4) אֲרֵי יְהֵי דִין בֵּין גֻּבְרַיָּא (WHEN THERE IS A CASE (din) BETWEEN MEN — the quarrel rendered a lawsuit) וִיזַכּוּן יָת זַכָּאָה וִיחַיְּבוּן יָת חַיָּבָא (ACQUIT THE INNOCENT AND CONVICT THE GUILTY — the court's two verdicts (zakkai, chayav), the answer sheet's own words) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:2 — MATERIAL. IF THE WICKED DESERVES FLOGGING, THE JUDGE LAYS HIM DOWN AND FLOGS HIM BEFORE HIM AS HIS WICKEDNESS DESERVES, BY NUMBER. "אִם בִּן הַכּוֹת הָרָשָׁע" (if the wicked deserves flogging — 286:4's class) "וְהִפִּילוֹ הַשֹּׁפֵט וְהִכָּהוּ לְפָנָיו" (the judge lays him down and flogs him before him — 286:5-7) "כְּדֵי רִשְׁעָתוֹ בְּמִסְפָּֽר" (as his wickedness deserves, by number — 286:7's one sanction, 286:8's count) בַּר חַיָּבָא לְאַלְקָאָה (A SON OF GUILT FOR FLOGGING — 'deserving' as 'son of') כְּמִסַּת חוֹבְתֵיהּ בְּמִנְיָן (according to his guilt, by count) 25:2 carries no head — 286:5-7 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:3 — MATERIAL. FORTY HE SHALL STRIKE HIM, NOT MORE, LEST HE ADD A GREAT BLOW AND YOUR BROTHER BE DEGRADED BEFORE YOUR EYES.
