"אַרְבָּעִים יַכֶּנּוּ לֹא יֹסִיף" (forty he shall strike him, not more — the parser's [40]; 286:8-10) "וְנִקְלָה אָחִיךָ לְעֵינֶֽיךָ" (and your brother is degraded before your eyes — 286:12-14) אַרְבְּעִין יַלְקִנֵּיהּ (FORTY HE SHALL FLOG HIM — the number kept whole (find 15), the spine's thirty-nine not written) וְיֵקַל אֲחוּךְ (and your brother be lightened — the verb kept) 25:3 carries no head. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:4 — MATERIAL. MUZZLE NOT AN OX IN ITS THRESHING. "לֹא תַחְסֹם שׁוֹר בְּדִישֽׁוֹ" (muzzle not an ox in its threshing — 287:1-3; the verse with no kin (find 12)) לָא תֵחוֹד פּוּם תּוֹרָא (BIND NOT THE MOUTH OF THE OX — the mouth added, the muzzle explained) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:5 — MATERIAL. WHEN BROTHERS DWELL TOGETHER AND ONE OF THEM DIES WITHOUT A SON, THE DEAD MAN'S WIFE SHALL NOT BE OUTSIDE TO A STRANGER; HER LEVIR COMES TO HER, TAKES HER TO WIFE AND MARRIES HER. "כִּֽי יֵשְׁבוּ אַחִים יַחְדָּו" (when brothers dwell together — 288:1-2) "וּמֵת אַחַד מֵהֶם וּבֵן אֵֽין לוֹ" (and one of them dies without a son — the parser's [1]; 288:3) "יְבָמָהּ יָבֹא עָלֶיהָ וּלְקָחָהּ לוֹ לְאִשָּׁה וְיִבְּמָֽהּ" (her levir comes to her, takes her and marries her — 288:5-6) לָא תְהֵי אִתַּת מֵתָנָא לְבָרָא (THE DEAD MAN'S WIFE SHALL NOT BE OUTSIDE) לִגְבַר אוֹחֲרָן (TO ANOTHER MAN — 'a stranger' rendered 'another'; THE EXPORT'S VARIANT MARK follows in parentheses ('another reading: an outsider', chiloni) — no token of the verse, CUT AROUND (RUN 1's lesson at 22:15)) יְבָמַהּ יֵעוּל עֲלַהּ (her levir shall come to her) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:6 — MATERIAL. AND THE FIRSTBORN SHE BEARS SHALL STAND ON HIS DEAD BROTHER'S NAME, THAT HIS NAME BE NOT BLOTTED OUT OF ISRAEL. "וְהָיָה הַבְּכוֹר אֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵד" (the firstborn she bears — 289:1's eldest brother, 289:2) "יָקוּם עַל שֵׁם אָחִיו הַמֵּת" (shall stand on his dead brother's name — 289:3-4, 289:9's inheritance) "וְלֹֽא יִמָּחֶה שְׁמוֹ מִיִּשְׂרָאֵֽל" (and his name not be blotted out of Israel — 289:5's eunuch; Amalek's verb (25:19)) בוּכְרָא דִּי תְלִיד (THE FIRSTBORN SHE BEARS — the verse plain, the spine's eldest not written) וְלָא יִתִּמְחֵי שְׁמֵיהּ (and his name not be blotted) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:7 — MATERIAL. IF THE MAN DOES NOT DESIRE TO TAKE HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW, SHE GOES UP TO THE GATE TO THE ELDERS AND SAYS: MY LEVIR REFUSES TO RAISE UP A NAME FOR HIS BROTHER IN ISRAEL, HE WILL NOT CONSENT. "וְאִם לֹא יַחְפֹּץ הָאִישׁ" (if the man does not desire — 289:6's three clauses) "וְעָלְתָה יְבִמְתּוֹ הַשַּׁעְרָה אֶל הַזְּקֵנִים" (she goes up to the gate to the elders — 289:8) "מֵאֵין יְבָמִי" (my levir refuses — 289:9) "בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל" (in Israel — 289:10's proselytes) לִתְרַע בֵּית דִּינָא (TO THE GATE OF THE COURT — the gate named the court, 289:8's reading written in) לָקֳדָם סָבַיָּא (before the elders) 25:7 carries no head. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:8 — MATERIAL. THE ELDERS OF HIS CITY CALL HIM AND SPEAK TO HIM; HE STANDS AND SAYS: I DO NOT DESIRE TO TAKE HER. "וְקָֽרְאוּ לוֹ זִקְנֵי עִירוֹ וְדִבְּרוּ אֵלָיו" (the elders of his city call him and speak to him — 290:1-2) "וְעָמַד וְאָמַר" (he stands and says — 290:3) וִימַלְּלוּן עִמֵּיהּ (AND SPEAK WITH HIM) לָא רְעֵנָא לְמִסְבַּהּ (I do not wish to take her) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:9 — MATERIAL. HIS LEVIRATE WIDOW COMES NEAR HIM BEFORE THE ELDERS' EYES, DRAWS HIS SHOE OFF HIS FOOT, SPITS BEFORE HIM AND ANSWERS: THUS SHALL BE DONE TO THE MAN WHO WILL NOT BUILD HIS BROTHER'S HOUSE. "לְעֵינֵי הַזְּקֵנִים" (before the elders' eyes — 291:1, 291:4's visible spittle) "וְחָלְצָה נַעֲלוֹ מֵעַל רַגְלוֹ" (she draws his shoe off his foot — 291:2-3) "וְיָרְקָה בְּפָנָיו" (and spits before him — 291:4, 291:7) "וְעָֽנְתָה וְאָמְרָה" (and answers and says — 291:5's holy tongue) וְתַשְׁרֵי סֵינֵיהּ מֵעַל רַגְלֵיהּ (AND LOOSE HIS SHOE FROM HIS FOOT — loosen (shera) the Aramaic's verb, the rite's name) וְתֵרוֹק בְּאַנְפּוֹהִי (and spit before his face) כְּדֵין יִתְעֲבֵד לִגְבַר (thus shall be done to the man) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:10 — MATERIAL. AND HIS NAME SHALL BE CALLED IN ISRAEL: THE HOUSE OF THE LOOSED SHOE. "וְנִקְרָא שְׁמוֹ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל" (his name is called in Israel — 291:8's court) "בֵּית חֲלוּץ הַנָּֽעַל" (the house of the loosed shoe — 291:9's judges) בֵּית שְׁרִי סֵינָא (THE HOUSE OF THE LOOSENING OF THE SHOE — the rite's Aramaic name (shera sena)) 25:10 carries no head — 291:7-9 its rows. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:11 — MATERIAL. WHEN MEN STRUGGLE TOGETHER, A MAN AND HIS BROTHER, AND THE WIFE OF THE ONE DRAWS NEAR TO SAVE HER HUSBAND FROM HIS STRIKER'S HAND, AND PUTS OUT HER HAND AND SEIZES HIS SECRETS. "כִּֽי יִנָּצוּ אֲנָשִׁים יַחְדָּו" (when men struggle together — 292:1-2) "וְקָֽרְבָה אֵשֶׁת הָֽאֶחָד" (the wife of the one draws near — the parser's [1] starred; 292:4) "וְהֶחֱזִיקָה בִּמְבֻשָֽׁיו" (and seizes his secrets — 292:5) אִתַּת חַד (THE WIFE OF ONE) וְתִתְקֵף בְּבֵית בַּהַתְּתֵיהּ (AND SEIZES THE PLACE OF HIS SHAME — the euphemism explained) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:12 — MATERIAL. CUT OFF HER HAND; YOUR EYE SHALL NOT PITY. "וְקַצֹּתָה אֶת כַּפָּהּ" (cut off her hand — 293:1's rescue, 293:2's money) "לֹא תָחוֹס עֵינֶֽךָ" (your eye shall not pity — the pity formula, 19:21's twin) וּתְקוּץ יָת יְדַהּ (AND CUT OFF HER HAND — the verb kept, no money written: the spine's reading (293:2) not in the translation, the pity formula's operator the compile's) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:13 — MATERIAL. YOU SHALL NOT HAVE IN YOUR BAG A STONE AND A STONE, GREAT AND SMALL. "לֹֽא יִהְיֶה לְךָ בְּכִֽיסְךָ אֶבֶן וָאָבֶן" (you shall not have in your bag a stone and a stone — 294:1-3; Proverbs 20:10's phrase (find 16)) מַתְקַל וּמַתְקָל (A WEIGHT AND A WEIGHT — the stone rendered weight (matkal)) רַבְּתָא וּזְעֶרְתָּא (great and small) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:14 — MATERIAL. YOU SHALL NOT HAVE IN YOUR HOUSE AN EPHAH AND AN EPHAH, GREAT AND SMALL. "אֵיפָה וְאֵיפָה גְּדוֹלָה וּקְטַנָּֽה" (an ephah and an ephah, great and small — 294:4-5) מְכִילָא וּמְכִילָא (A MEASURE AND A MEASURE (mekhila)) 25:14 carries no head. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:15 — MATERIAL. A WHOLE AND JUST STONE YOU SHALL HAVE, A WHOLE AND JUST EPHAH, THAT YOUR DAYS BE LONG ON THE LAND THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU. "אֶבֶן שְׁלֵמָה וָצֶדֶק" (a whole and just stone — 294:6-8) "לְמַעַן יַאֲרִיכוּ יָמֶיךָ" (that your days be long — 295:1's reward beside it; find 9's run with 5:16) מַתְקְלִין שָׁלְמִין דִּקְשׁוֹט (WHOLE WEIGHTS OF TRUTH — just rendered truth (keshot), the plural) בְּדִיל דְּיוֹרְכוּן יוֹמָיךְ (that your days be long) 295 headless inside; 294:6-9 too. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:16 — MATERIAL. FOR AN ABOMINATION OF THE LORD YOUR GOD IS EVERYONE WHO DOES THESE, EVERYONE WHO DOES WRONG. "כִּי תוֹעֲבַת יְהוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ כָּל עֹשֵׂה אֵלֶּה" (an abomination of the LORD is everyone who does these — find 5's run of seven with 22:5; 295:2) "כָּל עֹשֵׂה עָֽוֶל" (everyone who does wrong — 295:3-4) מְרָחָק קֳדָם יְיָ (DISTANCED BEFORE THE LORD — the formula's rendering at its seventh seat) כָּל עָבֵד שְׁקַר (ALL WHO DO FALSEHOOD — wrong rendered falsehood (shekar)) 25:16 carries no head. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:17 — MATERIAL. REMEMBER WHAT AMALEK DID TO YOU ON THE WAY WHEN YOU CAME OUT OF EGYPT. "זָכוֹר אֵת אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה לְךָ עֲמָלֵק" (remember what Amalek did to you — 296:1's mouth; find 2's third remembrance) "בַּדֶּרֶךְ בְּצֵאתְכֶם מִמִּצְרָֽיִם" (on the way when you came out of Egypt — 296:2-3, the phrase's third and last seat) הֲוֵי דְכִיר (BE REMEMBERING — 24:9's form again) Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:18 — MATERIAL. WHO MET YOU ON THE WAY AND CUT OFF ALL THE STRAGGLERS BEHIND YOU WHEN YOU WERE WEARY AND FAINT, AND FEARED NOT GOD. "אֲשֶׁר קָֽרְךָ בַּדֶּרֶךְ" (who met you on the way — 296:4) "וַיְזַנֵּב בְּךָ כָּל הַנֶּחֱשָׁלִים אַֽחַרֶיךָ" (and cut off the stragglers behind you — 296:5) "וְלֹא יָרֵא אֱלֹהִֽים" (and feared not God — 296:6; Elohim once in the chapter (find 14)) וְקָטֵל בָּךְ כָּל דַּהֲווֹ מִתְאַחֲרִין בַּתְרָךְ (AND KILLED AMONG YOU ALL WHO LAGGED BEHIND YOU — the tail-cutting as killing the laggards) וְלָא דָחֵיל מִן קֳדָם יְיָ (AND FEARED NOT BEFORE THE LORD — the subject Amalek (the spine reads both, 296:6); God rendered the Name) 25:18 carries no head. Fresh.
- Onkelos Deut 25:19 — MATERIAL. WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU REST FROM ALL YOUR ENEMIES AROUND, IN THE LAND HE GIVES YOU FOR AN INHERITANCE TO POSSESS, BLOT OUT THE MEMORY OF AMALEK FROM UNDER HEAVEN; FORGET NOT. "וְהָיָה בְּהָנִיחַ יְהוָה אֱלֹהֶיךָ לְךָ" (when the LORD gives you rest — 296:7's condition) "נַחֲלָה לְרִשְׁתָּהּ" (for an inheritance to possess it — find 9's two seats (15:4); 296:8) "תִּמְחֶה אֶת זֵכֶר עֲמָלֵק מִתַּחַת הַשָּׁמָיִם" (blot out the memory of Amalek from under heaven — Exodus 17:14's run of five (find 10); 296:9) "לֹא תִּשְׁכָּֽח" (forget not — 296:1's heart) כַּד יְנִיחַ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָךְ (WHEN THE LORD YOUR GOD GIVES YOU REST) תִּמְחֵי יָת דּוּכְרָנָא דַעֲמָלֵק (BLOT OUT THE MEMORY OF AMALEK — the memory a noun (dukhrana), no 'males' written (the English's bracket the translator's)) לָא תִּתִּנְשֵׁי (forget not) 25:19 carries no head — the chapter's, the portion's (Ki Teitzei 21:10-25:19) and the sitting's last verse; 1 Samuel 15 the receipt ahead, the pointer OWED. Fresh.
